  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 随着Video-LLMs处理更长更复杂的视频，大量视觉令牌导致推理成本快速增长。现有无需训练的时序压缩方法主要依赖跨帧令牌相似度或分割启发式，忽略了每个令牌在其帧内的语义角色，且未能根据每帧对的压缩性自适应调整压缩强度。

Method: 两阶段框架：1) 空间剪枝：识别每帧内的代表性内容；2) 帧间最优传输：引入非均匀令牌质量（保护重要语义令牌）和局部感知代价（同时捕捉特征和空间差异），通过求解最优传输问题估计时序可压缩性，并以传输总成本作为传输难度来动态分配压缩预算。

Result: 在视频问答和时序定位的6个基准上，OTT-Vid仅保留10%的令牌即可保持95.8%的VQA性能和73.9%的VTG性能，持续优于现有最先进的无需训练压缩方法。

Conclusion: 基于最优传输的令牌压缩框架能有效利用令牌语义重要性和空间信息自适应分配压缩预算，在极低令牌保留率下显著保持模型性能，为Video-LLMs的高效推理提供了实用方案。

Original Abstract: As Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs) scale to longer and more complex videos, their inference cost grows rapidly due to the large volume of visual tokens accumulated across frames. Training-free token compression has emerged as a practical solution to this bottleneck. However, existing temporal compression methods rely primarily on cross-frame token similarity or segmentation heuristics, overlooking each token's semantic role within its frame and failing to adapt compression strength to the compressibility of each frame pair. In this work, we propose OTT-Vid, a transport-derived allocation framework for temporal token compression. Our approach consists of two stages: spatial pruning identifies representative content within each frame, and optimal transport (OT) is then solved between neighboring frames to estimate temporal compressibility. We formulate this OT with non-uniform token mass, which protects semantically important tokens from aggressive compression, and a locality-aware cost that captures both feature and spatial disparities. The resulting transport plan jointly balances token importance and matching cost, while its total cost defines the transport difficulty of each frame pair, which we use to allocate compression budgets dynamically. Experiments on six benchmarks spanning video question answering and temporal grounding show that OTT-Vid preserves 95.8% of VQA and 73.9% of VTG performance while retaining only 10% of tokens, consistently outperforming existing state-of-the-art training-free compression methods.

中文摘要翻译: 随着视频大语言模型（Video-LLMs）扩展到更长、更复杂的视频，由于跨帧累积的大量视觉令牌，其推理成本快速增长。无需训练的令牌压缩已成为应对这一瓶颈的实用解决方案。然而，现有的时序压缩方法主要依赖跨帧令牌相似度或分割启发式，忽略了每个令牌在其帧内的语义角色，且未能根据每帧对的压缩性调整压缩强度。在本工作中，我们提出OTT-Vid，一种基于传输分配框架的时序令牌压缩方法。我们的方法包含两个阶段：空间剪枝识别每帧内的代表性内容，然后求解相邻帧之间的最优传输（OT）以估计时序可压缩性。我们构建了具有非均匀令牌质量的最优传输问题，以保护语义重要的令牌免受激进压缩，同时引入局部感知代价来同时捕捉特征和空间差异。得到的传输计划联合平衡了令牌重要性和匹配代价，其总代价定义了每帧对的传输难度，我们据此动态分配压缩预算。在涵盖视频问答和时序定位的六个基准上的实验表明，OTT-Vid在仅保留10%令牌的情况下保持了95.8%的VQA性能和73.9%的VTG性能，持续优于现有最先进的无需训练压缩方法。

</details>


### [60] [Mitigating Action-Relation Hallucinations in LVLMs via Relation-aware Visual Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11808)
*Zhenxin Qin,Qiang Li,Qingzhuo Wang,Ruiyang Qin,Zhihua Wei,Wen Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出通过定位动作关系相关图像区域并增强LVLM对该区域的注意力，以缓解大型视觉语言模型中的动作关系幻觉问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究主要关注缓解物体幻觉，但忽略了更复杂的动作关系幻觉（涉及对象间交互），且经验观察到LVLM中动作关系幻觉的主要原因是视觉信息注意力不足。

Method: 提出动作关系敏感性（ARS）分数来识别对动作关系变化最敏感的注意力头，进而定位包含关键视觉线索的动作相关图像区域；然后提出关系感知视觉增强（RVE）方法，增强LVLM对这些区域的注意力。

Result: 与现有基线相比，该方法在缓解动作关系幻觉方面取得了优越性能，且额外推理成本可忽略不计；此外，该方法还能有效泛化到空间关系幻觉和物体幻觉上。

Conclusion: 通过增强LVLM对动作相关图像区域的注意力，可以有效缓解动作关系幻觉，且该方法具有良好的泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have achieved remarkable performance on diverse vision-language tasks. However, LVLMs still suffer from hallucinations, generating text that contradicts the visual input. Existing research has primarily focused on mitigating object hallucinations, but often overlooks more complex relation hallucinations, particularly action relations involving interactions between objects. In this study, we empirically observe that the primary cause of action-relation hallucinations in LVLMs is the insufficient attention allocated to visual information. Thus, we propose a framework to locate action-relevant image regions and enhance the LVLM's attention to those regions. Specifically, we define the Action-Relation Sensitivity (ARS) score to identify attention heads that are most sensitive to action-relation changes, thereby localizing action-relevant image regions that contain key visual cues. Then, we propose the Relation-aware Visual Enhancement (RVE) method to enhance the LVLM's attention to these action-relevant image regions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that, compared to existing baselines, our method achieves superior performance in mitigating action-relation hallucinations with negligible additional inference cost. Furthermore, it effectively generalizes to spatial-relation hallucinations and object hallucinations.

中文摘要翻译: 大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）在多样的视觉-语言任务上取得了显著性能。然而，LVLMs仍然存在幻觉问题，即生成与视觉输入相矛盾的文本。现有研究主要聚焦于缓解物体幻觉，但往往忽略了更复杂的关系幻觉，特别是涉及对象间相互作用的动作关系幻觉。在本研究中，我们经验性地观察到，LVLMs中动作关系幻觉的主要原因是分配给视觉信息的注意力不足。因此，我们提出一个框架来定位与动作相关的图像区域并增强LVLM对这些区域的注意力。具体而言，我们定义了动作关系敏感性（ARS）分数，用于识别对动作关系变化最敏感的注意力头，从而定位包含关键视觉线索的动作相关图像区域。然后，我们提出关系感知视觉增强（RVE）方法，以增强LVLM对这些动作相关图像区域的注意力。大量实验表明，与现有基线相比，我们的方法在缓解动作关系幻觉方面取得了优越性能，且额外推理成本可忽略不计。此外，该方法还能有效泛化到空间关系幻觉和物体幻觉上。

</details>


### [61] [RevealLayer: Disentangling Hidden and Visible Layers via Occlusion-Aware Image Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11818)
*Binhao Wang,Shihao Zhao,Bo Cheng,Qiuyu Ji,Yuhang Ma,Liebucha Wu,Shanyuan Liu,Dawei Leng,Yuhui Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RevealLayer，一种基于扩散模型的框架，用于将自然图像分解为多个RGBA图层，结合区域感知注意力、遮挡引导适配器和复合损失函数，并构建了RevealLayer-100K数据集和RevealLayerBench基准，在图层分解任务上优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的图像图层分解方法在处理复杂自然图像时，在遮挡补全、图层解纠缠和前景边界精准度方面仍面临挑战，且缺乏高质量的多图层自然图像数据集。

Method: 提出RevealLayer框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 区域感知注意力模块（Region-Aware Attention），用于解纠缠隐藏图层和可见图层；(2) 遮挡引导适配器（Occlusion-Guided Adapter），利用上下文信息增强重叠区域；(3) 复合损失函数（composite loss），强化锐利alpha边界并抑制残留伪影。同时构建了由自动算法与人工标注协作生成的高质量多图层自然图像数据集RevealLayer-100K和基准RevealLayerBench。

Result: 大量实验表明，RevealLayer在图层分解任务上持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: RevealLayer通过创新的注意力机制、遮挡适配器和复合损失函数，结合高质量数据集，有效解决了复杂自然图像中的图层分解难题，性能超越现有方法。

Original Abstract: Recent diffusion-based approaches have made substantial progress in image layer decomposition. However, accurately decomposing complex natural images remains challenging due to difficulties in occlusion completion, robust layer disentanglement, and precise foreground boundaries. Moreover, the scarcity of high-quality multi-layer natural image datasets limits advancement. To address these challenges, we propose RevealLayer, a diffusion-based framework that decomposes an RGB image into multiple RGBA layers, enabling precise layer separation and reliable recovery of occluded content in natural images. RevealLayer incorporates three key components: (1) a Region-Aware Attention module to disentangle hidden and visible layers; (2) an Occlusion-Guided Adapter to leverage contextual information to enhance overlapping regions; and (3) a composite loss to enforce sharp alpha boundaries and suppress residual artifacts. To support training and evaluation, we introduce RevealLayer-100K, a high-quality multi-layer natural image constructed through a collaboration between automated algorithms and human annotation, and further establish RevealLayerBench for benchmarking layer decomposition in general natural scenes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RevealLayer consistently outperforms existing approaches in layer decomposition.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，基于扩散的方法在图像图层分解方面取得了显著进展。然而，由于遮挡补全、鲁棒的图层解纠缠以及精确的前景边界等方面的困难，准确分解复杂自然图像仍然具有挑战性。此外，高质量多图层自然图像数据集的稀缺也制约了该领域的发展。为解决这些问题，我们提出了RevealLayer——一种基于扩散的框架，能够将RGB图像分解为多个RGBA图层，实现精确的图层分离和自然图像中被遮挡内容的可靠恢复。RevealLayer包含三个关键组件：(1) 区域感知注意力模块，用于解纠缠隐藏图层与可见图层；(2) 遮挡引导适配器，利用上下文信息增强重叠区域；(3) 复合损失函数，用于强化锐利的alpha边界并抑制残留伪影。为支持训练与评估，我们引入了RevealLayer-100K——一个通过自动算法与人工标注协作构建的高质量多图层自然图像数据集，并进一步建立了RevealLayerBench，用于通用自然场景中图层分解的基准测试。大量实验表明，RevealLayer在图层分解任务上持续优于现有方法。

</details>


### [62] [REFNet++: Multi-Task Efficient Fusion of Camera and Radar Sensor Data in Bird's-Eye Polar View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11824)
*Kavin Chandrasekaran,Sorin Grigorescu,Gijs Dubbelman,Pavol Jancura*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于变分编码器-解码器架构的雷达与相机数据融合方法，将前视相机数据转换到鸟瞰极坐标域，同时从雷达原始距离-多普勒频谱恢复角度信息生成距离-方位角特征，实现两种模态在统一域中的高效对齐与融合，用于车辆检测和自由空间分割任务。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 相机传感器能提供丰富的视觉信息，但在恶劣天气条件下表现不佳；雷达传感器成本低廉且对天气变化鲁棒，但输出噪声大、分类能力弱。现有融合方法往往侧重精度而忽略计算效率，因此需要一种兼顾精度与计算效率的多模态传感器融合方法。

Method: 采用变分编码器-解码器架构：一个编码器-解码器将前视相机数据转换为鸟瞰极坐标域，另一个编码器-解码器从原始距离-多普勒频谱中恢复角度信息生成距离-方位角特征，使两种模态在兼容的域中对齐，实现鲁棒且高效的传感器融合。

Result: 在RADIal数据集上，针对车辆检测和自由空间分割任务，将所提融合策略与最先进方法进行了比较评估。

Conclusion: 通过在统一域中对齐雷达和相机数据，所提方法能够实现鲁棒且高效的多模态传感器融合，在车辆检测和自由空间分割任务上表现优异。

Original Abstract: A realistic view of the vehicle's surroundings is generally offered by camera sensors, which is crucial for environmental perception. Affordable radar sensors, on the other hand, are becoming invaluable due to their robustness in variable weather conditions. However, because of their noisy output and reduced classification capability, they work best when combined with other sensor data. Specifically, we address the challenge of multimodal sensor fusion by aligning radar and camera data in a unified domain, prioritizing not only accuracy, but also computational efficiency. Our work leverages the raw range-Doppler (RD) spectrum from radar and front-view camera images as inputs. To enable effective fusion, we employ a variational encoder-decoder architecture that learns the transformation of front-view camera data into the Bird's-Eye View (BEV) polar domain. Concurrently, a radar encoder-decoder learns to recover the angle information from the RD data that produce Range-Azimuth (RA) features. This alignment ensures that both modalities are represented in a compatible domain, facilitating robust and efficient sensor fusion. We evaluated our fusion strategy for vehicle detection and free space segmentation against state-of-the-art methods using the RADIal dataset.

中文摘要翻译: 相机传感器通常能够提供车辆周围环境的真实视图，这对环境感知至关重要。另一方面，价格实惠的雷达传感器因其在多变天气条件下的鲁棒性而变得不可或缺。然而，由于其输出噪声大且分类能力弱，雷达传感器在与其他传感器数据结合时效果最佳。具体而言，我们通过将雷达和相机数据对齐到统一域中来应对多模态传感器融合的挑战，不仅优先考虑准确性，还注重计算效率。我们的工作利用原始距离-多普勒频谱和前置摄像头图像作为输入。为了实现有效融合，我们采用变分编码器-解码器架构，学习将前置摄像头数据转换为鸟瞰极坐标域。同时，雷达编码器-解码器学习从距离-多普勒数据中恢复角度信息，生成距离-方位角特征。这种对齐确保两种模态在兼容的域中表示，从而促进鲁棒且高效的传感器融合。我们使用RADIal数据集，针对车辆检测和自由空间分割任务，将我们的融合策略与最先进方法进行了评估。

</details>


### [63] [UniVLR: Unifying Text and Vision in Visual Latent Reasoning for Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11856)
*Houcheng Jiang,Jiajun Fu,Junfeng Fang,Chen Gao,Xiang Wang,Xiangnan He,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UniVLR统一视觉潜在推理框架，将文本推理和辅助视觉证据融合为共享视觉工作空间，仅通过视觉潜在令牌进行推理，无需外部工具调用和冗长文本思维链，在视觉推理任务上超越此前方法且生成更少推理令牌。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉潜在推理方法仍依赖显式的文本思维链与视觉潜在令牌交错使用，这种交错设计限制了效率，并使推理在分离的文本和视觉通道中碎片化，缺乏统一的推理范式。

Method: UniVLR将文本推理轨迹与辅助图像一起渲染为统一的视觉表示，并通过学习将其压缩为紧凑的视觉潜在令牌；推理时仅通过视觉潜在令牌进行推理，直接解码最终答案，避免外部工具调用和冗长文本推理。

Result: 在真实世界感知和视觉推理任务上，UniVLR超越了先前的视觉潜在推理方法，同时生成的推理令牌数量大幅减少。

Conclusion: 提出了一种更统一且高效的视觉推理范式，使多模态大语言模型能够仅通过视觉潜在表示进行思考，无需文本思维链的介入。

Original Abstract: Multimodal large language models are increasingly expected to perform thinking with images, yet existing visual latent reasoning methods still rely on explicit textual chain-of-thought interleaved with visual latent tokens. This interleaved design limits efficiency and keeps reasoning fragmented across separate text and vision channels. We propose UniVLR, a unified visual latent reasoning framework that treats textual reasoning and auxiliary visual evidence as a shared visual workspace. Instead of preserving text CoT as an independent inference-time path, UniVLR renders reasoning traces together with auxiliary images and learns to compress this unified representation into compact visual latent tokens. At inference time, the model reasons only through visual latents and directly decodes the final answer, avoiding both external tool calls and verbose text reasoning. Experiments on real-world perception and visual reasoning tasks show that UniVLR outperforms prior visual latent reasoning methods while using substantially fewer generated reasoning tokens, suggesting a more unified and efficient paradigm for visual thinking in MLLMs.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型越来越被期望能够结合图像进行思考，然而现有的视觉潜在推理方法仍然依赖于显式的文本思维链与视觉潜在令牌交错使用。这种交错设计限制了效率，并使推理在分离的文本和视觉通道中碎片化。我们提出UniVLR，一种统一的视觉潜在推理框架，将文本推理和辅助视觉证据视为共享的视觉工作空间。UniVLR并非将文本思维链保留为独立的推理路径，而是将推理轨迹与辅助图像一起渲染，并学习将这种统一表示压缩为紧凑的视觉潜在令牌。在推理时，模型仅通过视觉潜在令牌进行推理，直接解码最终答案，避免了外部工具调用和冗长的文本推理。在真实世界感知和视觉推理任务上的实验表明，UniVLR优于先前的视觉潜在推理方法，同时生成的推理令牌数量大幅减少，为多模态大语言模型中的视觉思考提供了一种更统一且高效的范式。

</details>


### [64] [When Brains Disagree: Biological Ambiguity Underlies the Challenge of Amyloid PET Synthesis from Structural MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11867)
*Louise E. G. Baron,Ross Callaghan,David M. Cash,Philip S. Weston,Hojjat Azadbakht,Hui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过对照实验证明，MRI到淀粉样蛋白PET合成性能受限的根本原因在于数据中固有的生物学模糊性（神经退行性与淀粉样蛋白病理在时间上的解耦），而非模型架构的复杂程度。引入正交生物信息（血浆生物标志物）作为多模态输入可解决此模糊性，提升并稳定合成性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 已有研究表明MRI到淀粉样蛋白PET合成模型在不同研究中的表现差异巨大，且日益复杂的架构并未带来一致的性能提升。作者推测根本原因在于生物学上的固有模糊性：MRI捕获的是神经退行性变化，而PET测量的是淀粉样蛋白病理，两者在阿尔茨海默病中常存在时间上的解耦，导致相似的MRI模式可能对应不同的淀粉样蛋白状态，形成一对多的模糊映射。本文旨在通过科学实验验证这一假设。

Method: 设计了两组对照实验：(1) 通过按淀粉样蛋白和神经退行状态对配对的MRI-PET数据进行分层，控制训练数据分布。在受控设计下使用两种标准合成模型，比较生物学上无歧义映射与引入数据歧义后的性能差异。(2) 引入正交生物学信息（血浆生物标志物）作为多模态输入，观察是否能解决模糊性问题。

Result: (1) 生物学上无歧义的映射在隔离条件下是可学习的，但引入数据歧义后性能骤降，表明数据分布中的歧义而非模型容量限制了性能。(2) 引入血浆生物标志物作为多模态输入后，合成性能提升且稳定性恢复。

Conclusion: MRI到淀粉样蛋白PET合成中有限且不一致的性能是由内在的生物学模糊性所解释的，稳定且有意义的进展需要多模态融合，而非仅仅依赖架构复杂性的提升。

Original Abstract: Structural MRI-to-amyloid PET synthesis has been proposed as a non-invasive alternative for amyloid assessment in Alzheimer's disease (AD). However, reported performance of identical models varies widely across studies, and increasingly complex architectures have not led to consistent gains. This inconsistency is thought to be caused by a fundamental biological ambiguity: MRI captures neurodegeneration, while PET measures amyloid pathology - two processes that are often temporally decoupled in AD. As a result, similar MRI patterns may correspond to different amyloid states, creating ambiguous one-to-many mappings. MRI-to-amyloid PET synthesis may therefore be intrinsically ill-posed; however, this idea has yet to be tested scientifically. The aim of this work is to test this hypothesis through two controlled experiments. We first control the training distribution by stratifying paired MRI-PET data by amyloid and neurodegeneration status. Using two standard synthesis models under a controlled design, we show that biologically unambiguous mappings are learnable in isolation, but performance collapses when data ambiguity is introduced. This demonstrates that ambiguity in the data distribution, rather than architectural capacity, constrains performance. Second, we show that introducing orthogonal biological information in the form of plasma biomarkers resolves this ambiguity. When multimodal inputs are incorporated, performance improves and stability is restored. Together, these findings suggest that limited and inconsistent performance in MRI-to-amyloid PET synthesis is explained by intrinsic biological ambiguity, and that stable, meaningful progress requires multimodal integration rather than architectural complexity.

中文摘要翻译: 结构MRI到淀粉样蛋白PET合成已被提出作为阿尔茨海默病中淀粉样蛋白评估的一种非侵入性替代方法。然而，相同模型在不同研究中的报告性能差异巨大，且日益复杂的架构并未带来一致性的性能提升。这种不一致性被认为是由一种基本的生物学模糊性引起的：MRI捕获的是神经退行性变化，而PET测量的是淀粉样蛋白病理——这两个过程在阿尔茨海默病中常常在时间上是解耦的。因此，相似的MRI模式可能对应不同的淀粉样蛋白状态，形成模糊的一对多映射。MRI到淀粉样蛋白PET合成因此可能在本质上是不适定的；然而，这一想法尚未经过科学检验。本文旨在通过两个对照实验来验证这一假设。我们首先通过按淀粉样蛋白和神经退行状态对配对的MRI-PET数据进行分层，来控制训练数据分布。在受控设计下使用两种标准合成模型，我们证明生物学上无歧义的映射在隔离条件下是可学习的，但一旦引入数据歧义，性能便会崩溃。这表明限制性能的是数据分布中的歧义，而非模型容量。其次，我们证明以血浆生物标志物形式引入正交生物学信息可以解决这种歧义。当纳入多模态输入时，性能得到提升且稳定性得以恢复。综上所述，这些发现表明MRI到淀粉样蛋白PET合成中有限且不一致的性能是由内在的生物学模糊性所解释的，而稳定、有意义的进展需要多模态融合，而非架构复杂性。

</details>


### [65] [FIS-DiT: Breaking the Few-Step Video Inference Barrier via Training-Free Frame Interleaved Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11869)
*Jian Tang,Jiawei Fan,Qingbin Liu,Zheng Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FIS-DiT，一种无需训练、算子无关的框架，通过在潜帧维度上利用帧间稀疏性和结构一致性，将视频DiT的每步推理延迟降低2.11–2.41倍，且性能几乎无损。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频DiT加速方法主要利用去噪轨迹中的冗余，但在少步数场景下时间状态稀疏导致特征复用或预测建模失效，加速收益递减。需要一种新范式来突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出帧交错稀疏执行策略（FIS），在模型层级间交错操作不同帧子集，逐步刷新所有潜帧位置，避免全尺度块计算，实现计算量与性能的平衡。

Result: 在Wan 2.2和HunyuanVideo 1.5上，FIS-DiT实现2.11–2.41倍加速，VBench-Q和CLIP指标几乎无退化。

Conclusion: FIS-DiT为实时高清视频生成提供了一条可扩展、鲁棒的加速路径，证明了将优化焦点从时间轨迹转向潜帧维度的有效性。

Original Abstract: While the overall inference latency of Video Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) can be substantially reduced through model distillation, per-step inference latency remains a critical bottleneck. Existing acceleration paradigms primarily exploit redundancy across the denoising trajectory; however, we identify a limitation where these step-wise strategies encounter diminishing returns in few-step regimes. In such scenarios, the scarcity of temporal states prevents effective feature reuse or predictive modeling, creating a formidable barrier to further acceleration. To overcome this, we propose Frame Interleaved Sparsity DiT (FIS-DiT), a training-free and operator-agnostic framework that shifts the optimization focus from the temporal trajectory to the latent frame dimension. Our approach is motivated by an intrinsic duality within this dimension: the existence of frame-wise sparsity that permits reduced computation, coupled with a structural consistency where each frame position remains equally vital to the global spatiotemporal context. Leveraging this insight, we implement Frame Interleaved Sparsity (FIS) as an execution strategy that manipulates frame subsets across the model hierarchy, refreshing all latent positions without requiring full-scale block computation. Empirical evaluations on Wan 2.2 and HunyuanVideo 1.5 demonstrate that FIS-DiT consistently achieves 2.11--2.41$\times$ speedup with negligible degradation across VBench-Q and CLIP metrics, providing a scalable and robust pathway toward real-time high-definition video generation.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管视频扩散Transformer（Video DiTs）的整体推理延迟可以通过模型蒸馏大幅降低，但每步推理延迟仍然是关键的瓶颈。现有加速范式主要利用去噪轨迹中的冗余；然而，我们识别出一个局限性：在少步数场景下，这些逐步骤策略会遇到收益递减。在这种情况下，时间状态的稀缺性阻碍了有效的特征复用或预测建模，形成了进一步加速的严峻障碍。为克服这一问题，我们提出帧交错稀疏DiT（FIS-DiT），一种无需训练且算子无关的框架，将优化焦点从时间轨迹转向潜帧维度。我们的方法受该维度内在二元性的启发：存在帧级稀疏性允许减少计算量，同时每个帧位置对全局时空上下文的重要性保持结构一致性。利用这一洞察，我们实现帧交错稀疏（FIS）作为一种执行策略，在模型层级间操纵帧子集，无需全尺度块计算即可刷新所有潜帧位置。在Wan 2.2和HunyuanVideo 1.5上的实证评估表明，FIS-DiT在VBench-Q和CLIP指标上实现2.11–2.41倍加速且性能退化可忽略不计，为实时高清视频生成提供了可扩展且鲁棒的路径。

</details>


### [66] [$h$-control: Training-Free Camera Control via Block-Conditional Gibbs Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11871)
*Yuzhu Wang,Xi Ye,Duo Su,Yangyang Xu,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出h-control，通过在外层硬替换引导步骤中嵌入内循环块条件伪吉布斯细化，解决无训练相机控制中轨迹遵循与视觉质量间的权衡问题，在RealEstate10K和DAVIS上取得最优FVD。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无训练方法在轨迹遵循与视觉质量之间难以平衡，且启发式引导强度调参缺乏鲁棒性。

Method: 对采样器进行结构性改造：每个外层硬替换引导步骤增加一个内循环块条件伪吉布斯细化，对未观测补集在同一噪声水平下进行精炼，并利用条件局部性将未观测补集划分为3D块，每个块由自适应混合指示器跟踪，冻结已收敛的块以加速高维视频潜变量的收敛。

Result: 在RealEstate10K和DAVIS数据集上，h-control在FVD指标上优于所有七种无训练和基于训练的竞争对手，且在每一个报告指标上都优于所有无训练基线。

Conclusion: h-control通过结构化的采样器改进，在无训练相机控制任务中同时实现了更好的轨迹 adherence 和视觉质量，具有理论收敛保证。

Original Abstract: Training-free camera control for pretrained flow-matching video generators is a partial-observation inverse problem: a depth-warped guidance video supplies noisy evidence on a subset of latent sites, which the sampler must reconcile with the pretrained prior. Existing methods struggle to balance the trade-off between trajectory adherence and visual quality and the heuristic guidance-strength tuning lacks robustness. We propose \textbf{$h$-control}, which resolves this dilemma through a structural change to the sampler: each outer hard-replacement guidance step is augmented with an inner-loop \emph{block-conditional pseudo-Gibbs refinement} on the unobserved complement at the same noise level, with provable convergence to the partial-observation conditional data law. To accelerate convergence on high-dimensional video latents, we exploit their conditional locality, partitioning the unobserved complement into 3D patches, each tracked by a custom mixing indicator that adaptively freezes converged patches. On RealEstate10K and DAVIS, \textbf{$h$-control} attains the best FVD against all seven training-free and training-based competitors, outperforming every training-free baseline on every reported metric.

中文摘要翻译: 针对预训练流匹配视频生成器的无训练相机控制是一个部分观测逆问题：深度扭曲引导视频为潜在变量子集提供带噪证据，采样器必须将其与预训练先验协调。现有方法难以平衡轨迹遵循与视觉质量之间的权衡，且启发式引导强度调参缺乏鲁棒性。我们提出\textbf{$h$-control}，通过对采样器进行结构性改造来解决这一困境：每个外层硬替换引导步骤都增加一个内循环\emph{块条件伪吉布斯细化}，对同一噪声水平下的未观测补集进行精炼，并具有收敛到部分观测条件数据分布的可证明保证。为加速高维视频潜变量的收敛，我们利用其条件局部性，将未观测补集划分为3D块，每个块由自定义混合指示器跟踪，自适应地冻结已收敛的块。在RealEstate10K和DAVIS上，\textbf{$h$-control}在所有七种无训练和基于训练的竞争对手中取得了最佳FVD，并且在每个报告指标上都优于所有无训练基线。

</details>


### [67] [Learning Subspace-Preserving Sparse Attention Graphs from Heterogeneous Multiview Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11881)
*Jie Chen,Yuanbiao Gou,Chuanbin Liu,Zhu Wang,Xi Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种稀疏注意力图学习（SAGL）方法，通过双线性注意力分解、动态稀疏门控和α-entmax结构化稀疏投影，从异构多视图数据中学习子空间保持的稀疏注意力图，在无监督迁移学习中取得了最先进性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无监督迁移学习方法在利用多视图互补信息时难以忠实恢复内在子空间结构，无法构建保留潜在子空间结构的稀疏相似度图以实现异构视图间的语义对齐。

Method: 1) 双线性注意力分解机制捕获高维特征间的非对称相似性；2) 动态稀疏门控机制预测特征特定的压缩因子，自适应控制邻居的拓扑贡献；3) 通过α-entmax进行结构化稀疏投影，为每个视图生成子空间保持的稀疏注意力图；4) 利用视图特定图进行稀疏信息聚合，得到判别性表示。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，SAGL一致性地优于最先进的无监督迁移学习方法。

Conclusion: SAGL通过稀疏注意力图学习有效解决了异构多视图数据的子空间结构恢复问题，并提供了可微稀疏注意力与概率单纯形约束之间的理论桥梁。

Original Abstract: The high-dimensional features extracted from large-scale unlabeled data via various pretrained models with diverse architectures are referred to as heterogeneous multiview data. Most existing unsupervised transfer learning methods fail to faithfully recover intrinsic subspace structures when exploiting complementary information across multiple views. Therefore, a fundamental challenge involves constructing sparse similarity graphs that preserve these underlying subspace structures for achieving semantic alignment across heterogeneous views. In this paper, we propose a sparse attention graph learning (SAGL) method that learns subspace-preserving sparse attention graphs from heterogeneous multiview data. Specifically, we introduce a bilinear attention factorization scheme to capture asymmetric similarities among the high-dimensional features, which breaks the symmetry bottleneck that is inherent in the traditional representation learning techniques. A dynamic sparsity gating mechanism then predicts a feature-specific compression factor for adaptively controlling the topological contributions of neighbors. Furthermore, we employ a structured sparse projection via $α$-entmax to generate subspace-preserving sparse attention graphs for individual views. SAGL leverages these view-specific graphs to conduct sparse information aggregation, yielding discriminative representations for multiview learning tasks. In addition, we provide a rigorous theoretical analysis that bridges differentiable sparse attention and probability simplex constraints. Extensive experiments conducted on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that SAGL consistently outperforms the state-of-the-art unsupervised transfer learning approaches.

中文摘要翻译: 从具有不同架构的各种预训练模型中提取的大规模无标签数据的高维特征被称为异构多视图数据。现有的大多数无监督迁移学习方法在利用跨多个视图的互补信息时，无法忠实地恢复内在的子空间结构。因此，一个基本挑战在于构建保留这些底层子空间结构的稀疏相似度图，以实现异构视图间的语义对齐。本文提出了一种稀疏注意力图学习（SAGL）方法，该方法从异构多视图数据中学习子空间保持的稀疏注意力图。具体而言，我们引入了一种双线性注意力分解方案来捕获高维特征间的非对称相似性，这打破了传统表示学习技术中固有的对称瓶颈。随后，一种动态稀疏门控机制预测特征特定的压缩因子，以自适应地控制邻居的拓扑贡献。此外，我们通过α-entmax采用结构化稀疏投影，为单个视图生成子空间保持的稀疏注意力图。SAGL利用这些视图特定的图进行稀疏信息聚合，为多视图学习任务生成判别性表示。同时，我们提供了连接可微稀疏注意力与概率单纯形约束的严格理论分析。在多个基准数据集上进行的广泛实验表明，SAGL一致性地优于最先进的无监督迁移学习方法。

</details>


### [68] [Few-Shot Synthetic Data Generation with Diffusion Models for Downstream Vision Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11898)
*Daniil Dushenev,Nazariy Karpov,Daniil Zinovjev,Alexander Gorin,Konstantin Kulikov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级合成数据增强流程，利用LoRA微调扩散模型，仅用20-50张真实图像即可生成合成样本，有效缓解视觉识别中稀有类别的类别不平衡问题。在胸部X光病理分类和工业表面裂纹检测两个异构领域上的实验表明，合成增强能一致提升稀有类别的召回率和F1分数。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 视觉识别中类别不平衡长期存在，尤其在安全关键领域，收集正样本成本高昂且罕见事件本质上代表性不足，亟需一种简单可扩展的稀有类别增强方法。

Method: 提出轻量级合成数据增强流程：先对稀有类别的20-50张真实图像微调LoRA适配器，再利用预训练扩散模型生成合成样本用于训练，并通过系统变化合成-真实比例进行实验。

Result: 在两个异构领域（NIH ChestX-ray14胸部X光病理分类和Magnetic Tile Defect工业表面裂纹检测）中，仅使用真实数据训练相比，合成增强一致提升了稀有类别的召回率和F1分数；性能在适度合成增强时提升，随合成比例增加呈现边际递减效应。

Conclusion: LoRA适配的扩散模型为稀有类别增强提供了一种简单且可扩展的机制，能够在数据稀缺的异构视觉域中实现有效学习。

Original Abstract: Class imbalance is a persistent challenge in visual recognition, particularly in safety-critical domains where collecting positive examples is expensive and rare events are inherently underrepresented. We propose a lightweight synthetic data augmentation pipeline that fine-tunes a LoRA adapter on as few as 20-50 real images of a rare class and uses a pretrained diffusion model to generate synthetic samples for training.
  We systematically vary the synthetic-to-real ratio and evaluate the approach across two structurally different domains: chest X-ray pathology classification (NIH ChestX-ray14) and industrial surface crack detection (Magnetic Tile Defect dataset). All evaluations are performed on held-out sets of real images only.
  Across both domains, synthetic augmentation consistently improves rare-class recall and F1 compared to training with real data alone. Performance improves with moderate synthetic augmentation and shows diminishing returns as the synthetic ratio increases.
  These results suggest that LoRA-adapted diffusion models provide a simple and scalable mechanism for augmenting rare classes, enabling effective learning in data-scarce scenarios across heterogeneous visual domains.

中文摘要翻译: 类别不平衡是视觉识别中一个长期存在的挑战，尤其是在安全关键领域，收集正样本成本高昂且罕见事件本质上代表性不足。我们提出了一种轻量级合成数据增强流程，该流程在少至20-50张稀有类别的真实图像上微调LoRA适配器，并利用预训练扩散模型生成用于训练的合成样本。我们系统地改变合成与真实比例，并在两个结构异构的领域——胸部X光病理分类（NIH ChestX-ray14数据集）和工业表面裂纹检测（Magnetic Tile Defect数据集）上评估该方法。所有评估仅在保留的真实图像测试集上进行。在两个领域中，与仅使用真实数据训练相比，合成增强一致地提高了稀有类别的召回率和F1分数。性能在适度合成增强时提升，并随合成比例增加呈现边际递减效应。这些结果表明，LoRA适配的扩散模型为增强稀有类别提供了一种简单且可扩展的机制，能够在数据稀缺的场景下跨异构视觉域实现有效学习。

</details>


### [69] [Mobile Traffic Camera Calibration from Road Geometry for UAV-Based Traffic Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11900)
*Alexey Popov,Natalia Trukhina,Vadim Vashkelis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级流水线，利用可见道路几何结构（车道线、路缘、人行横道）将单目倾斜无人机视频转换为局部度量鸟瞰图（BEV）表示，从而支持车辆轨迹分析与3D立方体重建。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 无人机可提供灵活交通监控，但原始视频在透视图像坐标系中观测车辆运动，难以用于稳定的度量道路坐标系下的交通分析。现有方法依赖复杂传感器或高计算成本，需要一种轻量级、可部署的方案将单目无人机视频转化为可解释的交通监控数据。

Method: 利用可见道路几何结构（车道线、路缘、人行横道）估计从图像坐标到度量地面平面坐标的道路平面单应性矩阵；将来自数据集标注或检测器的车辆观测点通过估计的地面接触点投影到BEV坐标系；在BEV空间中估计车辆方向、速度、航向和动态3D立方体。

Result: 在UAVDT数据集M1401序列上，40帧采样产生632个度量立方体实例（23条轨迹）。结果表明道路几何标定可将单目无人机视频转化为可解释的交通监控分析（BEV轨迹与3D立方体可视化），同时揭示远场车辆对单应性误差敏感、完全自动标定不如人工验证可靠、单平面假设在非平面区域存在局限。

Conclusion: 所提出的轻量级流水线为可部署的无人机交通摄像头和未来实时交通数字孪生系统提供了实用基础。

Original Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) can provide flexible traffic surveillance where fixed roadside cameras are unavailable, costly, or impractical. However, raw UAV video is difficult to use for traffic analytics because vehicle motion is observed in perspective image coordinates rather than in a stable metric road coordinate system. This paper presents a lightweight pipeline for converting monocular oblique UAV traffic video into a local metric bird's-eye-view (BEV) representation. Visible road geometry, including lane markings, road borders, and crosswalks, is used to estimate a road-plane homography from image coordinates to metric ground-plane coordinates. Vehicle observations from dataset annotations or detectors are then projected to BEV using estimated ground contact points. The resulting trajectories support estimation of vehicle direction, speed, heading, and dynamic 3D cuboids on the road plane. We evaluate the pipeline on UAVDT using ground-truth annotations to isolate calibration and geometric reconstruction from detector and tracker errors. For sequence M1401, 40 sampled frames from img000001-img000196 produce 632 metric cuboid instances across 23 tracks. Results show that road-geometry calibration can transform monocular UAV footage into interpretable traffic-camera-style analytics, including BEV tracks and synchronized 3D cuboid visualizations. They also reveal key limitations: far-field vehicles are sensitive to homography errors, manual validation is currently more reliable than fully automatic calibration, and the single-plane assumption limits performance in non-planar or ambiguous road regions. The proposed pipeline provides a practical foundation for deployable UAV traffic cameras and future real-time traffic digital-twin systems.

中文摘要翻译: 无人机（UAV）可在固定 roadside 摄像头不可用、成本高昂或不切实际的情况下提供灵活的交通监控。然而，原始无人机视频难以直接用于交通分析，因为车辆运动是在透视图像坐标系中观测的，而非稳定的度量道路坐标系。本文提出了一种轻量级流水线，用于将单目倾斜无人机交通视频转换为局部度量鸟瞰图（BEV）表示。利用可见的道路几何结构，包括车道标线、路缘和人行横道，估计从图像坐标到度量地面平面坐标的道路平面单应性矩阵。然后，将来自数据集标注或检测器的车辆观测点通过估计的地面接触点投影到BEV空间。生成的轨迹支持在道路平面上估计车辆方向、速度、航向以及动态3D立方体。我们在UAVDT数据集上使用真值标注评估该流水线，以隔离标定和几何重建与检测器和跟踪器误差的影响。对于M1401序列，从img000001到img000196中采样的40帧产生了632个度量立方体实例，覆盖23条轨迹。结果表明，道路几何标定可以将单目无人机画面转化为可解释的交通监控风格分析，包括BEV轨迹和同步的3D立方体可视化。结果也揭示了关键局限性：远场车辆对单应性误差敏感，当前人工验证比全自动标定更可靠，以及单平面假设在非平面或模糊道路区域限制了性能。所提出的流水线为可部署的无人机交通摄像头和未来实时交通数字孪生系统提供了实用基础。

</details>


### [70] [Beyond Point-wise Neural Collapse: A Topology-Aware Hierarchical Classifier for Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11904)
*Huiyu Yi,Zhiming Xu,Dunwei Tu,Zhicheng Wang,Baile Xu,Furao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对类别增量学习中NCM分类器因特征非线性漂移和训练不足导致类流形坍塌不充分、单点原型次优的问题，提出HC-SOINN分类器（基于层次聚类SOINN捕获流形拓扑结构）和STAR方法（通过残差点轨迹跟踪主动适应非线性漂移），在七个SOTA方法上取得一致改进。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: NCM分类器在CIL中抗遗忘能力强，但神经坍塌（NC）理论要求的特征坍塌为单一点在非线性特征漂移和训练不足时难以实现，类呈现为复杂流形而非坍塌点，导致单点NCM次优。因此需要一种能捕获并适应复杂流形拓扑结构的分类器。

Method: 提出HC-SOINN分类器，通过"局部到全局"表示捕获类流形的拓扑结构；进一步提出STAR方法，利用细粒度逐点轨迹跟踪机制主动变形已学拓扑，精确适应复杂非线性特征漂移。

Result: 将HC-SOINN集成到七个SOTA方法的原始分类器替换中，均取得一致性改进；理论分析与Procrustes距离实验验证了框架对流行形变的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效处理CIL中特征的非线性漂移和类流形复杂性问题，具有强有效性和鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: The Nearest Class Mean (NCM) classifier is widely favored in Class-Incremental Learning (CIL) for its superior resistance to catastrophic forgetting compared to Fully Connected layers. While Neural Collapse (NC) theory supports NCM's optimality by assuming features collapse into single points, non-linear feature drift and insufficient training in CIL often prevent this ideal state. Consequently, classes manifest as complex manifolds rather than collapsed points, rendering the single-point NCM suboptimal. To address this, we propose Hierarchical-Cluster SOINN (HC-SOINN), a novel classifier that captures the topological structure of these manifolds via a ``local-to-global'' representation. Furthermore, we introduce Structure-Topology Alignment via Residuals (STAR) method, which employs a fine-grained pointwise trajectory tracking mechanism to actively deform the learned topology, allowing it to adapt precisely to complex non-linear feature drift. Theoretical analysis and Procrustes distance experiments validate our framework's resilience to manifold deformations. We integrated HC-SOINN into seven state-of-the-art methods by replacing their original classifiers, achieving consistent improvements that highlight the effectiveness and robustness of our approach. Code is available at https://github.com/yhyet/HC_SOINN.

中文摘要翻译: 最近邻类均值（NCM）分类器在类别增量学习（CIL）中因其相比全连接层具有更强的抗灾难性遗忘能力而广受欢迎。虽然神经坍塌（NC）理论通过假设特征坍塌为单一点来支持NCM的最优性，但非线性特征漂移和CIL中的训练不足常常阻碍这一理想状态的实现。因此，类表现为复杂流形而非坍塌点，使得单点NCM成为次优方案。为解决这一问题，我们提出层次聚类SOINN（HC-SOINN），一种通过"局部到全局"表示来捕获这些流形拓扑结构的新型分类器。此外，我们引入了基于残差的结构-拓扑对齐（STAR）方法，该方法采用细粒度的逐点轨迹跟踪机制主动变形已学拓扑，使其能够精确适应复杂的非线性特征漂移。理论分析和Procrustes距离实验验证了我们的框架对流行形变的鲁棒性。我们将HC-SOINN集成到七个最先进的方法中，替换其原始分类器，取得了一致的改进，突显了我们方法的有效性和鲁棒性。代码可在https://github.com/yhyet/HC_SOINN获取。

</details>


### [71] [Vector Scaffolding: Inter-Scale Orchestration for Differentiable Image Vectorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11913)
*Jaerin Lee,Kanggeon Lee,Kyoung Mu Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Vector Scaffolding，一种分层优化框架，通过内部梯度聚合、渐进分层和快速膨胀调度，解决可微分矢量图形优化中的拓扑坍塌问题，将优化速度提升2.5倍，PSNR提升高达1.4 dB。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有可微分矢量图形框架采用扁平优化（flat optimization），导致数百至数千条随机初始化的曲线盲目竞争像素级误差降低，引发拓扑坍塌——宏观结构被内部高频噪声扭曲，产生冗余且不可编辑的"多边形汤"，限制了实际可编辑性。

Method: 提出Vector Scaffolding分层优化框架：(1) 识别拓扑坍塌的关键原因是面积梯度和边界梯度之间的数学不平衡；(2) 引入内部梯度聚合（Interior Gradient Aggregation）稳定多尺度曲线混合的学习动态；(3) 采用渐进分层（Progressive Stratification）和快速膨胀调度（Rapid Inflation Scheduling），以极高学习率（×50）逐步稠密化矢量基元。

Result: 优化速度提升2.5倍，PSNR提升高达1.4 dB，超越此前最优方法。

Conclusion: 从扁平像素匹配转向结构化拓扑构建的分层优化框架能有效解决拓扑坍塌问题，显著提升矢量图形优化的效率和质量。

Original Abstract: Differentiable vector graphics have enabled powerful gradient-based optimization of vector primitives directly from raster images. However, existing frameworks formulate this as a flat optimization problem, forcing hundreds to thousands of randomly initialized curves to blindly compete for pixel-level error reduction. This disordered optimization leads to topology collapse, where macroscopic structures are distorted by internal high-frequency noise, resulting in a redundant and uneditable "polygon soup" that limits practical editability. To address this limitation, we propose Vector Scaffolding, a novel hierarchical optimization framework that shifts from flat pixel-matching to structured topological construction tailored for vector graphics. By identifying a key cause of topology collapse as the mathematical imbalance between area and boundary gradients, we introduce Interior Gradient Aggregation to stabilize the learning dynamics of multi-scale curve mixtures. Upon this stabilized landscape, we employ Progressive Stratification and Rapid Inflation Scheduling to progressively densify vector primitives with extremely high learning rates ($\times 50$). Experiments demonstrate that our approach accelerates optimization by $2.5\times$ while simultaneously improving PSNR by up to 1.4 dB over the previous state of the art.

中文摘要翻译: 可微分矢量图形技术已经能够直接从光栅图像中对矢量基元进行基于梯度的强大优化。然而，现有框架将这一问题建模为扁平优化，迫使数百到数千条随机初始化的曲线盲目竞争以降低像素级误差。这种无序优化会导致拓扑坍塌——宏观结构被内部高频噪声扭曲，产生冗余且不可编辑的"多边形汤"，限制了实际可编辑性。为解决这一局限，我们提出Vector Scaffolding，一种面向矢量图形的新型分层优化框架，从扁平的像素匹配转向结构化的拓扑构建。通过将拓扑坍塌的一个关键原因归结为面积梯度与边界梯度之间的数学不平衡，我们引入内部梯度聚合（Interior Gradient Aggregation）来稳定多尺度曲线混合的学习动态。在此稳定的优化景观上，我们采用渐进分层（Progressive Stratification）和快速膨胀调度（Rapid Inflation Scheduling），以极高学习率（×50）逐步稠密化矢量基元。实验表明，我们的方法将优化速度提升2.5倍，同时PSNR相比此前最优方法提高高达1.4 dB。

</details>


### [72] [RealDiffusion: Physics-informed Attention for Multi-character Storybook Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11927)
*Qi Zhao,Jun Chen,Ivor Tsang,Guang Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RealDiffusion 提出了一种基于热扩散物理先验的轻量级、无需训练的注意力机制，用于在扩散模型生成序列图像时平衡角色一致性与叙事动态性，显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现代扩散模型在生成单张图像时表现优异，但在扩展到序列生成时面临根本性挑战：如何在多角色一致性（避免角色身份丢失）与叙事动态性（避免故事停滞）之间取得平衡。现有方法在这两者权衡中表现不佳。

Method: 提出RealDiffusion统一框架，核心是一个轻量级、无需训练的物理信息注意力机制（Physics-informed Attention）：(1) 热扩散作为耗散先验，沿序列平均相邻特征并去除主体区域的高频噪声，抑制属性漂移、稳定跨帧身份；(2) 区域感知随机过程引入小扰动，探索邻近模态以防止坍塌，从而维持姿态变化和场景演进。该方法在推理阶段将可控物理先验注入自注意力层，无需额外训练。

Result: 大量实验证明，RealDiffusion在保持角色一致性的同时保留了叙事动态性，显著优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过将特征演化建模为可配置的物理系统，该方法有效正则化了时空关系，且不会抑制由提示驱动的有意变化，成功解决了序列生成中一致性与动态性的核心矛盾。

Original Abstract: While modern diffusion models excel at generating diverse single images, extending this to sequential generation reveals a fundamental challenge: balancing narrative dynamism with multi-character coherence. Existing methods often falter at this trade-off, leading to artifacts where characters lose their identity or the story stagnates. To resolve this critical tension, we introduce RealDiffusion, a unified framework designed to reconcile robust coherence with narrative dynamism. Heat diffusion serves as a dissipative prior that averages neighboring features along the sequence and removes high-frequency noise within the subject region. This suppresses attribute drift and stabilizes identity across frames. A region-aware stochastic process then introduces small perturbations that explore nearby modes and prevent collapse so the story maintains pose change and scene evolution. We thus introduce a lightweight, training-free Physics-informed Attention mechanism that injects controllable physical priors into the self-attention layers during inference. By modeling feature evolution as a configurable physical system, our method regularizes spatio-temporal relationships without suppressing intentional, prompt-driven changes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RealDiffusion achieves substantial gains in character coherence while preserving narrative dynamism, outperforming state-of-the-art approaches. Code is available at https://github.com/ShmilyQi-CN/RealDiffusion.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管现代扩散模型在生成多样化的单张图像方面表现出色，但将其扩展到序列生成时揭示了一个根本性挑战：平衡叙事动态性与多角色一致性。现有方法往往在这两者权衡中表现不佳，导致角色身份丢失或故事停滞的伪影。为解决这一关键矛盾，我们提出了RealDiffusion，一个旨在协调强一致性与叙事动态性的统一框架。热扩散作为一种耗散先验，沿序列平均相邻特征并移除主体区域的高频噪声，从而抑制属性漂移并稳定跨帧身份。随后，区域感知随机过程引入小扰动，探索邻近模态并防止坍塌，使故事能够维持姿态变化和场景演进。我们因此提出了一种轻量级、无需训练的物理信息注意力机制，在推理阶段将可控物理先验注入自注意力层。通过将特征演化建模为可配置的物理系统，我们的方法正则化了时空关系，同时不抑制由提示驱动的有意变化。大量实验证明，RealDiffusion在保持角色一致性的同时取得了显著提升，同时保留了叙事动态性，优于当前最先进的方法。代码已开源：https://github.com/ShmilyQi-CN/RealDiffusion。

</details>


### [73] [Learn to Think: Improving Multimodal Reasoning through Vision-Aware Self-Improvement Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11931)
*Qihuang Zhong,Liang Ding,Wenjie Xuan,Juhua Liu,Bo Du,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VISTA提出了一种视觉感知的自改进训练框架，通过前缀重采样策略和视觉感知注意力分数，解决了多模态大语言模型自改进训练中的数据不平衡和语言先验偏差问题，在多种后训练场景下显著提升多模态推理性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MLLMs的自改进训练存在两个问题：1) 数据不平衡，简单样本被过度训练而困难样本训练不足；2) 语言先验偏差，模型过度依赖语言先验而忽略视觉线索。

Method: 1) 前缀重采样策略：重用部分正确的推理轨迹以高效收集数据；2) 视觉感知注意力分数：量化模型对视觉信息的关注程度。

Result: VISTA可应用于监督微调和偏好学习等多种后训练场景，在多个MLLM和任务上有效提升多模态推理性能，例如为Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct带来最高+13.66%的平均性能提升。

Conclusion: VISTA是一种有效的视觉感知自改进训练框架，能够弥补现有自改进训练的不足，显著增强MLLMs的多模态推理能力。

Original Abstract: Post-training with explicit reasoning traces is common to improve the reasoning capabilities of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). However, acquiring high-quality reasoning traces is often costly and time-consuming. Hence, the self-improvement paradigm has emerged, enabling MLLMs to self-generate reasoning traces for training without external supervision. Despite its effectiveness, we reveal two shortcomings in the self-improvement training of MLLMs: 1) data imbalance, where simple samples are over-trained, but the challenging yet crucial samples are under-trained; 2) language prior bias, where MLLMs overly rely on linguistic priors while neglecting the visual cues. To this end, we propose VISTA, a vision-aware self-improvement training framework for enhancing the multimodal reasoning of MLLMs. Specifically, VISTA first introduces a prefix resampling strategy to reuse the partial correct reasoning traces for efficient data collection, and then designs a vision-aware attention score to quantify the model's focus on visual information. Extensive experiments show that VISTA can be applied to various post-training scenarios, i.e., supervised fine-tuning and preference learning, and effectively enhances the multimodal reasoning performance across various MLLMs and tasks, e.g., bringing up to +13.66% average performance gains for Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct.

中文摘要翻译: 使用显式推理轨迹进行后训练是提升多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）推理能力的常见方法。然而，获取高质量的推理轨迹通常成本高昂且耗时。因此，自改进范式应运而生，使MLLMs能够自生成推理轨迹用于训练，无需外部监督。尽管该方法有效，但我们揭示了MLLMs自改进训练中的两个缺陷：1）数据不平衡，简单样本被过度训练，而具有挑战性但至关重要的样本训练不足；2）语言先验偏差，MLLMs过度依赖语言先验而忽视视觉线索。为此，我们提出了VISTA，一种面向视觉感知的自改进训练框架，用于增强MLLMs的多模态推理能力。具体而言，VISTA首先引入前缀重采样策略，重用部分正确的推理轨迹以实现高效数据收集，然后设计视觉感知注意力分数来量化模型对视觉信息的关注程度。大量实验表明，VISTA可应用于各种后训练场景（即监督微调和偏好学习），并在多种MLLM和任务上有效提升多模态推理性能，例如为Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct带来高达+13.66%的平均性能提升。

</details>


### [74] [Interactive State Space Model with Cross-Modal Local Scanning for Depth Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11934)
*Chen Wu,Ling Wang,Zhuoran Zheng,Xiangyu Chen,Jingyuan Xia,Weidong Jiang,Jiantao Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于交互式状态空间模型（Mamba架构）的引导深度超分辨率框架，通过跨模态局部扫描机制和交叉模态匹配变换模块，实现RGB与深度特征之间的细粒度语义交互，以线性复杂度完成全局建模，在性能上达到SOTA水平。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有GDSR方法要么独立建模各模态，要么依赖计算复杂度为二次的注意力机制，难以建立高效且具有语义交互的联合表示。观察到不同模态的特征图在提取过程中存在语义级相关性，因此需要一种更灵活的方法来实现模态间密集、语义感知的深度交互。

Method: 提出基于交互式状态空间模型（Mamba架构）的GDSR框架：（1）设计跨模态局部扫描机制，实现RGB与深度特征的细粒度语义交互；（2）利用Mamba架构实现线性复杂度的全局建模；（3）引入交叉模态匹配变换模块，利用双模态代表性特征增强交互建模质量。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在引导深度超分辨率任务上取得了与SOTA方法相比具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于交互式状态空间模型的高效GDSR框架，通过跨模态局部扫描和匹配变换，以线性复杂度实现了RGB与深度特征的语义级密集交互，为多模态密集预测任务提供了新思路。

Original Abstract: Guided depth super-resolution (GDSR) reconstructs HR depth maps from LR inputs with HR RGB guidance. Existing methods either model each modality independently or rely on computationally expensive attention mechanisms with quadratic complexity, hindering the establishment of efficient and semantically interactive joint representations. In this paper, we observe that feature maps from different modalities exhibit semantic-level correlations during feature extraction. This motivates us to develop a more flexible approach enabling dense, semantically-aware deep interactions between modalities. To this end, we propose a novel GDSR framework centered around the Interactive State Space Model. Specifically, we design a cross-modal local scanning mechanism that enables fine-grained semantic interactions between RGB and depth features. Leveraging the Mamba architecture, our framework achieves global modeling with linear complexity. Furthermore, a cross-modal matching transform module is introduced to enhance interactive modeling quality by utilizing representative features from both modalities. Extensive experiments demonstrate competitive performance against state-of-the-art methods.

中文摘要翻译: 引导深度超分辨率（GDSR）旨在利用高分辨率RGB图像作为引导，从低分辨率深度输入重建高分辨率深度图。现有方法要么独立建模各模态，要么依赖于计算复杂度为二次的昂贵注意力机制，阻碍了高效且具有语义交互的联合表示的建立。本文观察到，不同模态的特征图在特征提取过程中表现出语义级相关性。这促使我们开发一种更灵活的方法，实现模态间密集、语义感知的深度交互。为此，我们提出了一种以交互式状态空间模型为核心的新型GDSR框架。具体地，我们设计了一种跨模态局部扫描机制，实现了RGB与深度特征之间的细粒度语义交互。借助Mamba架构，我们的框架以线性复杂度完成全局建模。此外，引入了一个交叉模态匹配变换模块，通过利用双模态的代表性特征来增强交互建模质量。大量实验表明，该方法在与现有最优方法的比较中取得了具有竞争力的性能。

</details>


### [75] [Cluster-Aware Neural Collapse Prompt Tuning for Long-Tailed Generalization of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11939)
*Boyang Guo,Liang Li,Lin Peng,Yuhan Gao,Xichun Sheng,Chenggang Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出聚类感知神经崩溃提示微调（CPT），通过构建聚类不变空间和神经崩溃驱动的判别性优化，增强提示微调视觉语言模型在类别不平衡数据集中尾部类的判别能力，同时保持对未见类的泛化性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有提示学习方法在适应类别不平衡数据集时，难以保持尾部类别的判别性，导致模型对少数类识别能力下降。

Method: 1) 设计聚类不变空间：从预训练VLM挖掘语义分配并映射到提示微调特征，计算聚类级边界并将约束限制在局部邻域；2) 引入神经崩溃驱动的判别性优化，包含三种损失：文本等角紧框架分离损失、类间收敛损失和旋转稳定损失，共同塑造聚类内几何结构以改善类间分离和类内对齐。

Result: 在11个不同数据集上的大量实验表明，CPT优于现有最先进方法，在长尾类别上表现更强，且对未见类别具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: CPT有效增强了尾部类判别性且不牺牲整体泛化性能，为不平衡数据下的提示学习提供了新的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Prompt learning has emerged as an efficient alternative to fine-tuning pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs). Despite its promise, current methods still struggle to maintain tail-class discriminability when adapting to class-imbalanced datasets. In this work, we propose cluster-aware neural collapse prompt tuning (CPT), which enhances the discriminability of tail classes in prompt-tuned VLMs without sacrificing their overall generalization. First, we design a cluster-invariant space by mining semantic assignments from the pre-trained VLM and mapping them to prompt-tuned features. This computes cluster-level boundaries and restricts the constraints to local neighborhoods, which reduces interference with the global semantic structure of the pre-trained VLM. Second, we introduce neural-collapse-driven discriminability optimization with three losses: textual Equiangular Tight Frame (ETF) separation loss, class-wise convergence loss, and rotation stabilization loss. These losses work together to shape intra-cluster geometry for better inter-class separation and intra-class alignment. Extensive experiments on 11 diverse datasets demonstrate that CPT outperforms SOTA methods, with stronger performance on long-tail classes and good generalization to unseen classes.

中文摘要翻译: 提示学习已成为微调预训练视觉-语言模型（VLM）的高效替代方案。尽管前景广阔，当前方法在适应类别不平衡数据集时仍难以保持尾部类别的判别性。本文提出聚类感知神经崩溃提示微调（CPT），在不牺牲整体泛化能力的前提下增强提示微调VLM中尾部类别的判别性。首先，通过挖掘预训练VLM的语义分配并将其映射到提示微调特征，设计聚类不变空间。该空间计算聚类级边界并将约束限制在局部邻域，从而减少对预训练VLM全局语义结构的干扰。其次，引入神经崩溃驱动的判别性优化，包含三种损失：文本等角紧框架分离损失、类间收敛损失和旋转稳定损失。这些损失共同塑造聚类内几何结构，以改善类间分离和类内对齐。在11个多样化数据集上的大量实验表明，CPT优于现有最先进方法，在长尾类别上表现更强，且对未见类别具有良好的泛化能力。

</details>


### [76] [Multimodal Abstractive Summarization of Instructional Videos with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11959)
*Maham Nazir,Muhammad Aqeel,Richong Zhang,Francesco Setti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ClipSum利用冻结的CLIP视觉-语言特征，通过显式时序建模和维度自适应融合，在指令性视频摘要任务中超越了传统CNN特征方法，验证了语义对齐比特征容量更重要。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统视频摘要方法使用基于目标分类训练的CNN特征，其视觉概念表示为离散类别，与自然语言在语义上不对齐，导致视觉与语言之间存在语义鸿沟。

Method: 提出ClipSum框架，采用冻结的CLIP视觉-语言特征（在4亿图像-文本对上进行对比预训练），结合显式时序建模和维度自适应融合，用于指令性视频摘要。

Result: 在YouCook2数据集上，ClipSum（使用冻结CLIP特征）ROUGE-1达到33.0%，优于ResNet-152的30.5%，且特征维度降低4倍（512 vs 2048）。冻结CLIP（33.0%）优于微调CLIP（32.3%）。

Conclusion: 视觉-语言语义对齐比特征容量更重要，且保留预训练的对齐能力比针对特定任务进行微调更有价值。

Original Abstract: Multimodal video summarization requires visual features that align semantically with language generation. Traditional approaches rely on CNN features trained for object classification, which represent visual concepts as discrete categories not aligned with natural language. We propose ClipSum, a framework that leverages frozen CLIP vision-language features with explicit temporal modeling and dimension-adaptive fusion for instructional video summarization. CLIP's contrastive pre-training on 400M image-text pairs yields visual features semantically aligned with the linguistic concepts that text decoders generate, bridging the vision-language gap at the representation level. On YouCook2, ClipSum achieves 33.0% ROUGE-1 versus 30.5% for ResNet-152 with 4x lower dimensionality (512 vs. 2048), demonstrating that semantic alignment matters more than feature capacity. Frozen CLIP (33.0%) surpasses fine-tuned CLIP (32.3%), showing that preserving pre-trained alignment is more valuable than task-specific adaptation. https://github.com/aqeeelmirza/clipsum

中文摘要翻译: 多模态视频摘要需要与语言生成在语义上对齐的视觉特征。传统方法依赖基于目标分类训练的CNN特征，其将视觉概念表示为与自然语言不对齐的离散类别。本文提出ClipSum框架，该框架利用冻结的CLIP视觉-语言特征，结合显式时序建模和维度自适应融合，用于指令性视频摘要。CLIP在4亿图像-文本对上的对比预训练产生了与文本解码器生成的语言概念在语义上对齐的视觉特征，在表示层面弥合了视觉-语言鸿沟。在YouCook2数据集上，ClipSum的ROUGE-1达到33.0%，而ResNet-152为30.5%，且特征维度降低4倍（512 vs 2048），这表明语义对齐比特征容量更重要。冻结的CLIP（33.0%）优于微调的CLIP（32.3%），表明保留预训练的对齐能力比针对特定任务的适应更有价值。https://github.com/aqeeelmirza/clipsum

</details>


### [77] [Chronicles-OCR: A Cross-Temporal Perception Benchmark for the Evolutionary Trajectory of Chinese Characters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11960)
*Gengluo Li,Shangpin Peng,Xingyu Wan,Chengquan Zhang,Hao Feng,Xin Xu,Pian Wu,Bang Li,Zengmao Ding,Yongge Liu,Yipei Ye,Yang Yang,Zhan Shu,Guojun Yan,Zhe Li,Can Ma,Weiping Wang,Yu Zhou,Han Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Chronicles-OCR基准，首个系统评估视觉大语言模型(VLLMs)在汉字七体跨数千年形态演化中视觉感知能力的综合数据集，包含2800张平衡图像和四项定量任务，揭示了当前VLLMs在跨时间历史文本感知方面的局限性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有古代文本数据集仅关注孤立历史时期，未能捕捉汉字在数千年间系统的视觉分布偏移，而VLLMs在历史书写体系形态持续演化下的感知鲁棒性尚未得到探索。为弥合这一差距并赋能数字人文，亟需一个全面评估VLLMs跨时间视觉感知能力的基准。

Method: 构建Chronicles-OCR数据集：与顶级领域专家合作，收集2800张严格平衡的图像，涵盖从甲骨到纸本书法的多样物理媒介，覆盖汉字七体完整演化轨迹。提出阶段自适应标注范式(Stage-Adaptive Annotation Paradigm)以适应不同历史阶段剧烈的形态和拓扑变化。设计四项定量任务：跨时期字符定位、基于视觉指代的细粒度古文字识别、古代文本解析和字体分类。

Result: 通过将视觉感知与语义推理分离，Chronicles-OCR提供了一个权威平台来暴露当前VLLMs的局限性，为鲁棒的、具有演化感知的历史文本感知铺平道路。数据集已公开。

Conclusion: Chronicles-OCR作为首个全面评估VLLMs在汉字完整演化轨迹上跨时间视觉感知能力的基准，揭示了现有模型在应对历史书写系统形态演化时的不足，为数字人文领域的发展提供了重要平台和方向。

Original Abstract: Vision Large Language Models (VLLMs) have achieved remarkable success in modern text-rich visual understanding. However, their perceptual robustness in the face of the continuous morphological evolution of historical writing systems remains largely unexplored. Existing ancient text datasets typically focus on isolated historical periods, failing to capture the systematic visual distribution shifts spanning thousands of years. To bridge this gap and empower Digital Humanities, we introduce Chronicles-OCR, the first comprehensive benchmark specifically designed to evaluate the cross-temporal visual perception capabilities of VLLMs across the complete evolutionary trajectory of Chinese characters, known as the Seven Chinese Scripts. Curated in collaboration with top-tier institutional domain experts, the dataset comprises 2,800 strictly balanced images encompassing highly diverse physical media, ranging from tortoise shells to paper-based calligraphy. To accommodate the drastic morphological and topological variations across different historical stages, we propose a novel Stage-Adaptive Annotation Paradigm. Based on this, Chronicles-OCR formulates four rigorous quantitative tasks: cross-period character spotting, fine-grained archaic character recognition via visual referring, ancient text parsing, and script classification. By isolating visual perception from semantic reasoning, Chronicles-OCR provides an authoritative platform to expose the limitations of current VLLMs, paving the way for robust, evolution-aware historical text perception. Chronicles-OCR is publicly available at https://github.com/VirtualLUOUCAS/Chronicles-OCR.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉大语言模型(VLLMs)在现代文本丰富的视觉理解任务中取得了显著成功。然而，它们在面对历史书写体系持续形态演化时的感知鲁棒性仍未得到充分探索。现有的古代文本数据集通常聚焦于孤立的历史时期，未能捕捉跨越数千年的系统性视觉分布偏移。为弥合这一鸿沟并赋能数字人文，我们提出了Chronicles-OCR——首个专门用于评估VLLMs在汉字完整演化轨迹（即汉字七体）上跨时间视觉感知能力的综合性基准。该数据集与顶级机构领域专家合作整理，包含2,800张严格平衡的图像，涵盖了从甲骨到纸本书法的极具多样性的物理媒介。为适应不同历史阶段剧烈的形态和拓扑变化，我们提出了一种新颖的阶段自适应标注范式。基于此，Chronicles-OCR设计了四项严格的定量任务：跨时期字符定位、基于视觉指代的细粒度古文字识别、古代文本解析以及字体分类。通过将视觉感知与语义推理分离，Chronicles-OCR为揭示当前VLLMs的局限性提供了权威平台，为构建鲁棒的、具有演化感知的历史文本感知铺平了道路。Chronicles-OCR已在https://github.com/VirtualLUOUCAS/Chronicles-OCR公开。

</details>


### [78] [What Does It Mean for a Medical AI System to Be Right?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11963)
*Antony Gitau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探讨医学AI系统“正确性”的多维含义，以多发性骨髓瘤诊断中骨髓涂片浆细胞自动分类为临床案例，论证正确性不仅限于基准性能，还涉及数据标注、模型可解释性、评估指标的临床意义及人机协作中的责任分配。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究通常将医学AI的正确性简单等同于基准测试性能，忽视了临床实践中正确性的复杂维度。本文旨在纠正这种还原论观点，从科学哲学和研究伦理学出发，系统解构医学AI正确性的多重内涵。

Method: 采用概念分析和哲学论证的方法，以多发性骨髓瘤诊断中数字化骨髓涂片浆细胞自动分类为具体临床场景，围绕四个相互关联的主题（金标准标签的不稳定性、过度自信AI的不透明性、标准临床指标的不充分性、时间压力下的自动化偏见风险）展开理论分析。

Result: 论证得出医学AI正确性是一个多维概念，不可还原为单一基准性能指标；识别出四个关键维度：金标准标签的构建问题、模型可解释性与可说明性、评估指标的临床相关性，以及人机协作中的责任分布。

Conclusion: 医学AI的正确性应被理解为包含数据质量、模型透明度、临床相关评估和责任分配在内的复合概念，这要求从更广阔的视角审视和评估医学AI系统在真实临床环境中的表现。

Original Abstract: This paper examines what it means for a medical AI system to be right by grounding the question in a specific clinical context: the automatic classification of plasma cells in digitized bone marrow smears for the diagnosis of multiple myeloma. Drawing on philosophy of science and research ethics, the paper argues that correctness in medical AI is not a singular property reducible to benchmark performance, but a multi-dimensional concept involving the availability of expertly labeled medical datasets, the explainability and interpretability of model outputs, the clinical meaningfulness of evaluation metrics, and the distribution of accountability in human-AI workflows. As such, the paper develops this argument through four interrelated themes: the instability of ground truth labels, the opacity of overconfident AI, the inadequacy of standard clinical metrics, and the risk of automation bias in time-pressured clinical settings.

中文摘要翻译: 本文以多发性骨髓瘤诊断中数字化骨髓涂片浆细胞的自动分类为具体临床场景，探讨医学AI系统“正确”意味着什么。借鉴科学哲学和研究伦理学，本文论证医学AI的正确性并非可还原为基准测试性能的单一属性，而是一个多维概念，涉及专家标注的医学数据集的可用性、模型输出的可解释性与可说明性、评估指标的临床意义，以及人机协作工作流中的责任分配。本文通过四个相互关联的主题展开论述：金标准标签的不稳定性、过度自信AI的不透明性、标准临床指标的不足，以及时间压力下临床环境中自动化偏见的风险。

</details>


### [79] [H2G: Hierarchy-Aware Hyperbolic Grouping for 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11967)
*ByungHa Ko,Youngmin Lee,Dong Hwan Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出H2G方法，利用双曲几何和基础模型亲和度，将2D线索转化为3D层次分组监督，实现无需语义标签的多粒度场景分组。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D分组方法依赖语义标签或固定词汇，无法灵活恢复多粒度的层次化场景分组。如何将2D基础模型的丰富知识转化为3D层次分组的有效监督是主要挑战。

Method: 提出H2G（Hyperbolic affinity field for Hierarchical 3D Grouping），利用Dasgupta目标函数从基础模型亲和度中推导语义组织的树状监督，将其蒸馏到单一的洛伦兹双曲特征场中，并通过层次感知目标对齐细粒度分配、粗粒度物体结构、紧凑特征聚类和LCA排序。

Result: 该方法能够在单一特征空间中表示多个分组层级，实现基于2D基础模型知识的语义层次化3D分组。

Conclusion: 双曲几何天然适合树状层次结构，通过将基础模型知识蒸馏到双曲特征场中，可以有效实现无需固定词汇的层次化3D场景分组。

Original Abstract: Hierarchical 3D grouping aims to recover scene groups across multiple granularities, from fine object parts to complete objects, without relying on semantic labels or a fixed vocabulary. The main challenge is to transform 2D foundation-model cues into coherent hierarchy supervision and embed that hierarchy in a 3D representation. We propose H2G, a hyperbolic affinity field for hierarchical 3D grouping. Our method derives semantically organized tree supervision by interpreting foundation-model affinities through Dasgupta's objective for similarity-based hierarchical clustering. This supervision is distilled into a single Lorentz hyperbolic feature field, whose geometry is well suited for tree-like branching structures. A hierarchy-aware objective aligns the field with fine-level assignments, coarse object structure, compact feature clusters, and LCA (Lowest Common Ancestor) ordering. This formulation represents multiple grouping levels in one feature space, enabling semantic hierarchical grouping grounded in 2D foundation-model knowledge.

中文摘要翻译: 层次化3D分组旨在跨多个粒度恢复场景分组——从精细的物体部件到完整的物体，而不依赖于语义标签或固定词汇。主要挑战在于将2D基础模型的线索转化为连贯的层次监督，并将该层次嵌入到3D表示中。我们提出H2G，一种用于层次化3D分组双曲亲和场。我们的方法通过Dasgupta的相似性层次聚类目标函数来解释基础模型亲和度，从而推导出语义组织的树状监督。该监督被蒸馏到一个单一的洛伦兹双曲特征场中，其几何结构天然适合树状分支结构。一种层次感知的目标函数将该特征场与细粒度分配、粗粒度物体结构、紧凑特征聚类以及LCA（最低公共祖先）排序对齐。该公式在单一特征空间中表示多个分组层级，实现了基于2D基础模型知识的语义层次化分组。

</details>


### [80] [Optimizing 4D Wires for Sparse 3D Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11977)
*Dong-Yi Wu,Tong-Yee Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种使用单一连续4D B样条曲线（x,y,z,w）进行3D几何抽象的统一框架，将3D草图绘制从局部密度累积转化为全局路由问题，实现了更优的拓扑连贯性和美学效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法通常将形状表示为多条独立曲线片段的集合，导致结构碎片化且物理可实现性有限，缺乏全局拓扑连贯性。

Method: 采用单一连续4D B样条（空间坐标+可变宽度）表示3D形状，通过连续性约束将3D草图绘制转化为全局路由问题；引入可微分渲染管线高效光栅化变宽度曲线，支持基于SDS或CLIP等信号的梯度优化。

Result: 实验表明，相比基于离散曲线集合的方法，该统一表示在图像到3D抽象、多视图线艺术生成和可微分风格化表面填充等任务中获得了更高的语义保真度和结构连贯性。

Conclusion: 单一连续样条曲线足以表达复杂体积形式并保证全局拓扑连贯性，为3D几何抽象提供了一种更简洁、物理可实现性更强的统一框架。

Original Abstract: We present a unified framework for 3D geometric abstraction using a single continuous 4D wire, parameterized as a B-spline with spatial coordinates and variable width $(x,y,z,w)$. Existing approaches typically represent shapes as collections of many independent curve segments, which often leads to fragmented structures and limited physical realizability. In contrast, we show that a single continuous spline is sufficiently expressive to capture complex volumetric forms while enforcing global topological coherence. By imposing continuity, our method transforms 3D sketching from a local density-accumulation process into a global routing problem, providing a strong inductive bias toward cleaner aesthetics and improved structural coherence. To enable gradient-based optimization, we introduce a differentiable rendering pipeline that efficiently rasterizes variable-width curves with bounded projection error. This formulation supports robust optimization using modern guidance signals such as Score Distillation Sampling (SDS) or CLIP. We demonstrate applications including image-to-3D abstraction, multi-view wire art generation, and differentiable stylized surface filling. Experiments show that our unified representation produces structures with higher semantic fidelity and improved structural coherence compared to approaches based on collections of discrete curves.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了一种使用单一连续4D线的3D几何抽象统一框架，该线参数化为带有空间坐标和可变宽度(x,y,z,w)的B样条。现有方法通常将形状表示为多条独立曲线片段的集合，这常常导致结构碎片化和有限的物理可实现性。相比之下，我们证明单一连续样条足以表达复杂的体积形式，同时强制全局拓扑连贯性。通过施加连续性约束，我们的方法将3D草图绘制从局部密度累积过程转化为全局路由问题，为更清晰的美学和改善的结构连贯性提供了强归纳偏置。为实现基于梯度的优化，我们引入了一种可微分渲染管线，能以有界投影误差高效光栅化变宽度曲线。该公式支持使用现代引导信号（如分数蒸馏采样（SDS）或CLIP）进行鲁棒优化。我们展示了包括图像到3D抽象、多视图线艺术生成和可微分风格化表面填充在内的应用。实验表明，与基于离散曲线集合的方法相比，我们的统一表示能生成具有更高语义保真度和改善结构连贯性的结构。

</details>


### [81] [EDGER: EDge-Guided with HEatmap Refinement for Generalizable Image Forgery Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12002)
*Minh-Khoa Le-Phan,Minh-Hoang Le,Minh-Triet Tran,Trong-Le Do*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EDGER是一个基于补丁的双分支框架，结合边缘引导分割（利用频域边缘检测器增强篡改边界的高频不一致性）和合成热图分支（基于CLIP-ViT对完全合成补丁进行分类），实现任意分辨率图像的篡改区域定位，在MediaEval 2025 SynthIM挑战中表现出强跨域泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法难以跨域指征图像中的可疑信号，且文本引导的修补技术使得图像伪造越来越逼真，对图像篡改检测（SID）和图像伪造定位（IFL）构成挑战。

Method: 提出EDGER双分支框架：(1) 边缘引导分割分支——引入基于频域的边缘检测器增强篡改边界处的高频不一致性，微调SegFormer融合RGB和边缘特征以生成像素级掩码；(2) 合成热图分支——基于分类的定位器，微调CLIP-ViT图像编码器（使用LoRA）标记完全合成补丁，提供粗粒度的补丁级合成先验。两分支互补实现全面定位。

Result: 在MediaEval 2025 SynthIM挑战的篡改区域定位任务上，该方法可扩展至多百万像素图像，表现出强跨域泛化能力。大量消融实验突出了频域边缘线索和补丁级合成先验在驱动准确、分辨率无关定位中的互补作用。

Conclusion: 频域边缘线索与补丁级合成先验在篡改定位任务中具有互补性，EDGER通过双分支设计有效结合二者，实现了对任意分辨率图像的准确且具有强泛化能力的篡改区域定位。

Original Abstract: Text-guided inpainting has made image forgery increasingly realistic, challenging both SID and IFL. However, existing methods often struggle to point out suspicious signals across domains. To address this problem, we propose EDGER, a patch-based, dual-branch framework that localizes manipulated regions in arbitrary resolution images without sacrificing native resolution. The first branch, Edge-Guided Segmentation, introduces a Frequency-based Edge Detector to emphasize high-frequency inconsistencies at manipulation boundaries, and fine-tunes a SegFormer to fuse RGB and edge features for pixel-level masks. Since edge evidence is most informative only when patches contain both authentic and manipulated pixels, we complement Edge-Guided Segmentation with a Synthetic Heatmapping branch, a classification-based localizer that fine-tunes a CLIP-ViT image encoder with LoRA to flag fully synthetic patches. Together, Synthetic Heatmapping provides coarse, patch-level synthetic priors, while Edge-Guided Segmentation sharpens boundaries within partially manipulated patches, yielding comprehensive localization. Evaluated in the MediaEval 2025, SynthIM challenge, Manipulated Region Localization Task's setting, our approach scales to multi-megapixel imagery and exhibits strong cross-domain generalization. Extensive ablations highlight the complementary roles of frequency-based edge cues and patch-level synthetic priors in driving accurate, resolution-agnostic localization.

中文摘要翻译: 文本引导的图像修补使得图像伪造越来越逼真，对图像篡改检测（SID）和图像伪造定位（IFL）均构成挑战。然而，现有方法通常难以跨域指征图像中的可疑信号。为解决这一问题，我们提出EDGER，一种基于补丁的双分支框架，能够在任意分辨率图像中定位篡改区域，且不牺牲原生分辨率。第一个分支——边缘引导分割——引入基于频域的边缘检测器，增强篡改边界处的高频不一致性，并微调SegFormer以融合RGB和边缘特征，生成像素级掩码。由于边缘证据仅在补丁同时包含真实和篡改像素时最具信息量，我们以合成热图分支作为边缘引导分割的补充，该分支是一个基于分类的定位器，通过微调带有LoRA的CLIP-ViT图像编码器来标记完全合成的补丁。综合而言，合成热图提供粗粒度的补丁级合成先验，而边缘引导分割在部分篡改的补丁内锐化边界，从而实现全面定位。在MediaEval 2025 SynthIM挑战的篡改区域定位任务设定下评估，我们的方法可扩展至多百万像素图像，并展现出强跨域泛化能力。大量消融实验凸显了频域边缘线索与补丁级合成先验在驱动准确、分辨率无关的定位过程中的互补作用。

</details>


### [82] [Robust Promptable Video Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12006)
*Sohyun Lee,Yeho Gwon,Lukas Hoyer,Konrad Schindler,Christos Sakaridis,Suha Kwak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究鲁棒提示性视频目标分割（RobustPVOS），构建了包含真实与合成退化数据的基准，并提出基于记忆目标条件门控秩适应（MoGA）方法，有效应对目标特定退化并保持时序一致性，显著提升模型在多种腐败下的鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有PVOS模型在输入腐败（如噪声、模糊等）下性能严重下降，阻碍了其在安全关键领域的部署，但目前缺乏对此鲁棒性的系统研究。

Method: 提出MoGA方法，核心是利用跨帧记忆中的目标特定表征来条件化鲁棒化过程，使得模型能以时序一致的方式差异化处理每个被跟踪目标；同时构建包含真实世界退化数据集和时序变化合成腐败训练数据的综合基准。

Result: 在合成的多样化腐败类型和真实世界退化数据集上的大量实验表明，MoGA相比基线方法在不同腐败类型上均取得了一致且显著的性能提升，为未来鲁棒PVOS研究建立了强 baseline。

Conclusion: 本文首次对鲁棒PVOS进行系统性研究，所提基准和MoGA方法为该方向提供了重要基础，验证了处理目标特定退化和保持时序一致性是提升PVOS鲁棒性的关键。

Original Abstract: The performance of promptable video object segmentation (PVOS) models substantially degrades under input corruptions, which prevents PVOS deployment in safety-critical domains. This paper offers the first comprehensive study on robust PVOS (RobustPVOS). We first construct a new, comprehensive benchmark with two real-world evaluation datasets of 351 video clips and more than 2,500 object masks under real-world adverse conditions. At the same time, we generate synthetic training data by applying diverse and temporally varying corruptions to existing VOS datasets. Moreover, we present a new RobustPVOS method, dubbed Memory-object-conditioned Gated-rank Adaptation (MoGA). The key to successfully performing RobustPVOS is two-fold: effectively handling object-specific degradation and ensuring temporal consistency in predictions. MoGA leverages object-specific representations maintained in memory across frames to condition the robustification process, which allows the model to handle each tracked object differently in a temporally consistent way. Extensive experiments on our benchmark validate MoGA's efficacy, showing consistent and significant improvements across diverse corruption types on both synthetic and real-world datasets, establishing a strong baseline for future RobustPVOS research. Our benchmark is publicly available at https://sohyun-l.github.io/RobustPVOS_project_page/.

中文摘要翻译: 提示性视频目标分割（PVOS）模型在输入腐败条件下的性能会显著下降，这阻碍了其在安全关键领域的部署。本文首次对鲁棒提示性视频目标分割（RobustPVOS）进行了全面的研究。我们首先构建了一个全新的综合性基准，包含两个真实世界评估数据集，涵盖351个视频片段和超过2,500个目标掩码，这些数据均采集自真实世界的恶劣条件。同时，我们通过对现有VOS数据集应用多样化且随时间变化的腐败来生成合成训练数据。此外，我们提出了一种新的鲁棒PVOS方法，称为基于记忆目标条件门控秩适应（MoGA）。成功实现鲁棒PVOS的关键有两点：有效处理目标特定的退化并保持预测中的时序一致性。MoGA利用跨帧记忆中维护的目标特定表征来条件化鲁棒化过程，这使得模型能够以时序一致的方式差异化处理每个被跟踪目标。在我们的基准上进行的广泛实验验证了MoGA的有效性，结果显示在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上的多种腐败类型中均取得了一致且显著的改进，为未来的鲁棒PVOS研究建立了强基线。我们的基准已公开于 https://sohyun-l.github.io/RobustPVOS_project_page/。

</details>


### [83] [L2P: Unlocking Latent Potential for Pixel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12013)
*Zhennan Chen,Junwei Zhu,Xu Chen,Jiangning Zhang,Jiawei Chen,Zhuoqi Zeng,Wei Zhang,Chengjie Wang,Jian Yang,Ying Tai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Latent-to-Pixel (L2P)迁移范式，利用预训练潜在扩散模型(LDM)构建像素级扩散模型，仅用8块GPU即可实现高效训练，并支持原生4K分辨率生成。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 从头训练先进的像素空间模型需要极高的计算和数据资源，而现有的潜在扩散模型虽已具备丰富知识，却受限于VAE瓶颈。因此希望借助预训练LDM的知识，高效构建像素空间模型。

Method: L2P舍弃VAE，采用大块分词化；冻结源LDM的中间层，仅训练浅层来学习潜在到像素的变换；使用LDM生成的合成图像作为唯一训练数据，拟合平滑的数据流形，实现快速收敛且无需真实数据。

Result: 仅需8块GPU，训练开销可忽略不计；在DPG-Bench上与源LDM性能持平，在GenEval上达到其93%的性能；支持原生4K超高清分辨率生成。

Conclusion: L2P能够无缝地将大量潜在先验迁移到像素空间，以极低的训练成本实现与源LDM相当的性能，并突破了VAE的内存瓶颈，实现超高分辨率生成。

Original Abstract: Pixel diffusion models have recently regained attention for visual generation. However, training advanced pixel-space models from scratch demands prohibitive computational and data resources. To address this, we propose the Latent-to-Pixel (L2P) transfer paradigm, an efficient framework that directly harnesses the rich knowledge of pre-trained LDMs to build powerful pixel-space models. Specifically, L2P discards the VAE in favor of large-patch tokenization and freezes the source LDM's intermediate layers, exclusively training shallow layers to learn the latent-to-pixel transformation. By utilizing LDM-generated synthetic images as the sole training corpus, L2P fits an already smooth data manifold, enabling rapid convergence with zero real-data collection. This strategy allows L2P to seamlessly migrate massive latent priors to the pixel space using only 8 GPUs. Furthermore, eliminating the VAE memory bottleneck unlocks native 4K ultra-high resolution generation. Extensive experiments across mainstream LDM architectures show that L2P incurs negligible training overhead, yet performs on par with the source LDM on DPG-Bench and reaches 93% performance on GenEval.

中文摘要翻译: 像素扩散模型近期在视觉生成领域重新受到关注。然而，从头训练先进的像素空间模型需要极高的计算和数据资源。为解决这一问题，我们提出潜在到像素（L2P）迁移范式，这是一种高效的框架，直接利用预训练潜在扩散模型（LDM）的丰富知识来构建强大的像素空间模型。具体而言，L2P舍弃了变分自编码器（VAE），采用大块分词化，并冻结源LDM的中间层，仅训练浅层来学习潜在到像素的变换。通过使用LDM生成的合成图像作为唯一训练语料，L2P拟合了已然平滑的数据流形，从而实现快速收敛且无需任何真实数据采集。这一策略使得L2P仅用8块GPU即可将海量潜在先验无缝迁移到像素空间。此外，消除VAE内存瓶颈后，L2P支持原生4K超高清分辨率生成。在主流LDM架构上的大量实验表明，L2P的训练开销几乎可以忽略不计，同时在DPG-Bench上与源LDM性能持平，在GenEval上达到其93%的性能。

</details>


### [84] [4DVGGT-D: 4D Visual Geometry Transformer with Improved Dynamic Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12027)
*Ying Zang,Xuanyi Liu,Yidong Han,Deyi Ji,Chaotao Ding,Yuanqi Hu,Qi Zhu,Xuanfu Li,Jin Ma,Lingyun Sun,Tianrun Chen,Lanyun Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练、渐进解耦的框架，通过动态掩码引导的位姿解耦、拓扑子空间手术和信息论置信度感知融合，从单目视频中稳健重建动态4D场景，在标准基准上取得显著提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D基础模型在动态环境下性能严重下降，根源在于全局注意力机制中相机自运动与物体运动的固有耦合，亟需一种原理性的动态-静态解耦方法。

Method: 提出三组分协同的渐进解耦框架：(1) 动态掩码引导的位姿解耦模块，隔离动态干扰以获得稳定参考帧；(2) 拓扑子空间手术机制，正交分解深度流形，保留动态物体并注入掩码感知几何；(3) 信息论置信度感知融合策略，将深度集成建模为异方差贝叶斯推理问题，通过逆方差加权自适应融合多通道预测。

Result: 在标准4D重建基准上，点云主要指标均取得一致且显著的改进，展示了无需微调的鲁棒4D场景重建性能。

Conclusion: 通过数学驱动的动态-静态解耦方法，无需微调即可实现鲁棒的4D场景重建，展现了原理性解耦的潜力。

Original Abstract: Reconstructing dynamic 4D scenes from monocular videos is a fundamental yet challenging task. While recent 3D foundation models provide strong geometric priors, their performance significantly degrades in dynamic environments. This degradation stems from a fundamental tension: the inherent coupling of camera ego-motion and object motion within global attention mechanisms. In this paper, we propose a novel, training-free progressive decoupling framework that disentangles dynamics from statics in a principled, coarse-to-fine manner. Our core insight is to resolve the tension by first stabilizing the camera pose, followed by geometric refinement. Specifically, our approach consists of three synergistic components: (1) a Dynamic-Mask-Guided Pose Decoupling module that isolates pose estimation from dynamic interference, yielding a stable motion-free reference frame; (2) a Topological Subspace Surgery mechanism that orthogonally decomposes the depth manifold, safely preserving dynamic objects while injecting refined, mask-aware geometry into static regions; and (3) an Information-Theoretic Confidence-Aware Fusion strategy that formulates depth integration as a heteroscedastic Bayesian inference problem, adaptively blending multi-pass predictions via inverse-variance weighting. Extensive experiments on standard 4D reconstruction benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves consistent and substantial improvements across principal point-cloud metrics. Notably, our approach shows competitive performance in robust 4D scene reconstruction without requiring fine-tuning, suggesting the potential of mathematically grounded dynamic-static disentanglement.

中文摘要翻译: 从单目视频重建动态4D场景是一项基础但具有挑战性的任务。尽管最近的3D基础模型提供了强大的几何先验，但其性能在动态环境中显著下降。这种退化源于一个基本矛盾：全局注意力机制中相机自运动与物体运动的固有耦合。本文提出了一种新颖的、无需训练的渐进解耦框架，以由粗到细的方式原理性地将动态与静态分离。我们的核心洞见是首先稳定相机位姿，然后进行几何精化，以此解决上述矛盾。具体而言，我们的方法包含三个协同组件：(1) 动态掩码引导的位姿解耦模块，将位姿估计与动态干扰隔离，生成稳定的无运动参考帧；(2) 拓扑子空间手术机制，对深度流形进行正交分解，在安全保留动态物体的同时将掩码感知的精化几何注入静态区域；(3) 信息论置信度感知融合策略，将深度集成建模为异方差贝叶斯推理问题，通过逆方差加权自适应融合多通道预测。在标准4D重建基准上的大量实验表明，我们的方法在点云主要指标上取得了一致且显著的改进。值得注意的是，我们的方法无需微调即展现出竞争性的鲁棒4D场景重建性能，表明基于数学原理的动态-静态解耦具有巨大潜力。

</details>


### [85] [OmniHumanoid: Streaming Cross-Embodiment Video Generation with Paired-Free Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12038)
*Yiren Song,Xiyao Deng,Pei Yang,Yihan Wang,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniHumanoid提出了一种跨实体视频生成框架，通过解耦可迁移运动学习与实体特定适应，利用运动对齐的配对视频训练共享运动迁移模型，并借助轻量级适配器和分支隔离注意力机制，仅需未配对视频即可适应新的人形实体，在合成和真实基准上实现了高运动 fidelity 和实体一致性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有跨实体视频生成方法常将运动动力学与外观形态纠缠在一起，且多数方法需要每个目标实体都有配对数据，这限制了向新机器人的可扩展性。因此需要一种能够解耦可迁移运动学习与实体特定适应的方法，仅利用未配对视频即可适应新实体。

Method: 1）从运动对齐的跨实体配对视频中学习共享运动迁移模型；2）通过轻量级实体特定适配器，仅使用未配对视频适应新实体；3）引入分支隔离注意力（branch-isolated attention）设计，将运动条件与实体特定调制分离，减少两者之间的干扰；4）构建了包含多样人形资产、场景和视角的合成跨实体数据集。

Result: 在合成和真实基准上的实验表明，OmniHumanoid实现了强运动保真度和实体一致性，且能够无需重新训练共享运动模型即可可扩展地适应未见过的实体。

Conclusion: OmniHumanoid通过解耦运动迁移和实体适应，实现了高效且可扩展的跨实体视频生成，为具身智能领域的大规模数据生成提供了有效方案。

Original Abstract: Cross-embodiment video generation aims to transfer motions across different humanoid embodiments, such as human-to-robot and robot-to-robot, enabling scalable data generation for embodied intelligence. A major challenge in this setting is that motion dynamics are partly transferable across embodiments, whereas appearance and morphology remain embodiment-specific. Existing approaches often entangle these factors, and many require paired data for every target embodiment, which limits scalability to new robots. We present OmniHumanoid, a framework that factorizes transferable motion learning and embodiment-specific adaptation. Our method learns a shared motion transfer model from motion-aligned paired videos spanning multiple embodiments, while adapting to a new embodiment using only unpaired videos through lightweight embodiment-specific adapters. To reduce interference between motion transfer and embodiment adaptation, we further introduce a branch-isolated attention design that separates motion conditioning from embodiment-specific modulation. In addition, we construct a synthetic cross-embodiment dataset with motion-aligned paired videos rendered across diverse humanoid assets, scenes, and viewpoints. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world benchmarks show that OmniHumanoid achieves strong motion fidelity and embodiment consistency, while enabling scalable adaptation to unseen humanoid embodiments without retraining the shared motion model.

中文摘要翻译: 跨实体视频生成旨在将运动在不同人形实体之间进行迁移，例如从人到机器人以及机器人到机器人，从而为具身智能实现可扩展的数据生成。该设置中的一个主要挑战是：运动动力学在不同实体间部分可迁移，而外观和形态则是实体特定的。现有方法常常纠缠这些因素，且许多方法需要每个目标实体都有配对数据，这限制了向新机器人的可扩展性。我们提出了OmniHumanoid，一个将可迁移运动学习与实体特定适应进行解耦的框架。我们的方法从跨多个实体的运动对齐配对视频中学习共享运动迁移模型，同时通过轻量级实体特定适配器仅使用未配对视频适应新实体。为了减少运动迁移与实体适应之间的干扰，我们进一步引入了分支隔离注意力设计，将运动条件与实体特定调制分离开来。此外，我们构建了一个合成跨实体数据集，其中包含在多样的人形资产、场景和视角下渲染的运动对齐配对视频。在合成和真实基准上的实验表明，OmniHumanoid实现了强运动保真度和实体一致性，同时能够无需重新训练共享运动模型即可可扩展地适应未见过的实体。

</details>


### [86] [TAR: Text Semantic Assisted Cross-modal Image Registration Framework for Optical and SAR Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12064)
*Zhuoyu Cai,Dou Quan,Ning Huyan,Pei He,Shuang Wang,Licheng Jiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TAR框架，利用文本语义先验（遥感场景和土地覆盖类别）辅助光学与SAR图像的跨模态配准，通过多尺度视觉特征学习、文本辅助特征增强和由粗到精的密集匹配模块，在大几何形变下实现优于现有方法的配准性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的跨模态配准方法在较大几何形变下效果不佳，因为模型需要同时处理跨模态外观差异和复杂的空间变换，而单纯依赖视觉特征难以有效弥合模态鸿沟。

Method: 提出TAR框架，包含三个模块：(1) 多尺度视觉特征学习(MSFL)模块，提取光学和SAR图像的多尺度视觉特征；(2) 文本辅助特征增强(TAFE)模块，构建遥感场景和地物类别的文本描述，利用冻结的RemoteCLIP文本编码器提取文本特征，通过视觉-文本交互增强高层视觉特征；(3) 由粗到精的密集匹配(CFDM)模块，先基于增强后的高层特征建立粗对应关系，再使用低层特征细化匹配位置。

Result: 在跨模态遥感图像上，TAR在配准性能上优于多种现有方法，尤其在大几何形变场景下取得了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 文本语义先验能有效缓解模态差异并增强跨模态特征学习，从而提升光学与SAR图像在大几何形变下的配准精度。

Original Abstract: Existing deep learning-based methods can capture shared features from optical and synthetic aperture radar (SAR) images for spatial alignment. However, optical-SAR registration remains challenging under large geometric deformations, because the model needs to simultaneously handle cross-modal appearance discrepancies and complex spatial transformations. To address this issue, this paper proposes a text semantic-assisted cross-modal image registration framework, named TAR, for optical and SAR images. TAR exploits text semantic priors from remote sensing scenes and land-cover categories to alleviate the modality gap and enhance cross-modal feature learning. TAR consists of three components: a multi-scale visual feature learning (MSFL) module, a text-assisted feature enhancement (TAFE) module, and a coarse-to-fine dense matching (CFDM) module. MSFL extracts multi-scale visual features from optical and SAR images. TAFE constructs text descriptors related to remote sensing scenes and land-cover objects, and uses a frozen RemoteCLIP text encoder to extract text features. These text features are introduced through visual-text interaction to enhance high-level visual features for more reliable coarse matching. CFDM then establishes coarse correspondences based on the enhanced high-level features and refines the matched locations using low-level features. Experimental results on cross-modal remote sensing images demonstrate the effectiveness of TAR, which achieves stronger matching performance than several state-of-the-art methods and yields significant gains under large geometric deformations.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的基于深度学习方法能够从光学图像和合成孔径雷达(SAR)图像中捕获共享特征以实现空间对齐。然而，在大几何形变下，光学-SAR图像配准仍然具有挑战性，因为模型需要同时处理跨模态外观差异和复杂的空间变换。针对这一问题，本文提出了一种名为TAR的文本语义辅助跨模态图像配准框架，用于光学和SAR图像。TAR利用来自遥感场景和土地覆盖类别的文本语义先验来缓解模态差异并增强跨模态特征学习。TAR包含三个组件：多尺度视觉特征学习(MSFL)模块、文本辅助特征增强(TAFE)模块和由粗到精的密集匹配(CFDM)模块。MSFL从光学和SAR图像中提取多尺度视觉特征。TAFE构建与遥感场景和地物对象相关的文本描述符，并使用冻结的RemoteCLIP文本编码器提取文本特征。这些文本特征通过视觉-文本交互引入，以增强高层视觉特征，从而实现更可靠的粗匹配。CFDM随后基于增强后的高层特征建立粗对应关系，并使用低层特征细化匹配位置。在跨模态遥感图像上的实验结果证明了TAR的有效性，其配准性能优于多种现有方法，并在大几何形变下取得了显著的性能提升。

</details>


### [87] [Anomaly-Aware Vision-Language Adapters for Zero-Shot Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12069)
*Muhammad Aqeel,Maham Nazir,Uzair Khan,Marco Cristani,Francesco Setti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AVA-DINO提出了一种异常感知的视觉-语言适应框架，通过双专用分支（分别处理正常和异常模式）和文本引导的路由机制，在不进行目标特定训练的情况下实现零样本异常检测，在MVTec-AD上达到93.5%的图像级AUROC，并展现出强跨域泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有零样本异常检测方法对所有样本采用相同的特征变换，忽略了正常数据（紧凑分布）与异常数据（多样分布）之间固有的非对称分布特性。本文旨在利用这种自然不对称性，通过设计专门的、非对称的特征变换来更好地建模正常与异常模式。

Method: 提出AVA-DINO框架，包含两个专用分支（正常分支和异常分支），对冻结的DINOv3视觉特征进行适应。训练时，通过文本引导的路由机制和显式路由正则化联合学习两个分支，鼓励分支专业化。测试时，仅利用输入图像和固定的语言描述动态组合两个分支，实现非对称激活，避免退化的均匀路由。

Result: 在9个工业与医学基准上达到最先进性能，MVTec-AD上图像级AUROC为93.5%，无需领域特定微调即可强泛化到医学影像。

Conclusion: 利用正常与异常数据的固有非对称性设计双专用分支，结合文本引导路由机制，能够有效提升零样本异常检测性能，并实现跨领域泛化。

Original Abstract: Zero-shot anomaly detection aims to identify defects in unseen categories without target-specific training. Existing methods usually apply the same feature transformation to all samples, treating normal and anomalous data uniformly despite their fundamentally asymmetric distributions, compact normals versus diverse anomalies. We instead exploit this natural asymmetry by proposing AVA-DINO, an anomaly-aware vision-language adaptation framework with dual specialized branches for normal and anomalous patterns that adapt frozen DINOv3 visual features. During training on auxiliary data, the two branches are learned jointly with a text-guided routing mechanism and explicit routing regularization that encourages branch specialization. At test time, only the input image and fixed, predefined language descriptions are used to dynamically combine the two branches, enabling an asymmetric activation. This design prevents degenerate uniform routing and allows context-specific feature transformations. Experiments across nine industrial and medical benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, achieving 93.5% image-AUROC on MVTec-AD and strong cross-domain generalization to medical imaging without domain-specific fine-tuning. https://github.com/aqeeelmirza/AVA-DINO

中文摘要翻译: 零样本异常检测旨在无需目标特定训练的情况下识别未见类别的缺陷。现有方法通常对所有样本应用相同的特征变换，将正常数据和异常数据统一处理，尽管它们具有根本性的非对称分布——紧凑的正常数据与多样的异常数据。我们转而利用这种自然不对称性，提出AVA-DINO，一种异常感知的视觉-语言适应框架，具有针对正常和异常模式的双专用分支，用于适应冻结的DINOv3视觉特征。在辅助数据训练期间，两个分支通过文本引导的路由机制和显式路由正则化联合学习，鼓励分支专业化。在测试时，仅使用输入图像和固定的、预定义的语言描述来动态组合两个分支，实现非对称激活。这种设计防止了退化的均匀路由，并允许上下文特定的特征变换。在9个工业和医学基准上的实验证明了最先进的性能，在MVTec-AD上达到93.5%的图像级AUROC，并且无需领域特定微调即可实现向医学影像的强跨域泛化。

</details>


### [88] [PairDropGS: Paired Dropout-Induced Consistency Regularization for Sparse-View Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12072)
*Hantang Li,Qiang Zhu,Xiandong Meng,Xingtao Wang,Debin Zhao,Xiaopeng Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PairDropGS提出了一种基于成对丢弃的一致性正则化框架，通过低频一致性约束和渐进式调度策略，解决了稀疏视图3DGS中不同丢弃子集间的不一致性问题，提升了重建质量和训练稳定性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于丢弃的稀疏视图3DGS方法虽设计了复杂的丢弃策略，但忽视了不同丢弃高斯子集之间存在的不一致性，导致重建不稳定且表示学习次优。

Method: 1) 从共享高斯场中构建一对丢弃高斯子集；2) 设计低频一致性正则化，约束低频频谱的渲染结构，保留稳定场景布局和粗略几何，避免对模糊高频细节的过度约束；3) 引入渐进式一致性调度策略，逐步增强一致性正则化以提升训练稳定性与鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个稀疏视图基准测试上，PairDropGS在训练稳定性和重建质量上显著优于现有基于丢弃的3DGS方法，且具有即插即用的简便性。

Conclusion: PairDropGS从一致性正则化角度出发，有效解决了丢弃机制带来的不一致性问题，为提升稀疏视图3DGS提供了一种简单、稳定且高效的新范式。

Original Abstract: Dropout-based sparse-view 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) methods alleviate overfitting by randomly suppressing Gaussian primitives during training. Existing methods mainly focus on designing increasingly sophisticated dropout strategies, while they overlook the resulting inconsistencies among different dropped Gaussian subsets. This oversight often leads to unstable reconstruction and suboptimal Gaussian representation learning.In this paper, we revisit dropout-based sparse-view 3DGS from a consistency regularization perspective and propose PairDropGS, a Paired Dropout-induced Consistency Regularization framework for sparse-view Gaussian splatting. Specifically, PairDropGS first constructs a pair of the dropped Gaussian subsets from a shared Gaussian field and designs a low-frequency consistency regularization to constrain their low-frequency rendered structures. This design encourages the shared Gaussian field to preserve stable scene layout and coarse geometry under different random dropouts, while avoiding excessive constraints on ambiguous high-frequency details. Moreover, we introduce a progressive consistency scheduling strategy to gradually strengthen the consistency regularization during training for stability and robustness of reconstruction. Extensive experiments on widely-used sparse-view benchmarks demonstrate that PairDropGS achieves superior training stability, significantly outperforms existing dropout-based 3DGS methods in reconstruction quality, while exhibiting the simplicity and plug-and-play nature for improving dropout-based optimization.

中文摘要翻译: 基于丢弃的稀疏视图三维高斯泼溅（3DGS）方法通过在训练中随机抑制高斯原语来缓解过拟合。现有方法主要集中于设计日益复杂的丢弃策略，却忽视了不同丢弃高斯子集之间存在的不一致性。这种疏忽往往导致重建不稳定以及高斯表示学习次优。本文从一致性正则化的视角重新审视基于丢弃的稀疏视图3DGS，并提出PairDropGS——一种用于稀疏视图高斯泼溅的成对丢弃诱导一致性正则化框架。具体而言，PairDropGS首先从共享高斯场中构建一对丢弃高斯子集，并设计低频一致性正则化来约束其低频渲染结构。该设计鼓励共享高斯场在不同随机丢弃下保持稳定的场景布局和粗略几何，同时避免对模糊的高频细节施加过度约束。此外，我们引入渐进式一致性调度策略，在训练过程中逐步增强一致性正则化，以提升重建的稳定性和鲁棒性。在广泛使用的稀疏视图基准上的大量实验表明，PairDropGS实现了优越的训练稳定性，在重建质量上显著优于现有基于丢弃的3DGS方法，同时展现出为改进基于丢弃的优化所带来的简洁性与即插即用特性。

</details>


### [89] [BARISTA: A Multi-Task Egocentric Benchmark for Compositional Visual Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12074)
*Patrick Knab,Orgest Xhelili,Inis Buzi,Drago Andres Guggiana Nilo,Mohd Saquib Khan,Lorenz Kolb,Manuel Scherzer,Kerem Yildirir,Christian Bartelt,Philipp Johannes Schubert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BARISTA是一个密集标注的自我中心视频数据集和基准，包含185个真实咖啡准备视频，提供逐帧场景图以支持零样本语言任务，揭示了现有模型在程序性视频理解中的显著性能差异。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基准通常单独评估场景理解的不同能力（如目标定位、手物交互、关系解析、时序推理等），导致难以诊断模型在程序性任务上的失败原因。

Method: 构建了BARISTA数据集，包含185个自我中心咖啡准备视频，提供逐帧验证的场景图，连接持久化目标身份与掩码、轨迹、边界框、属性、类型化关系、手物交互、活动和过程步骤，并从中派生出多种零样本语言任务。

Result: 实验表明不同任务系列间存在显著性能差异，且没有一致占优的模型系列，BARISTA可作为程序性视频理解的高难度诊断基准。

Conclusion: BARISTA是一个具有挑战性的程序性视频理解诊断基准，能够有效揭示现有模型在不同推理维度上的能力差异。

Original Abstract: Scene understanding is central to general physical intelligence, and video is a primary modality for capturing both state and temporal dynamics of a scene. Yet understanding physical processes remains difficult, as models must combine object localization, hand-object interactions, relational parsing, temporal reasoning, and step-level procedural inference. Existing benchmarks usually evaluate these capabilities separately, limiting diagnosis of why models fail on procedural tasks. We introduce BARISTA, a densely annotated egocentric dataset and benchmark of 185 real-world coffee-preparation videos covering fully automatic, portafilter-based, and capsule-based workflows. BARISTA provides verified per-frame scene graphs linking persistent object identities to masks, tracks, boxes, attributes, typed relations, hand-object interactions, activities, and process steps.
