From these graphs, we derive zero-shot language-based tasks spanning phrase grounding, hand-object interaction recognition, referring, activity recognition, relation extraction, and temporal visual question answering. Experiments reveal strong variation across task families and no consistently dominant model family, positioning BARISTA as a challenging diagnostic benchmark for procedural video understanding. Code and dataset available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/ramblr/BARISTA.

中文摘要翻译: 场景理解是通用物理智能的核心，而视频是捕获场景状态和时序动态的主要模态。然而，理解物理过程仍然困难，因为模型必须结合目标定位、手物交互、关系解析、时序推理和步骤级程序推理。现有基准通常单独评估这些能力，限制了诊断模型在程序性任务上失败原因的能力。我们提出了BARISTA，这是一个密集标注的自我中心数据集和基准，包含185个真实咖啡准备视频，涵盖全自动、意式手柄和胶囊三种工作流程。BARISTA提供经过逐帧验证的场景图，将持久化目标身份与掩码、轨迹、边界框、属性、类型化关系、手物交互、活动和过程步骤联系起来。从这些图中，我们派生出了涵盖短语定位、手物交互识别、指代、活动识别、关系抽取和时序视觉问答的零样本语言任务。实验揭示了不同任务系列间存在显著性能差异，且没有一致占优的模型系列，这使得BARISTA成为程序性视频理解的一个高难度诊断基准。代码和数据集发布在https://huggingface.co/datasets/ramblr/BARISTA。

</details>


### [90] [The Missing GAP: From Solving Square Jigsaw Puzzles to Handling Real World Archaeological Fragments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12077)
*Ofir Itzhak Shahar,Gur Elkin,Ohad Ben-Shahar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GAP数据集（含不规则形状的拼图碎片）和PuzzleFlow框架（基于ViT和Flow-Matching），用于解决非方形、不规则拼图碎片的拼图问题，在GAP数据集上优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有拼图求解方法大多局限于严格的方形拼图碎片，而真实世界（如考古碎片）中的拼图碎片形状不规则且高度残缺，现有方法无法处理此类复杂场景。

Method: 提出PuzzleFlow框架，基于Vision Transformer（ViT）和Flow-Matching模型，能够处理任意形状的复杂拼图碎片，通过学习碎片的分布特征完成拼图重构。

Result: 在提出的GAP数据集上，PuzzleFlow相比经典和最新的拼图求解方法均展现出更优的性能。

Conclusion: PuzzleFlow能够有效处理非方形、不规则形状的拼图碎片，为真实考古碎片等复杂场景的拼图任务提供了可行的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Jigsaw puzzle solving has been an increasingly popular task in the computer vision research community. Recent works have utilized cutting-edge architectures and computational approaches to reassemble groups of pieces into a coherent image, while achieving increasingly good results on well established datasets. However, most of these approaches share a common, restricting setting: operating solely on strictly square puzzle pieces. In this work, we introduce GAP, a set of novel jigsaw puzzles datasets containing synthetic, heavily eroded pieces of unrestricted shapes, generated by a learned distribution of real-world archaeological fragments. We also introduce PuzzleFlow, a novel ViT and Flow-Matching based framework for jigsaw puzzle solving, capable of handling complex puzzle pieces and demonstrating superior performance on GAP when compared to both classic and recent prominent works in this domain.

中文摘要翻译: 拼图求解在计算机视觉研究领域日益受到关注。近期工作利用先进的架构和计算方法将碎片组重新组合成连贯的图像，并在已有数据集上取得了越来越好的结果。然而，这些方法大多共享一个限制性设定：仅操作严格的方形拼图碎片。在这项工作中，我们引入了GAP，一组包含合成的高度侵蚀的不规则形状拼图碎片的新颖拼图数据集，这些碎片由学习到的真实世界考古碎片分布生成。我们还引入了PuzzleFlow，一种基于ViT和Flow-Matching的新型拼图求解框架，能够处理复杂形状的拼图碎片，并在GAP数据集上相比该领域中经典和近期的重要工作展现出优越的性能。

</details>


### [91] [When Policy Entropy Constraint Fails: Preserving Diversity in Flow-based RLHF via Perceptual Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12112)
*Xiaofeng Tan,Jun Liu,Bin-Bin Gao,Yuanting Fan,Xi Jiang,Chengjie Wang,Hongsong Wang,Feng Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了流匹配文本到图像生成模型在RLHF微调后多样性坍塌的问题，指出传统策略熵无法反映感知多样性，并提出感知熵及其两种正则化策略（PEC和PCGS）来改善质量-多样性权衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: RLHF微调流匹配文本到图像模型会导致严重的多样性坍塌，而传统假设认为策略熵与多样性相关，但在流模型中策略熵在感知多样性坍塌时保持不变，因此需要新的机制来捕获和保持感知多样性。

Method: 提出感知熵（Perceptual Entropy）来在感知空间中捕获多样性，并基于此提出两种熵正则化策略：感知熵约束（PEC）和生成空间感知约束（PCGS），以在微调过程中保持感知多样性并提升生成质量。

Result: 实验表明，PEC综合得分0.734（基线0.366），PCGS的多样性平均达0.989（基线0.047），在两种基座模型、神经/规则奖励和三种感知空间上均一致提升了质量-多样性权衡。

Conclusion: 标准策略熵不适用于流模型的多样性正则化，感知熵是更有效的替代方案；所提方法能有效缓解多样性坍塌并改善生成质量。

Original Abstract: RLHF is widely used to align flow-matching text-to-image models with human preferences, but often leads to severe diversity collapse after fine-tuning. In RL, diversity is often assumed to correlate with policy entropy, motivating entropy regularization. However, we show this intuition breaks in flow models: policy entropy remains constant, even while perceptual diversity collapses. We explain this mismatch both theoretically and empirically: the constant entropy arises from the fixed, pre-defined noise schedule, while the diversity collapse is driven by the mode-seeking nature of policy gradients. As a result, policy entropy fails to prevent the model from converging to a narrow high-reward region in the perceptual space. To this end, we introduce perceptual entropy that captures diversity in a perceptual space and maintains the property of standard entropy. Building upon this insight, we propose two entropy-regularized strategies, Perceptual Entropy Constraint and Perceptual Constraints on Generation Space, to preserve perceptual diversity and improve the quality. Experiments across two base models, neural and rule-based rewards, and three perceptual spaces demonstrate consistent gains in the quality-diversity trade-off; PEC achieves the best overall score of 0.734 (vs. baseline's 0.366); a complementary setting of PEC further reaches a diversity average of 0.989 (vs. baseline's 0.047). Our project page (https://xiaofeng-tan.github.io/projects/PEC) is publicly available.

中文摘要翻译: RLHF被广泛用于将流匹配文本到图像模型与人类偏好对齐，但微调后往往导致严重的多样性坍塌。在强化学习中，多样性通常被认为与策略熵相关，这促使了熵正则化的使用。然而，我们表明这一直觉在流模型中失效：策略熵保持恒定，而感知多样性却坍塌了。我们从理论和实验两方面解释了这种不匹配：恒定熵源于固定的预定义噪声调度，而多样性坍塌则是由策略梯度的模式寻求特性驱动的。因此，策略熵无法阻止模型收敛到感知空间中的一个狭窄高奖励区域。为此，我们引入了感知熵，它在感知空间中捕获多样性并保持标准熵的性质。基于这一见解，我们提出了两种熵正则化策略——感知熵约束（PEC）和生成空间感知约束（PCGS），以保持感知多样性并提升生成质量。在两种基座模型、神经奖励和基于规则的奖励以及三种感知空间上的实验表明，质量-多样性权衡一致得到提升；PEC的综合得分最佳达到0.734（基线为0.366）；PCGS的互补设置进一步使多样性平均值达到0.989（基线为0.047）。我们的项目页面（https://xiaofeng-tan.github.io/projects/PEC）已公开。

</details>


### [92] [MoCam: Unified Novel View Synthesis via Structured Denoising Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12119)
*Haofeng Liu,Yang Zhou,Ziheng Wang,Zhengbo Xu,Zhan Peng,Jie Ma,Jun Liang,Shengfeng He,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoCam通过结构化的去噪动态过程，在扩散模型中实现从几何到外观的协调渐进，统一了静态和动态视图合成，有效解决了几何先验与外观先验的冲突问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 生成式新视角合成面临根本困境：几何先验能提供空间对齐但在视角变化下变得稀疏且不准确，外观先验能提供视觉保真度但缺乏几何对应。现有方法要么传播几何误差，要么在静态融合两种先验时产生信号冲突。

Method: MoCam采用结构化去噪动态，在扩散过程中编排从几何到外观的协调渐进：早期阶段利用几何先验锚定粗糙结构并容忍其不完整性，后期阶段切换为外观先验主动纠正几何误差并细化细节。通过时间上解耦几何对齐和外观精炼，自然地统一了静态和动态视图合成。

Result: 实验表明，MoCam显著优于先前方法，特别是在点云包含严重空洞或畸变的情况下，实现了鲁棒的几何-外观解耦。

Conclusion: MoCam通过扩散过程中的时序解耦策略，有效解决了几何与外观先验的冲突问题，在复杂场景下实现了鲁棒的新视角合成。

Original Abstract: Generative novel view synthesis faces a fundamental dilemma: geometric priors provide spatial alignment but become sparse and inaccurate under view changes, while appearance priors offer visual fidelity but lack geometric correspondence. Existing methods either propagate geometric errors throughout generation or suffer from signal conflicts when fusing both statically. We introduce MoCam, which employs structured denoising dynamics to orchestrate a coordinated progression from geometry to appearance within the diffusion process.MoCam first leverages geometric priors in early stages to anchor coarse structures and tolerate their incompleteness, then switches to appearance priors in later stages to actively correct geometric errors and refine details. This design naturally unifies static and dynamic view synthesis by temporally decoupling geometric alignment and appearance refinement within the diffusion process.Experiments demonstrate that MoCam significantly outperforms prior methods, particularly when point clouds contain severe holes or distortions, achieving robust geometry-appearance disentanglement.

中文摘要翻译: 生成式新视角合成面临一个根本困境：几何先验提供了空间对齐，但在视角变化下变得稀疏且不准确；而外观先验提供了视觉保真度，但缺乏几何对应。现有方法要么在整个生成过程中传播几何误差，要么在静态融合两种先验时遭受信号冲突。我们提出MoCam，该方法采用结构化的去噪动态，在扩散过程中编排从几何到外观的协调渐进。MoCam首先在早期阶段利用几何先验锚定粗糙结构并容忍其不完整性，然后在后期阶段切换为外观先验，主动纠正几何误差并细化细节。这种设计通过在扩散过程中时间上解耦几何对齐与外观精炼，自然地统一了静态和动态视图合成。实验表明，MoCam显著优于先前方法，特别是在点云包含严重空洞或畸变的情况下，实现了鲁棒的几何-外观解耦。

</details>


### [93] [MULTI: Disentangling Camera Lens, Sensor, View, and Domain for Novel Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12134)
*Sonali Godavarthy,Matthias Neuwirth-Trapp,Tim-Felix Faasch,Maarten Bieshaar,Michael Moeller,Danda Pani Paudel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对现有文本到图像模型在控制成像因素（如镜头、传感器、视角、场景域特征）方面的不足，提出了一种名为MULTI的多因素解耦方法，通过两阶段文本反演实现成像因素的解耦与组合，在DF-RICO基准上验证了有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有文本到图像模型虽能生成高质量图像，但文本歧义导致无法精确控制特定风格或对象。当前工作几乎只关注图像内容，忽略了镜头、传感器类型、成像视角和场景域特征等成像因素，因此提出成像因素解耦这一新挑战。

Method: 提出MULTI方法，包含两个阶段：第一阶段学习通用因素（general factors），第二阶段提取数据集特定的因素（dataset-specific factors）。该设置支持扩展现有数据集和新型因素组合，缩小分布差距；同时支持特定因素修改和基于ControlNets的图像到图像生成。

Result: 在新建的DF-RICO基准上评估，实验结果表明MULTI方法有效，且突出了因素解耦作为一个新研究方向的重要性。

Conclusion: MULTI方法能够有效解耦成像因素，支持更灵活的图像生成控制，因素解耦是一个值得深入探索的新研究方向。

Original Abstract: Recent text-to-image models produce high-quality images, yet text ambiguity hinders precise control when specific styles or objects are required. There have been a number of recent works dealing with learning and composing multiple objects and patterns. However, current work focuses almost entirely on image content, overlooking imaging factors such as camera lens, sensor types, imaging viewpoints, and scenes' domain characteristics. We introduce this new challenge as Imaging Factor Disentanglement and show limitations of current approaches in the regime. We, therefore, propose the new method Multi-factor disentanglement through Textual Inversion (MULTI). It consists of two stages: in the first stage, we learn general factors, and in the second stage, we extract dataset-specific ones. This setup enables the extension of existing datasets and novel factor combinations, thereby reducing distribution gaps. It further supports modifications of specific factors and image-to-image generation via ControlNets. The evaluation on our new DF-RICO benchmark demonstrates the effectiveness of MULTI and highlights the importance of Factor Disentanglement as a new direction of research.

中文摘要翻译: 当前的文本到图像模型能够生成高质量的图像，但文本歧义问题阻碍了在需要特定风格或对象时的精确控制。已有一些近期工作处理了多个对象和模式的学习与组合问题。然而，现有研究几乎完全关注图像内容，忽略了诸如相机镜头、传感器类型、成像视角和场景域特征等成像因素。我们将这一新挑战定义为"成像因素解耦"（Imaging Factor Disentanglement），并展示了现有方法在该场景下的局限性。为此，我们提出了新的方法——基于文本反演的多因素解耦（MULTI）。该方法包含两个阶段：第一阶段学习通用因素，第二阶段提取数据集特定的因素。这种设置能够扩展现有数据集并实现新型因素组合，从而缩小分布差距。该方法还支持特定因素的修改以及通过ControlNets进行图像到图像的生成。在我们的新基准DF-RICO上的评估证明了MULTI的有效性，并凸显了因素解耦作为一个新的研究方向的重要性。

</details>


### [94] [Design Your Ad: Personalized Advertising Image and Text Generation with Unified Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12138)
*Yexing Xu,Wei Feng,Shen Zhang,Haohan Wang,Yuxin Qin,Yaoyu Li,Ao Ma,Yuhao Luo,Lu Wang,Xudong Ren,Haoran Wang,Run Ling,Zheng Zhang,Jingjing Lv,Junjie Shen,Ching Law,Longguang Wang,Yulan Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Uni-AdGen统一自回归框架，联合生成个性化图文广告，利用粗到细的用户偏好模块从历史行为中捕捉兴趣，并构建PAd1M数据集与PBS指标，实验证明其优于基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法使用多个独立模型、缺乏跨模态感知，且仅依赖反映平均偏好的CTR指标，无法生成个性化的图文广告。

Method: 设计Unified Advertisement Generative model（Uni-AdGen），采用单一自回归框架联合生成广告图像和文本；引入前景感知模块和指令微调增强内容真实感；提出粗到细的偏好理解模块，从含噪声的多模态历史行为中捕捉用户兴趣以驱动个性化生成；构建大规模个性化广告图文数据集PAd1M，并提出产品背景相似度（PBS）指标。

Result: 大量实验表明，Uni-AdGen在通用和个性化广告生成任务上均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: Uni-AdGen通过统一自回归框架和粗到细的偏好理解，有效实现了高质量的个性化图文广告生成。

Original Abstract: Generating realistic and user-preferred advertisements is a key challenge in e-commerce. Existing approaches utilize multiple independent models driven by click-through-rate (CTR) to controllably create attractive image or text advertisements. However, their pipelines lack cross-modal perception and rely on CTR that only reflects average preferences. Therefore, we explore jointly generating personalized image-text advertisements from historical click behaviors. We first design a Unified Advertisement Generative model (Uni-AdGen) that employs a single autoregressive framework to produce both advertising images and texts. By incorporating a foreground perception module and instruction tuning, Uni-AdGen enhances the realism of the generated content. To further personalize advertisements, we equip Uni-AdGen with a coarse-to-fine preference understanding module that effectively captures user interests from noisy multimodal historical behaviors to drive personalized generation. Additionally, we construct the first large-scale Personalized Advertising image-text dataset (PAd1M) and introduce a Product Background Similarity (PBS) metric to facilitate training and evaluation. Extensive experiments show that our method outperforms baselines in general and personalized advertisement generation. Our project is available at https://github.com/JD-GenX/Uni-AdGen.

中文摘要翻译: 生成逼真且符合用户偏好的广告是电子商务中的一个关键挑战。现有方法利用多个由点击率（CTR）驱动的独立模型，可控地生成有吸引力的图像或文本广告。然而，这些流水线缺乏跨模态感知能力，且仅依赖反映平均偏好的CTR。因此，我们探索从历史点击行为中联合生成个性化的图文广告。我们首先设计了一个统一的广告生成模型（Uni-AdGen），该模型采用单一自回归框架同时生成广告图像和文本。通过引入前景感知模块和指令微调，Uni-AdGen增强了生成内容的真实感。为了进一步实现广告个性化，我们为Uni-AdGen配备了一个粗到细的偏好理解模块，该模块能够从含噪声的多模态历史行为中有效捕捉用户兴趣，从而驱动个性化生成。此外，我们构建了首个大规模个性化广告图文数据集（PAd1M），并引入了产品背景相似度（PBS）指标以促进训练和评估。大量实验表明，我们的方法在通用和个性化广告生成任务上均优于基线方法。我们的项目可在 https://github.com/JD-GenX/Uni-AdGen 获取。

</details>


### [95] [EchoTracker2: Enhancing Myocardial Point Tracking by Modeling Local Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12140)
*Md Abulkalam Azad,Vegard Holmstrøm,John Nyberg,Lasse Lovstakken,Håvard Dalen,Bjørnar Grenne,Andreas Østvik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EchoTracker2，一种仅用精细阶段的架构用于超声心动图心肌点跟踪，通过增强像素级特征的局部时空上下文并整合长程时序推理，在分布内、分布外及公共合成数据集上均显著优于现有方法，并提升了全局纵向应变的一致性和测试-重测可重复性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 心肌运动源于生理约束下的形变，具有时空连续性且轨迹局部受限，而现有通用点跟踪方法采用的两阶段粗细粒度架构中粗初始化阶段在此领域可能是不必要的，因此作者重新设计了适用于心肌点跟踪的架构。

Method: 提出仅精细阶段的架构EchoTracker2，该架构用局部时空上下文丰富像素级精确特征，并整合长程联合时序推理以实现鲁棒的跟踪。

Result: 相比领域专用SOTA模型，位置精度提升6.5%，中位轨迹误差降低12.2%；相比最佳通用点跟踪方法，分别提升2.0%和5.3%；与专家导出的全局纵向应变（GLS）一致性更好，测试-重测可重复性增强。

Conclusion: 在心肌点跟踪任务中，精细阶段架构足以胜任，粗初始化阶段并非必要，EchoTracker2在多个指标上达到领域最优性能。

Original Abstract: Myocardial point tracking (MPT) has recently emerged as a promising direction for motion estimation in echocardiography, driven by advances in general-purpose point tracking methods. However, myocardial motion fundamentally differs from motion encountered in natural videos, as it arises from physiologically constrained deformation that is spatially and temporally continuous throughout the cardiac cycle. Consequently, motion trajectories typically remain locally confined despite substantial tissue deformation. Motivated by these properties, we revisit the architectural design for MPT and find that coarse initialization in commonly used two-stage coarse-to-fine architectures may be unnecessary in this domain. In this work, we propose a fine-stage-only architecture, \textbf{EchoTracker2}, which enriches pixel-precise features with local spatiotemporal context and integrates them with long-range joint temporal reasoning for robust tracking. Experimental results across in-distribution, out-of-distribution (OOD), and public synthetic datasets show that our model improves position accuracy by $6.5\%$ and reduces median trajectory error by $12.2\%$ relative to a domain-specific state-of-the-art (SOTA) model. Compared to the best general-purpose point tracking method, the improvements are $2.0\%$ and $5.3\%$, respectively. Moreover, EchoTracker2 shows better agreement with expert-derived global longitudinal strain (GLS) and enhances test-rest reproducibility. Source code will be available at: https://github.com/riponazad/ptecho.

中文摘要翻译: 心肌点跟踪（MPT）近年来因通用点跟踪方法的进展而成为超声心动图运动估计的一个有前景的方向。然而，心肌运动与自然视频中的运动有着根本区别，因为它源于生理约束下的形变，在整个心动周期中在空间和时间上都是连续的。因此，尽管组织形变显著，运动轨迹通常仍局限于局部区域。受这些特性启发，我们重新审视了MPT的架构设计，发现该领域中常用的两阶段粗细粒度架构中的粗初始化可能是不必要的。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种仅精细阶段的架构EchoTracker2，该架构用局部时空上下文丰富像素级精确特征，并整合长程联合时序推理以实现鲁棒的跟踪。在分布内、分布外（OOD）以及公共合成数据集上的实验结果表明，相比领域专用最先进（SOTA）模型，我们的模型位置精度提升了6.5%，中位轨迹误差降低了12.2%。与最佳通用点跟踪方法相比，改进分别为2.0%和5.3%。此外，EchoTracker2与专家导出的全局纵向应变（GLS）表现出更好的一致性，并增强了测试-重测可重复性。源代码将在https://github.com/riponazad/ptecho公开。

</details>


### [96] [PoseCompass: Intelligent Synthetic Pose Selection for Visual Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12144)
*Yanan Zhou,Zhaoyan Qian,Yanli Li,Nan Yang,Zhongliang Guo,Dong Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PoseCompass，一种基于3DGS的智能位姿选择管道，通过价值排序机制筛选信息量丰富的合成视角，用于绝对位姿回归（APR）的数据增强，在7-Scenes数据集上实现3倍加速和53.8%的位姿误差降低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于3DGS的数据增强方法在随机采样时会产生冗余视角和噪声样本（来自重建质量差的区域），导致APR模型微调效率低且精度受限。亟需一种智能的合成位姿选择方法来解决这些问题。

Method: 提出PoseCompass管道：1）构建基于价值的位姿排序机制，综合三个维度——定位难度（偏好挑战性区域）、覆盖新颖性（探索欠采样区域）和渲染可观测性（过滤伪影和噪声）；2）生成轨迹约束的候选位姿；3）选取Top-K排名位姿，用3DGS结合轻量级扩散对齐合成视图；4）在混合真实与合成数据上微调位姿回归器。

Result: 在7-Scenes数据集上，适应时间从15.2分钟降至5.1分钟（3倍加速）；中位位姿误差降低53.8%；显著优于随机采样基线方法。

Conclusion: PoseCompass通过智能选择信息量丰富的合成位姿，有效解决了3DGS增强中的冗余和噪声问题，大幅提升了APR微调的效率与精度。

Original Abstract: In visual localization, Absolute Pose Regression (APR) enables real-time 6-DoF camera pose inference from single images, yet critically depends on fine-tuning data quality and coverage. While recent methods leverage 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) for novel view synthesis-based data augmentation, random sampling generates redundant views and noisy samples from poorly reconstructed regions. To mitigate this research gap, we propose PoseCompass, an intelligent pose selection pipeline for 3DGS-based APR. PoseCompass formulates synthetic pose selection and derives a value-based pose ranking mechanism to identify informative poses. The ranking integrates three dimensions: Localization Difficulty, favoring challenging regions; Coverage Novelty, exploring under-sampled areas; and Rendering Observability, filtering artifacts and noise. PoseCompass then generates trajectory-constrained candidates, selects the top-K ranked poses, and synthesizes views using 3DGS with lightweight diffusion-based alignment. Finally, the pose regressor is fine-tuned on mixed real and synthetic data. We evaluate PoseCompass on 7-Scenes, where it reduces adaptation time from 15.2 to 5.1 minutes, a 3x speedup, while cutting median pose errors by 53.8 percent and significantly outperforming random baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 在视觉定位中，绝对位姿回归（APR）能够从单张图像实现实时6自由度相机位姿推断，但其性能严重依赖于微调数据的质量和覆盖范围。虽然最近有方法利用3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）进行基于新视角合成的数据增强，但随机采样会生成冗余视角以及来自重建质量差区域的噪声样本。为弥补这一研究空白，我们提出了PoseCompass，一种面向基于3DGS的APR的智能位姿选择管道。PoseCompass对合成位姿选择进行形式化建模，并推导出一种基于价值的位姿排序机制来识别信息丰富的位姿。该排序融合了三个维度：定位难度（偏好具有挑战性的区域）、覆盖新颖性（探索欠采样区域）以及渲染可观测性（过滤伪影和噪声）。PoseCompass随后生成轨迹约束的候选位姿，选取排名前K的位姿，并利用3DGS结合轻量级扩散对齐来合成视图。最后，在混合真实与合成数据上对位姿回归器进行微调。我们在7-Scenes数据集上评估了PoseCompass，其将适应时间从15.2分钟缩短至5.1分钟，实现了3倍加速，同时将中位位姿误差降低了53.8%，显著优于随机基线方法。

</details>


### [97] [Cross-Modal-Domain Generalization Through Semantically Aligned Discrete Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12145)
*Souptik Sen,Raneen Younis,Zahra Ahmadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoDAAR 通过索引级对齐建立跨模态离散语义共识，在保留模态特有结构的同时实现可泛化的跨模态表示，在多模态泛化基准上达到 SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法难以平衡跨模态泛化能力与模态特有结构的保留：连续（隐式）方法保留细粒度先验但泛化困难，离散（显式）方法强制共享原型但牺牲了模态特异性。

Method: 提出 CoDAAR 框架，核心是通过索引级对齐建立跨模态码本间的语义共识，包含两个互补机制：离散时间对齐（DTA）实现细粒度时间量化，级联语义对齐（CSA）促进渐进式跨模态语义一致性，在统一离散空间中实现无竞争的表示学习。

Result: 在事件分类、定位、视频分割和跨数据集迁移等跨模态泛化基准上达到 SOTA 性能。

Conclusion: CoDAAR 解决了一直存在的跨模态泛化与模态特异性之间的权衡问题，建立了离散化、可泛化的多模态表示学习新范式。

Original Abstract: Multimodal learning seeks to integrate information across diverse sensory sources, yet current approaches struggle to balance cross-modal generalizability with modality-specific structure. Continuous (implicit) methods preserve fine-grained priors but render generalization challenging, while discrete (explicit) approaches enforce shared prototypes at the expense of modality specificity. We introduce CoDAAR (Cross-modal Discrete Alignment And Reconstruction), a novel framework that resolves this long-standing trade-off by establishing semantic consensus across modality-specific codebooks through index-level alignment. This design uniquely allows CoDAAR to preserve modality-unique structures while achieving generalizable cross-modal representations within a unified discrete space. CoDAAR combines two complementary mechanisms: Discrete Temporal Alignment (DTA), which enables fine-grained temporal quantization, and Cascading Semantic Alignment (CSA), which promotes progressive cross-modal semantic agreement. Together, they establish a competition-free unified representation space. Trained with self-supervised reconstruction objectives on paired multimodal sequences, CoDAAR demonstrates robust cross-modal and cross-domain generalization. Across Cross-Modal Generalization benchmarks, including event classification, localization, video segmentation, and cross-dataset transfer, CoDAAR achieves state-of-the-art performance, establishing a new paradigm for discrete and generalizable multimodal representation learning.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态学习旨在整合来自不同感官来源的信息，然而当前的方法难以平衡跨模态泛化能力与模态特有结构。连续（隐式）方法保留了细粒度先验但使泛化变得困难，而离散（显式）方法强制使用共享原型却以牺牲模态特异性为代价。我们提出了 CoDAAR（跨模态离散对齐与重建），这是一个新颖的框架，通过索引级对齐建立跨模态码本间的语义共识，解决了这一长期存在的权衡问题。该设计独特地使 CoDAAR 能够保留模态独特结构，同时在统一的离散空间中实现可泛化的跨模态表示。CoDAAR 结合了两种互补机制：离散时间对齐（DTA），实现细粒度时间量化；以及级联语义对齐（CSA），促进渐进式跨模态语义一致性。二者共同建立了一个无竞争的统一表示空间。通过对配对多模态序列进行自监督重建目标训练，CoDAAR 展现了鲁棒的跨模态和跨域泛化能力。在跨模态泛化基准测试中，包括事件分类、定位、视频分割和跨数据集迁移，CoDAAR 取得了最先进的性能，为离散化、可泛化的多模态表示学习建立了新范式。

</details>


### [98] [Self-Consistent Latent Reasoning: Long Latent Sequence Reasoning for Vision-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12163)
*Chenfeng Wang,Wei He,Xuhan Zhu,Chunpeng Zhou,Qizhen Li,Song Yan,Yufei Zheng,Chengjun Yu,Fan Lu,Wei Zhai,Yang Cao,Pengfei Yu,Zheng-Jun Zha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCOLAR 提出了一种自一致潜在推理方法，通过轻量级去变换器将 LLM 的完整序列隐状态一次性生成独立锚定于原始视觉空间的辅助视觉 token，结合三阶段 SFT 和 ALPO 强化学习，解决了视觉潜在推理中序列变长导致性能下降的信息增益坍缩问题，将可接受潜在思维链长度提升 30 倍以上，在真实推理基准上取得最优结果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉潜在推理方法存在反直觉现象：随着潜在序列变长，性能反而系统性下降。作者揭示其根本原因是信息增益坍缩——自回归生成导致后续 token 几乎不引入新信息；同时，高度池化的图像嵌入作为监督目标几乎不提供有效信号。因此需要一种能打破信息增益坍缩、支持长序列潜在推理的方法。

Method: 提出 SCOLAR，包含：(1) 轻量级去变换器（detransformer），利用 LLM 的完整序列隐状态一次性生成辅助视觉 token，每个 token 独立锚定到原始视觉空间；(2) 三阶段监督微调（SFT）；(3) ALPO 强化学习。

Result: 将可接受的潜在思维链长度提升 30 倍以上；在真实推理基准上达到开源模型最优性能，比骨干模型提升 14.12%；展现出强大的分布外泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过打破信息增益坍缩，SCOLAR 有效支持长序列视觉潜在推理，显著提升了推理性能和泛化能力，验证了视觉潜在推理可以从更长潜在序列中受益。

Original Abstract: In language reasoning, longer chains of thought consistently yield better performance, which naturally suggests that visual latent reasoning may likewise benefit from longer latent sequences. However, we discover a counterintuitive phenomenon: the performance of existing latent visual reasoning methods systematically degrades as the latent sequence grows longer. We reveal the root cause: Information Gain Collapse -- autoregressive generation makes each step highly dependent on prior outputs, so subsequent tokens can barely introduce new information. We further identify that heavily pooled ($\geq 128\times$) image embeddings used as supervision targets provide no more signal than meaningless placeholders. Motivated by these insights, we propose SCOLAR (Self-COnsistent LAtent Reasoning), which introduces a lightweight detransformer that leverages the LLM's full-sequence hidden states to generate auxiliary visual tokens in a single shot, with each token independently anchored to the original visual space. Combined with three-stage SFT and ALPO reinforcement learning, SCOLAR extends acceptable latent CoT length by over $30\times$, achieves state-of-the-art among open-source models on real-world reasoning benchmarks (+14.12% over backbone), and demonstrates strong out-of-distribution generalization.

中文摘要翻译: 在语言推理中，更长的思维链 consistently 带来更好的性能，这自然暗示视觉潜在推理也可能从更长的潜在序列中受益。然而，我们发现了一个反直觉的现象：现有的视觉潜在推理方法会随着潜在序列变长而系统性退化。我们揭示了根本原因：信息增益坍缩——自回归生成使得每一步高度依赖于先前的输出，导致后续 token 几乎无法引入新信息。我们进一步发现，高度池化（≥128倍）的图像嵌入作为监督目标所提供的信号并不比无意义的占位符更多。受这些洞察的启发，我们提出了 SCOLAR（自一致潜在推理），该方法引入了一个轻量级去变换器，利用 LLM 的完整序列隐状态一次性生成辅助视觉 token，每个 token 独立地锚定到原始视觉空间。结合三阶段 SFT 和 ALPO 强化学习，SCOLAR 将可接受的潜在思维链长度提升了 30 倍以上，在真实推理基准上达到了开源模型中的最优水平（比骨干模型提升 14.12%），并展现出强大的分布外泛化能力。

</details>


### [99] [SyncDPO: Enhancing Temporal Synchronization in Video-Audio Joint Generation via Preference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12179)
*Xin Cheng,Xihua Wang,Ying Ba,Yuyue Wang,Kaisi Guan,Yinbo Wang,Wenpu Li,Ruihua Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SyncDPO，一种基于直接偏好优化（DPO）的视频-音频联合生成后训练框架，通过规则驱动的负样本构建和课程学习策略，在不增加额外采样成本的情况下提升时序对齐精度，在多个基准上显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频-音频联合生成方法在语义对应上表现良好，但难以实现精确的时序同步（音频事件与视觉触发源的细粒度对齐）。后训练阶段广泛使用监督微调（SFT）结合MSE损失，但MSE对微小时间错位的惩罚不足。直接偏好优化（DPO）虽能引入显式错位样本提升时序敏感性，但其传统流程依赖昂贵的采样-排序构建偏好对，计算成本过高。

Method: 提出了SyncDPO后训练框架，核心包括：（1）一套基于规则的即时负样本构建策略，通过扭曲时序结构（如音频-视频错位）生成负样本，无需额外标注或采样；（2）利用DPO对扭曲的视-音频对提供显式负监督，强化时序对齐能力；（3）采用课程学习策略，逐步增加负样本难度，从粗糙错位过渡到细微不一致。

Result: 在环境音视频、人类语音视频等四个多样化基准上进行的主观和客观实验表明，SyncDPO在提升模型时序对齐能力方面显著优于其他方法；在分布外基准上通过捕捉内在运动-声音动态展现出优异的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过DPO结合高效规则驱动的负样本构建与课程学习，能够在不引入额外计算开销的前提下显著提升视频-音频联合生成模型的时序对齐精度，且具有良好的泛化性能。

Original Abstract: Recent advancements in video-audio joint generation have achieved remarkable success in semantic correspondence. However, achieving precise temporal synchronization, which requires fine-grained alignment between audio events and their visual triggers, remains a challenging problem. The post-training method for joint generation is largely dominated by Supervised Fine-Tuning, but the commonly used Mean Squared Error loss provides insufficient penalties for subtle temporal misalignments. Direct Preference Optimization offers an alternative by introducing explicit misaligned counterparts to better improve temporal sensitivity. In this paper we propose a post-training framework SyncDPO, leveraging DPO to improve the temporal sensitivity of V-A joint generation. Conventional DPO pipelines typically depend on costly sampling-and-ranking procedures to construct preference pairs, resulting in substantial computational cost. To improve efficiency, we introduce a suite of on-the-fly rule-based negative construction strategies that distort temporal structures without incurring additional annotation or sampling. We demonstrate that the temporal alignment capability can be effectively reinforced by providing explicit negative supervision through temporally distorted V-A pairs. Accordingly, we implement a curriculum learning strategy that progressively increases the difficulty of negative samples, transitioning from coarse misalignment to subtle inconsistencies. Extensive objective and subjective experiments across four diverse benchmarks, ranging from ambient sound videos to human speech videos, demonstrate that SyncDPO significantly outperforms other methods in improving model's temporal alignment capability. It also demonstrates superior generalization on out-of-distribution benchmark by capturing intrinsic motion-sound dynamics. Demo and code is available in https://syncdpo.github.io/syncdpo/.

中文摘要翻译: 近期视频-音频联合生成在语义对应方面取得了显著进展。然而，实现精确的时序同步——即音频事件与其视觉触发源之间的细粒度对齐——仍然是一个具有挑战性的问题。联合生成的后训练方法主要由监督微调主导，但常用的均方误差损失对微小的时间错位惩罚不足。直接偏好优化提供了一种替代方案，通过引入显式的错位对应样本来更好地提升时序敏感性。本文提出了一个后训练框架SyncDPO，利用DPO提升视频-音频联合生成的时序敏感性。传统的DPO流程通常依赖昂贵的采样-排序过程来构建偏好对，导致大量计算开销。为提高效率，我们引入了一套基于规则的即时负样本构建策略，在不增加额外标注或采样的情况下扭曲时序结构。我们证明，通过扭曲的视-音频对提供显式负监督，可以有效增强时序对齐能力。相应地，我们实施了一种课程学习策略，逐步增加负样本的难度，从粗糙错位过渡到细微不一致。在从环境音视频到人类语音视频的四个多样化基准上进行的大量主观和客观实验表明，SyncDPO在提升模型时序对齐能力方面显著优于其他方法。通过捕捉内在的运动-声音动态，它在分布外基准上也展现出优异的泛化能力。演示和代码可在https://syncdpo.github.io/syncdpo/获取。

</details>


### [100] [Enhancing Domain Generalization in 3D Human Pose Estimation through Controllable Generative Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12198)
*Xinhao Hu,Yiyi Zhang,Liqing Zhang,Jianfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可控人体姿态生成框架，通过系统化改变姿态、背景和相机视角来合成多样化视频数据，以增强训练数据集、提升模型泛化能力，有效缓解域差异问题。实验表明该方法显著提升了模型在未见场景和数据集上的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 人体运动具有因果特性，训练与测试数据分布之间的域差异会严重影响3D人体姿态估计模型的性能。现有方法在处理域差异方面存在局限性，亟需一种能够丰富训练数据、增强模型泛化能力的方法。

Method: 提出可控人体姿态生成框架，通过系统化改变姿态、背景和相机视角来合成多样化视频数据；利用室内/真实世界和室外/虚拟数据集进行跨域数据融合与可控视频生成，构建针对实际部署场景的增强训练数据。

Result: 大规模实验表明，增强后的数据集显著提升了模型在未见场景和未见数据集上的性能，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过生成式数据增强，能够有效缓解域差异问题，提升3D人体姿态估计模型的泛化能力，为处理域差异问题提供了一种可行的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Pedestrian motion, due to its causal nature, is strongly influenced by domain gaps arising from discrepancies between training and testing data distributions. Focusing on 3D human pose estimation, this work presents a controllable human pose generation framework that synthesizes diverse video data by systematically varying poses, backgrounds, and camera viewpoints. This generative augmentation enriches training datasets, enhances model generalization, and alleviates the limitations of existing methods in handling domain discrepancies. By leveraging both indoor/real-world and outdoor/virtual datasets, we perform cross-domain data fusion and controllable video generation to construct enriched training data, tailored to realistic deployment settings. Extensive experiments show that the augmented datasets significantly improve model performance on unseen scenarios and datasets, validating the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

中文摘要翻译: 由于人体运动具有因果特性，其受训练数据与测试数据分布差异导致的域间差异影响显著。本文聚焦于3D人体姿态估计任务，提出了一种可控人体姿态生成框架，通过系统化改变姿态、背景和相机视角来合成多样化视频数据。这种生成式数据增强丰富了训练数据集，提升了模型泛化能力，并缓解了现有方法在处理域差异方面的局限性。通过利用室内/真实世界和室外/虚拟数据集，我们进行跨域数据融合与可控视频生成，以构建针对实际部署场景的增强训练数据。大规模实验表明，增强后的数据集显著提升了模型在未见场景和未见数据集上的性能，验证了所提方法的有效性。

</details>


### [101] [UHR-Micro: Diagnosing and Mitigating the Resolution Illusion in Earth Observation VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12237)
*Shuo Ni,Tong Wang,Jing Zhang,He Chen,Haonan Guo,Ning Zhang,Bo Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出高分辨率遥感视觉语言模型存在"分辨率幻觉"——高分辨率并未带来对微小目标的可靠感知。为此，构建了UHR-Micro基准（11,253条指令/1,212张超高分影像）并提出MAP-Agent，将查询分解为证据搜索步骤，主动检查候选区域，实现以证据为中心的高分辨率推理。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VLM在处理超高分遥感影像时，虽能提供丰富视觉细节，但对空间尺度极小、任务相关的微目标感知不可靠，存在"分辨率幻觉"现象。缺乏专门评估VLM在原生遥感影像空间极限下微目标感知能力的基准。

Method: 提出微证据主动感知（MAP）智能体：将查询分解为证据搜索步骤，主动检查候选区域，将答案基于局部观测进行定位推理，使高分辨率推理从以图像为中心转向以证据为中心。

Result: 实验表明，现有高分辨率VLM在空间定位和证据解析方面存在严重失败；增大模型容量无法完全解决该问题，失败与缺乏定位和使用任务相关微证据的指导密切相关。

Conclusion: UHR-Micro与MAP-Agent共同为评估、理解和推进遥感VLM的高分辨率推理提供了一个诊断平台，揭示了分辨率幻觉的本质原因并提出了有效的证据中心推理范式。

Original Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) increasingly operate on ultra-high-resolution (UHR) Earth observation imagery, yet they remain vulnerable to a severe scale mismatch between large-scale scene context and micro-scale targets. We refer to this empirical gap as a "resolution illusion": higher input resolution provides the appearance of richer visual detail, but does not necessarily yield reliable perception of spatially small, task-relevant evidence. To benchmark this challenge, we introduce UHR-Micro, a benchmark comprising 11,253 instructions grounded in 1,212 UHR images, designed to evaluate VLMs at the spatial limits of native Earth observation imagery. UHR-Micro spans diverse micro-target scales, context requirements, task families, and visual conditions, and provides diagnostic annotations that support controlled evaluation and fine-grained error attribution. Experiments with representative high-resolution VLMs show substantial failures in spatial grounding and evidence parsing, despite access to high-resolution inputs. Further analysis suggests that these failures are not fully resolved by increasing model capacity, but are closely tied to insufficient guidance in locating and using task-relevant micro-evidence. Motivated by this finding, we propose Micro-evidence Active Perception (MAP), a reference agent that decomposes queries into evidence-seeking steps, actively inspects candidate regions, and grounds its answers in localized observations. MAP-Agent improves micro-level perception by making high-resolution reasoning evidence-centered rather than image-centered. Together, UHR-Micro and MAP-Agent provide a diagnostic platform for evaluating, understanding, and advancing high-resolution reasoning in Earth observation VLMs. Datasets and source code were released at https://github.com/MiliLab/UHR-Micro.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉语言模型（VLM）越来越多地应用于超高分辨率（UHR）地球观测影像，但它们仍然容易受到大规模场景上下文与微尺度目标之间严重的尺度不匹配问题的影响。我们将这种经验性差距称为"分辨率幻觉"：更高的输入分辨率提供了更丰富视觉细节的表象，但并不一定能产生对空间上微小、与任务相关证据的可靠感知。为了对这一挑战进行基准测试，我们引入了UHR-Micro，这是一个基于1,212张UHR图像构建的包含11,253条指令的基准，旨在评估VLM在原生地球观测影像空间极限下的表现。UHR-Micro涵盖多种微目标尺度、上下文需求、任务类型和视觉条件，并提供支持受控评估和细粒度错误归因的诊断性标注。使用代表性高分辨率VLM进行的实验表明，尽管能够访问高分辨率输入，模型在空间定位和证据解析方面仍存在严重失败。进一步分析表明，这些失败无法通过增加模型容量完全解决，而是与缺乏定位和使用任务相关微证据的指导密切相关。基于这一发现，我们提出了微证据主动感知（MAP），一种将查询分解为证据搜索步骤、主动检查候选区域并将其答案基于局部观测进行定位推理的参考智能体。MAP-Agent通过使高分辨率推理以证据为中心而非以图像为中心，改善了微层级感知。UHR-Micro和MAP-Agent共同为评估、理解和推进地球观测VLM中的高分辨率推理提供了一个诊断平台。数据集和源代码已在https://github.com/MiliLab/UHR-Micro发布。

</details>


### [102] [From Image Hashing to Scene Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12259)
*Anh-Kiet Duong,Marie-Claire Iatrides,Petra Gomez-Krämer,Jean-Michel Carozza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HashSCD，一种基于分块哈希的无监督场景变化检测框架，通过在汉明空间进行XOR类操作实现高效的全局变化检测与局部定位，显著降低计算和存储成本。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统图像哈希仅支持全局比较，无法定位变化发生的位置，这阻碍了哈希在场景变化检测（需要空间定位）中的直接应用。本文旨在克服这一局限性。

Method: 提出HashSCD框架：将空间对齐的图像块编码为紧凑哈希码，通过类XOR操作在汉明空间直接进行变化检测与定位；采用无监督对比学习，在分块和全局两个层面联合训练。

Result: 与最先进的无监督哈希和场景变化检测方法相比，HashSCD取得了具有竞争力的性能，同时显著降低了计算成本和存储需求。

Conclusion: 分块哈希框架能够同时实现高效的全局变化检测和局部变化定位，无需对历史图像进行重复推理，是一种兼具效率与效果的无监督场景变化检测方案。

Original Abstract: Image hashing provides compact representations for efficient storage and retrieval but is inherently limited to global comparison and cannot reason about where changes occur. This limitation prevents hashing from being directly applicable to scene change detection, where spatial localization is essential. In this work, we revisit hashing from a scene change detection perspective and propose HashSCD, a patch-wise hashing framework that enables both efficient global change detection and localized change identification. HashSCD encodes spatially aligned patches into compact hash codes and aggregates them through an XOR-like operation, allowing change detection and localization to be performed directly in the Hamming space without repeated inference on previous images. The model is trained in an unsupervised manner using contrastive learning at both patch and global levels. Experiments demonstrate that HashSCD achieves competitive performance compared to state-of-the-art unsupervised hashing and scene change detection methods, while significantly reducing computational cost and storage requirements.

中文摘要翻译: 图像哈希通过紧凑表示实现高效的存储与检索，但其本质上局限于全局比较，无法推理变化发生的位置。这一局限性阻碍了哈希在需要空间定位的场景变化检测中的直接应用。本文从场景变化检测的视角重新审视哈希，提出HashSCD——一种分块哈希框架，能够同时实现高效的全局变化检测和局部变化定位。HashSCD将空间对齐的图像块编码为紧凑的哈希码，并通过类XOR操作进行聚合，从而无需对历史图像进行重复推理即可直接在汉明空间完成变化检测与定位。该模型采用对比学习以无监督方式在分块和全局两个层面进行训练。实验表明，与最先进的无监督哈希和场景变化检测方法相比，HashSCD取得了具有竞争力的性能，同时显著降低了计算成本和存储需求。

</details>


### [103] [Large-Small Model Collaboration for Farmland Semantic Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12282)
*Xinjia Li,Rui Wang,Qiurong Peng,Lingfei Ye,Dengrong Zhang,Haoyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了大规模细粒度农田变化检测基准数据集HZNU-FCD，并提出一种大-小模型协同的语义变化检测框架（FD-Mamba + CMLA），有效抑制了物候伪变化，在多个数据集上取得了优异性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有农田语义变化检测数据集缺乏精细的"从-到"标注，且视觉变化检测模型易受作物轮作、季节变化和光照差异等物候伪变化干扰，难以满足细粒度的农田转换监测需求。

Method: 提出大-小模型协同的SCD框架：小模型为细粒度差异感知Mamba（FD-Mamba），学习密集变化表征以保持边界和定位小区域；大模型路径为跨模态逻辑仲裁（CMLA），引入CLIP文本先验进行提示引导的语义仲裁和伪变化抑制；采用难区域协同训练策略，仅在低置信度像素上监督CMLA语义得分图。

Result: 在HZNU-FCD上达到97.63% F1、96.32% IoU和96.35% SCD_IoU_mean，仅需6.65M可训练参数；相比ChangeCLIP-ViT，F1提升10.19个百分点；在LEVIR-CD和WHU-CD上也取得优异结果，展现强鲁棒性和泛化性。

Conclusion: 所提出的HZNU-FCD基准和大-小模型协同框架有效解决了农田变化检测中的伪变化干扰问题，在多个数据集上取得了领先性能，验证了方法的有效性和泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Farmland Semantic Change Detection (SCD) is essential for cultivated land protection, yet existing benchmarks and models remain insufficient for fine-grained farmland conversion monitoring. Current datasets often lack dedicated "from-to" annotations, while visual change detection models are easily disturbed by phenology-induced pseudo-changes caused by crop rotation, seasonal variation, and illumination differences. To address these challenges, we construct HZNU-FCD, a large-scale fine-grained farmland SCD benchmark with a unified five-class farmland-to-non-farmland annotation protocol. It contains 4,588 bitemporal image pairs with pixel-level labels for practical farmland protection. Based on this benchmark, we propose a large-small collaborative SCD framework that integrates a task-driven small visual model with a frozen large vision-language model. The small model, Fine-grained Difference-aware Mamba (FD-Mamba), learns dense change representations for boundary preservation and small-region localization. The large-model pathway, Cross-modal Logical Arbitration (CMLA), introduces CLIP-based textual priors for prompt-guided semantic arbitration and pseudo-change suppression. To enable effective collaboration, we design a hard-region co-training strategy that supervises the CMLA semantic score map only on low-confidence pixels. Experiments show that our method achieves 97.63% F1, 96.32% IoU, and 96.35% SCD_IoU_mean on HZNU-FCD with only 6.65M trainable parameters. Compared with the multimodal ChangeCLIP-ViT, which leverages vision-language information for change detection, our method improves F1 by 10.19 percentage points on HZNU-FCD. It also achieves 91.43% F1 and 84.21% IoU on LEVIR-CD, and 93.85% F1 and 88.41% IoU on WHU-CD, demonstrating strong robustness and generalization. The code is available at https://github.com/Lovelymili/FD-Mamba.

中文摘要翻译: 农田语义变化检测（SCD）对于耕地保护至关重要，然而现有的基准和模型仍不足以支持细粒度的农田转换监测。当前数据集通常缺乏专用的"从-到"标注，而视觉变化检测模型容易受到作物轮作、季节性变化和光照差异引起的物候伪变化干扰。为应对这些挑战，我们构建了HZNU-FCD，一个大规模细粒度农田SCD基准，采用统一的五类农田到非农田标注协议。该基准包含4,588对双时相图像及像素级标签，服务于实际耕地保护需求。基于该基准，我们提出了一种大-小模型协同的SCD框架，集成了任务驱动的小型视觉模型与冻结的大型视觉-语言模型。小模型为细粒度差异感知Mamba（FD-Mamba），学习密集变化表征以保持边界并定位小区域；大模型路径为跨模态逻辑仲裁（CMLA），引入基于CLIP的文本先验进行提示引导的语义仲裁和伪变化抑制。为实现有效协同，我们设计了难区域协同训练策略，仅在低置信度像素上监督CMLA语义得分图。实验表明，我们的方法在HZNU-FCD上以仅6.65M可训练参数达到97.63%的F1、96.32%的IoU和96.35%的SCD_IoU_mean。与利用视觉-语言信息进行变化检测的多模态ChangeCLIP-ViT相比，我们的方法在HZNU-FCD上的F1提升了10.19个百分点。该方法在LEVIR-CD上达到91.43%的F1和84.21%的IoU，在WHU-CD上达到93.85%的F1和88.41%的IoU，展现了强鲁棒性和泛化能力。代码已开源：https://github.com/Lovelymili/FD-Mamba。

</details>


### [104] [Images in Sentences: Scaling Interleaved Instructions for Unified Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12305)
*Yabo Zhang,Kunchang Li,Dewei Zhou,Xinyu Huang,Xun Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: INSET提出将图像作为原生词汇嵌入文本指令中，利用Transformer的上下文局部性实现精准对象绑定，并通过大规模数据引擎合成1500万高质量交错样本，在多图像一致性和文本对齐上显著超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有多模态语言模型在处理复杂交错指令时性能下降，根本原因在于图像与文本在结构上的分离，导致模型需要建立困难的长距离依赖关系来匹配描述与视觉目标。

Method: 提出INSET统一生成模型，将图像作为原生词汇嵌入文本指令，使视觉特征直接位于对应语义槽位，利用Transformer的上下文局部性实现精准对象绑定；同时设计可扩展数据引擎，从标准图像和视频数据集中合成1500万高质量交错样本。

Result: 在InterleaveBench上，INSET在多图像一致性和文本对齐方面显著优于现有方法，且随着输入复杂度增加性能差距进一步扩大；该方法还能自然扩展到多模态图像编辑任务。

Conclusion: 将图像作为文本中的原生词汇嵌入能有效解决交错指令下的长距离依赖问题，提升多模态生成的准确性和一致性，且方法具有良好扩展性。

Original Abstract: While recent advancements in multimodal language models have enabled image generation from expressive multi-image instructions, existing methods struggle to maintain performance under complex interleaved instructions. This limitation stems from the structural separation of images and text in current paradigms, which forces models to bridge difficult long-range dependencies to match descriptions with visual targets. To address these challenges, we propose \texttt{I}mages i\texttt{N} \texttt{SE}n\texttt{T}ences (\textit{a.k.a}, INSET), a unified generation model that seamlessly embeds images as native vocabulary within textual instructions. By positioning visual features directly at their corresponding semantic slots, INSET leverages the contextual locality of transformers for precise object binding, effectively treating images as dense, expressive language tokens. Furthermore, we introduce a scalable data engine that synthesizes 15M high-quality interleaved samples from standard image and video datasets, utilizing VLMs and LLMs to construct rich, long-horizon sequences. Evaluation results on InterleaveBench demonstrate that INSET significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in multi-image consistency and text alignment, with performance gaps widening as input complexity increases. Beyond standard generation, our approach inherently extends to multimodal image editing, integrating visual content as part of the instruction to facilitate highly expressive and creative visual manipulations.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管多模态语言模型的最新进展使得从表达性多图像指令生成图像成为可能，但现有方法在处理复杂交错指令时难以保持性能。这一限制源于当前范式下图像与文本的结构分离，迫使模型建立困难的长距离依赖关系以将描述与视觉目标匹配。为解决这些挑战，我们提出INSET（Images in Sentences），一种统一生成模型，将图像作为原生词汇无缝嵌入文本指令中。通过将视觉特征直接定位到对应的语义槽位，INSET利用Transformer的上下文局部性实现精准对象绑定，有效将图像视为稠密、具有表达力的语言令牌。此外，我们引入一个可扩展的数据引擎，从标准图像和视频数据集中合成1500万高质量交错样本，利用视觉语言模型和大型语言模型构建丰富的长程序列。InterleaveBench上的评估结果表明，INSET在多图像一致性和文本对齐方面显著优于最先进方法，且随着输入复杂度增加性能差距进一步扩大。除标准生成任务外，我们的方法还能自然扩展到多模态图像编辑领域，将视觉内容作为指令的一部分，从而促进高度表达性和创造性的视觉操作。

</details>


### [105] [G$^2$TR: Generation-Guided Visual Token Reduction for Separate-Encoder Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12309)
*Junxian Li,Kai Liu,Zizhong Ding,Zhixin Wang,Zhikai Chen,Renjing Pei,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出G²TR，一种面向分离编码器统一多模态模型的生成引导视觉令牌缩减框架，通过VAE潜在一致性评估令牌重要性、平衡选择和合并冗余令牌，在保持理解和编辑能力的同时实现1.94倍的令牌与预填充计算缩减。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉令牌缩减方法（如基于注意力分数、图文相似度）隐式假设最终目标是判别式推理，但该假设不适用于统一多模态模型（UMMs），因为理解侧的视觉令牌还需保留图像编辑能力。需要一种能同时兼顾理解与生成能力的令牌缩减方法。

Method: G²TR框架包含三个核心步骤：（1）通过视觉令牌与VAE潜在表示的一致性估计令牌重要性；（2）进行平衡的令牌选择；（3）将冗余令牌合并到保留的代表性令牌中以减少信息损失。该方法无需训练、即插即用，仅在理解编码阶段之后应用。

Result: 在图像理解与编辑基准上，G²TR将视觉令牌和预填充计算减少了1.94倍，同时保持了推理准确率和编辑质量，在几乎所有基准上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 生成分支能够为识别理解侧视觉令牌提供任务无关的信号，这些令牌不仅具有语义相关性，还对潜在空间图像重建和生成至关重要。G²TR是一种训练免费、即插即用的高效视觉令牌缩减方案。

Original Abstract: The development of separate-encoder Unified multimodal models (UMMs) comes with a rapidly growing inference cost due to dense visual token processing. In this paper, we focus on understanding-side visual token reduction for improving the efficiency of separate-encoder UMMs. While this topic has been widely studied for MLLMs, existing methods typically rely on attention scores, text-image similarity and so on, implicitly assuming that the final objective is discriminative reasoning. This assumption does not hold for UMMs, where understanding-side visual tokens must also preserve the model's capabilities for editing images. We propose G$^2$TR, a generation-guided visual token reduction framework for separate-encoder UMMs. Our key insight is that the generation branch provides a task-agnostic signal for identifying understanding-side visual tokens that are not only semantically relevant but also important for latent-space image reconstruction and generation. G$^2$TR estimates token importance from consistency with VAE latent, performs balanced token selection, and merges redundant tokens into retained representatives to reduce information loss. The method is training-free, plug-and-play, and applied only after the understanding encoding stage, making it compatible with existing UMM inference pipelines. Experiments on image understanding and editing benchmarks show that G$^2$TR substantially reduces visual tokens and prefill computation by 1.94x while maintaining both reasoning accuracy and editing quality, outperforming baselines on almost all benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 分离编码器统一多模态模型（UMMs）的发展伴随着由于密集视觉令牌处理而导致推理成本的快速增长。本文聚焦于理解侧视觉令牌缩减，以提高分离编码器UMMs的效率。虽然该主题已在多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）中得到广泛研究，但现有方法通常依赖注意力分数、图像-文本相似度等，隐式假设最终目标是判别式推理。这一假设对UMMs不成立，因为理解侧的视觉令牌还必须保留模型编辑图像的能力。我们提出G²TR，一种面向分离编码器UMMs的生成引导视觉令牌缩减框架。我们的关键见解是：生成分支能够为识别理解侧视觉令牌提供任务无关的信号，这些令牌不仅具有语义相关性，还对潜在空间图像重建和生成至关重要。G²TR通过评估令牌与VAE潜在表示的一致性来估计令牌重要性，执行平衡令牌选择，并将冗余令牌合并到保留的代表性令牌中以减少信息损失。该方法无需训练、即插即用，且仅在理解编码阶段之后应用，使其与现有UMM推理流水线兼容。在图像理解与编辑基准上的实验表明，G²TR将视觉令牌和预填充计算减少了1.94倍，同时保持了推理准确率和编辑质量，在几乎所有基准上优于基线方法。

</details>


### [106] [Contrastive Learning under Noisy Temporal Self-Supervision for Colonoscopy Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12320)
*Luca Parolari,Pietro Gori,Lamberto Ballan,Carlo Biffi,Loic Le Folgoc*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种噪声感知的对比学习框架（NTSSL），利用结肠镜检查视频的时间结构自监督地学习息肉轨迹表示，无需昂贵的人工标注，在息肉检索、重识别、尺寸估计和组织学分类等多个下游任务上超越了现有自监督和监督基线，甚至匹配或超过了最近的基础模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 监督对比学习需要正确的正负样本定义，而收集这些标签需要将视频中描述同一息肉的轨迹链接起来，成本高昂且需要专业临床知识。因此，需要一种无需昂贵标注的自监督方法来学习鲁棒的息肉轨迹表示。

Method: 利用结肠镜检查过程的连续工作流，从时间结构中推导自监督的关联；由于时间关联不一定正确，引入了一种噪声感知的对比损失（noise-aware contrastive loss）来处理有噪声的关联。

Result: 在息肉检索、重识别、尺寸估计和组织学分类等多个下游任务上，该方法优于先前的自监督和监督基线，并且在使用仅27个视频训练的轻量级编码器的情况下，匹配或超过了所有任务上最近的基础模型。

Conclusion: 利用时间结构进行噪声感知的自监督对比学习，可以在无需昂贵临床标注的情况下有效学习息肉轨迹表示，并在多个下游任务上取得优异性能。

Original Abstract: Learning robust representations of polyp tracklets is key to enabling multiple AI-assisted colonoscopy applications, from polyp characterization to automated reporting and retrieval. Supervised contrastive learning is an effective approach for learning such representations, but it typically relies on correct positive and negative definitions. Collecting these labels requires linking tracklets that depict the same underlying polyp entity throughout the video, which is costly and demands specialized clinical expertise. In this work, we leverage the sequential workflow of colonoscopy procedures to derive self-supervised associations from temporal structure. Since temporally derived associations are not guaranteed to be correct, we introduce a noise-aware contrastive loss to account for noisy associations. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the learned representations across multiple downstream tasks, including polyp retrieval and re-identification, size estimation, and histology classification. Our method outperforms prior self-supervised and supervised baselines, and matches or exceeds recent foundation models across all tasks, using a lightweight encoder trained on only 27 videos. Code is available at https://github.com/lparolari/ntssl.

中文摘要翻译: 学习鲁棒的息肉轨迹表示是实现多种AI辅助结肠镜检查应用（从息肉表征到自动报告和检索）的关键。监督对比学习是学习此类表示的有效方法，但它通常依赖于正确的正负样本定义。收集这些标签需要链接视频中描述同一息肉的轨迹，这成本高昂且需要专业的临床知识。在这项工作中，我们利用结肠镜检查过程的连续工作流，从时间结构中推导自监督的关联。由于时间推导的关联不能保证正确，我们引入了一种噪声感知的对比损失来处理有噪声的关联。我们在多个下游任务上证明了所学表示的有效性，包括息肉检索与重识别、尺寸估计和组织学分类。我们的方法优于先前的自监督和监督基线，并且在使用仅27个视频训练的轻量级编码器的情况下，匹配或超过了所有任务上最近的基础模型。代码可在 https://github.com/lparolari/ntssl 获取。

</details>


### [107] [VIP: Visual-guided Prompt Evolution for Efficient Dense Vision-Language Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12325)
*Hao Zhu,Shuo Jin,Wenbin Liao,Jiayu Xiao,Yan Zhu,Siyue Yu,Feng Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VIP通过视觉引导的提示进化（Visual-guided Prompt evolution）方法，在无需额外训练的情况下，利用dino.txt框架解决文本查询语义歧义问题，实现了高效且通用的开放词汇语义分割，在多个基准上超越现有方法1.4%~8.4%平均mIoU。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于CLIP的方法存在深层空间偏差，而dino.txt虽然具备良好的空间感知能力，但其密集跨模态交互中存在文本查询语义歧义导致的严重不匹配问题。为释放dino.txt在细粒度物体感知中的潜力，需修正其文本查询的语义表达能力。

Method: 提出视觉引导的提示进化（VIP）方法，包括：1) 别名扩展（alias expansion）丰富文本查询的语义多样性；2) 视觉引导的蒸馏机制挖掘有价值的语义线索；3) 显著性感知的稳健聚合策略生成高保真预测结果。整个流程无需训练。

Result: VIP在多个基准上超越顶尖方法1.4%~8.4%平均mIoU，在多种具有挑战性的领域上具有良好的泛化能力，且推理时间和内存开销极小。

Conclusion: VIP通过视觉引导的提示进化有效解决了dino.txt中文本查询语义歧义问题，在无需训练的情况下实现了高效、通用且高质量的开放词汇语义分割。

Original Abstract: Pursuing training-free open-vocabulary semantic segmentation in an efficient and generalizable manner remains challenging due to the deep-seated spatial bias in CLIP. To overcome the limitations of existing solutions, this work moves beyond the CLIP-based paradigm and harnesses the recent spatially-aware dino.txt framework to facilitate more efficient and high-quality dense prediction. While dino.txt exhibits robust spatial awareness, we find that the semantic ambiguity of text queries gives rise to severe mismatch within its dense cross-modal interactions. To address this, we introduce \textcolor{oursblue}{\textbf{VI}}sual-guided \textcolor{oursblue}{\textbf{P}}rompt evolution (\textcolor{oursblue}{\textbf{\textit{VIP}}}) to rectify the semantic expressiveness of text queries in dino.txt, unleashing its potential for fine-grained object perception. Towards this end, \VIP integrates alias expansion with a visual-guided distillation mechanism to mine valuable semantic cues, which are robustly aggregated in a saliency-aware manner to yield a high-fidelity prediction. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that \VIP: \ding{182} surpasses the top-leading methods by $1.4\% \sim 8.4\%$ average mIoU, \ding{183} generalizes well to diverse challenging domains, and \ding{184} requires marginal inference time and memory overhead. \href{https://github.com/MiSsU-HH/VIP}{Our code is publicly available at GitHub \faGithub}.

中文摘要翻译: 在实际无训练的条件下追求高效且通用的开放词汇语义分割，由于CLIP中根深蒂固的空间偏差，仍然具有挑战性。为克服现有解决方案的局限性，本工作超越了基于CLIP的范式，利用近期提出的具有空间感知能力的dino.txt框架来实现更高效、更高质量的密集预测。尽管dino.txt展现出强大的空间感知能力，但我们发现文本查询的语义歧义会导致其密集跨模态交互中出现严重的不匹配问题。为解决这一问题，我们引入了视觉引导的提示进化（VIP），用于修正dino.txt中文本查询的语义表达能力，释放其在细粒度物体感知方面的潜力。为此，VIP将别名扩展与视觉引导的蒸馏机制相结合，以挖掘有价值的语义线索，并以显著性感知的方式稳健聚合这些线索，生成高保真预测结果。大量实验表明，VIP在平均mIoU上超越顶尖方法1.4%~8.4%，能够很好地泛化到多种具有挑战性的领域，且仅需极少的推理时间和内存开销。我们的代码已在GitHub上公开。

</details>


### [108] [Fill the GAP: A Granular Alignment Paradigm for Visual Reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12374)
*Yanting Miao,Yutao Sun,Dexin Wang,Mengyu Zhou,Pascal Poupart,Lei Lv,Qi Zhao,Li Wang,Hao Li,Xiaoxi Jiang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GAP（粒度对齐范式）用于视觉潜在推理，解决多模态大语言模型中因特征空间不匹配导致的潜在推理不稳定问题，通过三级对齐（特征级、上下文级、能力引导级）提升感知与推理性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉潜在推理方法遵循“输出即输入”的潜在范式，但效果不稳定。作者发现根本原因在于：基于预归一化（pre-norm）MLLM构建的视觉潜在模型，直接复用解码器隐藏状态作为预测的潜在输入，而这些状态与模型训练时所消费的输入嵌入处于截然不同的范数（norm）区间，导致直接潜在反馈不可靠。

Method: GAP包含三级对齐：(1) 特征级对齐：通过轻量级PCA对齐的潜在头（latent head）将解码器输出映射为与输入兼容的视觉潜在表示；(2) 上下文级对齐：利用可检查的辅助视觉监督来锚定潜在目标；(3) 能力引导级对齐：仅在基础MLLM难以处理样本上选择性施加潜在监督。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL 7B模型上，GAP在所有受监督变体中取得了最佳的聚合感知与推理性能。推理时干预探测进一步表明，生成的潜在表示提供的不仅是额外token槽位，而是任务相关的视觉信号。

Conclusion: 特征空间不匹配是现有视觉潜在推理不稳定的关键原因，GAP通过多级对齐有效缓解该问题，使潜在表示提供实质性的任务相关视觉信息。

Original Abstract: Visual latent reasoning lets a multimodal large language model (MLLM) create intermediate visual evidence as continuous tokens, avoiding external tools or image generators. However, existing methods usually follow an output-as-input latent paradigm and yield unstable gains. We identify evidence for a feature-space mismatch that can contribute to this instability: dominant visual-latent models build on pre-norm MLLMs and reuse decoder hidden states as predicted latent inputs, even though these states occupy a substantially different norm regime from the input embeddings the model was trained to consume~\citep{xie2025mhc,li2026siamesenorm,team2026attention}. This mismatch can make direct latent feedback unreliable. Motivated by this diagnosis, we propose \textbf{GAP}, a \textbf{G}ranular \textbf{A}lignment \textbf{P}aradigm for visual latent modeling. GAP aligns visual latent reasoning at three levels: feature-level alignment maps decoder outputs into input-compatible visual latents through a lightweight PCA-aligned latent head; context-level alignment grounds latent targets with inspectable auxiliary visual supervision; and capacity-guided alignment assigns latent supervision selectively to examples where the base MLLM struggles. On Qwen2.5-VL 7B, the resulting model achieves the best mean aggregate perception and reasoning performance among our supervised variants. Inference-time intervention probing further suggests that generated latents provide task-relevant visual signal beyond merely adding token slots.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉潜在推理使多模态大语言模型（MLLM）能够以连续令牌的形式创建中间视觉证据，从而避免使用外部工具或图像生成器。然而，现有方法通常遵循一种“输出即输入”的潜在范式，导致增益不稳定。我们识别出导致这种不稳定的特征空间不匹配的证据：主流的视觉潜在模型构建在预归一化（pre-norm）MLLM之上，直接复用解码器隐藏状态作为预测的潜在输入，尽管这些状态所处的范数（norm）区间与模型训练时所消费的输入嵌入存在显著差异。这种不匹配使得直接的潜在反馈变得不可靠。基于这一诊断，我们提出了GAP——一种用于视觉潜在建模的粒度对齐范式。GAP在三个层面进行视觉潜在推理对齐：特征级对齐通过轻量级PCA对齐的潜在头将解码器输出映射为与输入兼容的视觉潜在表示；上下文级对齐利用可检查的辅助视觉监督来锚定潜在目标；能力引导级对齐仅在基础MLLM难以处理的样本上选择性施加潜在监督。在Qwen2.5-VL 7B模型上，所得模型在我们所有受监督变体中取得了最佳的聚合感知与推理性能。推理时干预探测进一步表明，生成的潜在表示提供的不仅是额外token槽位，而是任务相关的视觉信号。

</details>


### [109] [Fast Image Super-Resolution via Consistency Rectified Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12377)
*Jiaqi Xu,Wenbo Li,Haoze Sun,Fan Li,Zhixin Wang,Long Peng,Jingjing Ren,Haoran Yang,Xiaowei Hu,Renjing Pei,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowSR提出了一种基于修正流（Rectified Flow）的单步图像超分辨率方法，通过改进的一致性学习策略和快慢调度机制，在单步采样中实现高质量、高保真的超分辨率重建。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有扩散模型在超分辨率任务中虽效果优异，但多步采样耗时巨大；而现有的少步或单步方法要么对噪声输入建模效率低下，要么未能充分利用迭代生成先验，导致重建图像的保真度和质量受损。

Method: 将超分辨率问题重构为从低分辨率到高分辨率的修正流（Rectified Flow）；引入HR正则化改进一致性蒸馏过程，使学习到的SR流既保持自一致性又精确收敛到真实HR目标；提出快慢调度策略，相邻时间步分别从快速调度器（少步高效）和慢速调度器（多步捕捉细节纹理）采样。

Result: 大量实验表明FlowSR在效率和图像质量方面均取得了优异性能。

Conclusion: FlowSR通过修正流框架、HR正则化的一致性学习和快慢调度策略，成功实现了单步、高质量、高保真的图像超分辨率重建。

Original Abstract: Diffusion models (DMs) have demonstrated remarkable success in real-world image super-resolution (SR), yet their reliance on time-consuming multi-step sampling largely hinders their practical applications. While recent efforts have introduced few- or single-step solutions, existing methods either inefficiently model the process from noisy input or fail to fully exploit iterative generative priors, compromising the fidelity and quality of the reconstructed images. To address this issue, we propose FlowSR, a novel approach that reformulates the SR problem as a rectified flow from low-resolution (LR) to high-resolution (HR) images. Our method leverages an improved consistency learning strategy to enable high-quality SR in a single step. Specifically, we refine the original consistency distillation process by incorporating HR regularization, ensuring that the learned SR flow not only enforces self-consistency but also converges precisely to the ground-truth HR target. Furthermore, we introduce a fast-slow scheduling strategy, where adjacent timesteps for consistency learning are sampled from two distinct schedulers: a fast scheduler with fewer timesteps to improve efficiency, and a slow scheduler with more timesteps to capture fine-grained texture details. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FlowSR achieves outstanding performance in both efficiency and image quality.

中文摘要翻译: 扩散模型（DMs）在真实图像超分辨率（SR）中已展现出显著成功，但其对耗时多步采样的依赖极大地阻碍了实际应用。尽管近期工作引入了少步或单步解决方案，但现有方法要么对噪声输入的过程建模低效，要么未能充分利用迭代生成先验，损害了重建图像的保真度和质量。为解决这一问题，我们提出FlowSR，一种将超分辨率问题重构为从低分辨率（LR）到高分辨率（HR）图像的修正流（Rectified Flow）的新方法。我们的方法利用改进的一致性学习策略，实现单步高质量超分辨率。具体而言，我们通过引入HR正则化来改进原始一致性蒸馏过程，确保所学习的SR流不仅保持自一致性，还能精确收敛到真实HR目标。此外，我们提出了一种快慢调度策略，其中用于一致性学习的相邻时间步从两个不同的调度器中采样：快速调度器使用较少时间步以提高效率，慢速调度器使用更多时间步以捕捉细粒度的纹理细节。大量实验表明，FlowSR在效率和图像质量方面均取得了卓越性能。

</details>


### [110] [Beyond Localization: A Comprehensive Diagnosis of Perspective-Conditioned Spatial Reasoning in MLLMs from Omnidirectional Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12413)
*Yuangong Chen,Wai Keung Wong,Jiaxing Li,Ioannis Patras,Xu Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PCSR-Bench基准（84,373个问答对，来自2,600张全景图），系统评估多模态大语言模型在360°全景图像中视角条件空间推理能力。实验发现模型存在显著感知-推理鸿沟（基础方向任务57.59% vs. 自我中心旋转13.49%），并通过基于强化学习的诊断研究证明该能力具有部分可塑性（7B模型从31.10%提升至60.06%），但提升具有任务选择性和奖励设计敏感性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MLLMs在静态视角下视觉感知能力强，但在视角变化条件下的空间推理能力严重不足。作者希望系统性地评估和诊断这一瓶颈，并探索其是否可通过优化手段缓解。

Method: 1) 构建PCSR-Bench基准：从26个室内环境的2,600张全景图中生成84,373个QA对，覆盖8个任务（基础感知+高级PCSR）；2) 评估14个代表性MLLMs；3) 对7B模型进行基于强化学习的诊断研究，探索该能力的可塑性及奖励设计影响。

Result: 基础相对方向准确率57.59%，但自我中心旋转仅13.49%，自我中心畸变7.13%，开放式组合推理仅0.64%。RL诊断中，7B基线从31.10%提升至60.06%，但增益具有任务选择性，对奖励权重分配和奖励公式敏感，且部分依赖评估协议。

Conclusion: PCSR是当前MLLMs的关键瓶颈，但并非完全不可改变——在有针对性的优化下存在有限但有意义的恢复空间。这一定位为后续研究提供了明确方向。

Original Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) show strong visual perception, yet remain limited in reasoning about space under changing viewpoints. We study this challenge as Perspective-Conditioned Spatial Reasoning (PCSR) in 360-degree omnidirectional images, where broad scene coverage reduces ambiguity from partial observations without eliminating the need for viewpoint-dependent inference. To assess this capability, we introduce PCSR-Bench, a diagnostic benchmark of 84,373 question-answer pairs from 2,600 omnidirectional images across 26 indoor environments. PCSR-Bench contains eight tasks spanning foundational perception (e.g., object counting, relative distance, and relative direction) and advanced PCSR, including compositional chains, egocentric rotation, perspective re-anchoring, ego-distortion, and limited-FOV visibility. We evaluate 14 representative MLLMs and observe a substantial perception-reasoning gap: accuracy reaches 57.59% on foundational relative direction, but drops to 13.49% on egocentric rotation, 7.13% on egocentric distortion, and 0.64% on open-ended compositional reasoning. To probe the plasticity of this gap, we conduct an RL-based diagnostic study on a 7B-scale model. Reward shaping improves a matched 7B baseline from 31.10% to 60.06% under a controlled setting, suggesting that PCSR is partial plasticity rather than being fully immutable. Still, the gains are task-selective, sensitive to reward design including both weight allocation and reward formulation, and partially dependent on the evaluation protocol. These results position PCSR as a key bottleneck in current MLLMs and highlight limited but meaningful room for recovery under targeted optimization.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）展现出强大的视觉感知能力，但在视角变化条件下的空间推理方面仍然受限。本文将这一挑战定义为360度全向图像中的视角条件空间推理（PCSR），其中广阔的场景覆盖减少了部分观测带来的模糊性，但并未消除视角依赖推理的需求。为评估该能力，我们引入了PCSR-Bench，一个诊断性基准，包含来自26个室内环境中2,600张全向图像的84,373个问答对。PCSR-Bench包含八项任务，涵盖基础感知（如物体计数、相对距离和相对方向）和高级PCSR，包括组合链、自我中心旋转、视角重新锚定、自我中心畸变以及有限视野可见性。我们评估了14个代表性MLLMs，观察到显著的感知-推理鸿沟：基础相对方向准确率达到57.59%，但自我中心旋转降至13.49%，自我中心畸变降至7.13%，开放式组合推理降至0.64%。为探究这一鸿沟的可塑性，我们对一个7B规模的模型进行了基于强化学习的诊断研究。奖励塑造在受控设置下将匹配的7B基线从31.10%提升至60.06%，表明PCSR具有部分可塑性而非完全不可改变。然而，增益具有任务选择性，对奖励设计（包括权重分配和奖励公式）敏感，且部分依赖评估协议。这些结果将PCSR定位为当前MLLMs的关键瓶颈，并揭示了在针对性优化下有限但有意义的恢复空间。

</details>


### [111] [AOI-SSL: Self-Supervised Framework for Efficient Segmentation of Wire-bonded Semiconductors In Optical Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12430)
*Joaquín Figueira,Rob Van Gastel,Giacomo D'Amicantonio,Zhuoran Liu,Ioan Gabriel Bucur,Faysal Boughorbel,Egor Bondarev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AOI-SSL框架，通过小领域自监督预训练视觉Transformer并结合上下文检索推理，实现线焊半导体自动光学检测的高效语义分割，显著减少标注需求并支持快速设备适配。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的线焊半导体分割模型通常针对特定设备训练，当新设备出现或数据分布偏移时需要重新训练，缺乏训练效率和泛化能力。

Method: 结合小领域自监督预训练（采用Masked Autoencoders）与上下文补丁级检索方法，直接从密集编码器嵌入预测掩码，无需大量额外训练。

Result: 1) Masked Autoencoders在小数据自监督预训练中最有效；2) 简单相似度检索与复杂注意力聚合方法性能相当；3) 自监督预训练显著优于从头训练和ImageNet预训练骨干；4) 基于检索的分割在单设备图像上优于微调，支持近即时适配。

Conclusion: AOI-SSL框架通过小领域自监督预训练和上下文检索推理，实现了高效、少标注、可快速适配的线焊半导体语义分割。

Original Abstract: Segmentation models in automated optical inspection of wire-bonded semiconductors are typically device-specific and must be re-trained when new devices or distribution shifts appear. We introduce AOI-SSL, a training-efficient framework for semantic segmentation of wire-bonded semiconductors by combining small-domain self-supervised pre-training of vision transformers with in-context inference that minimizes the need of labeled examples. We pre-train SOTA self-supervised algorithms in a small industrial inspection dataset and find that Masked Autoencoders are the most effective in this small-data setting, improving downstream segmentation while reducing the labeled fine-tuning effort. We further introduce in-context, patch-level retrieval methods that predict masks directly from dense encoder embeddings with negligible additional training. We show that, in this setting, simple similarity-based retrieval performs on par with more complex attention-based aggregation used currently in the literature. Furthermore, our experiments demonstrate that self-supervised pre-training significantly improves segmentation quality compared to training from scratch and to ImageNet pre-trained backbones under a fixed fine-tuning computational budget. Finally, the results reveal that retrieval based segmentation outperforms fine-tuning when targeting single device images, allowing for near-instant adaptation to difficult samples.

中文摘要翻译: 在自动光学检测中，线焊半导体的分割模型通常针对特定设备，当新设备出现或数据分布偏移时需要重新训练。本文提出AOI-SSL，一个训练高效的语义分割框架，通过将小领域自监督预训练的视觉Transformer与上下文推理相结合，最小化对标注样本的需求。我们在一个小型工业检测数据集上预训练了最先进的自监督算法，发现掩码自编码器在小数据场景下最为有效，能在减少标注微调工作量的同时提升下游分割性能。我们进一步引入了上下文补丁级检索方法，可直接从密集编码器嵌入预测掩码，且几乎无需额外训练。实验表明，在此设定下，简单的基于相似度的检索与当前文献中更复杂的基于注意力的聚合方法性能相当。此外，实验证明，在固定的微调计算预算下，自监督预训练相比从头训练和使用ImageNet预训练骨干显著提升了分割质量。最后，结果表明，针对单一设备图像，基于检索的分割优于微调，能够实现对困难样本的近即时适配。

</details>


### [112] [GaitProtector: Impersonation-Driven Gait De-Identification via Training-Free Diffusion Latent Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12431)
*Huiran Duan,Qian Zhou,Zhongliang Guo,Junhao Dong,Yuqi Li,Guoying Zhao,Yingli Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GaitProtector，一种基于模仿驱动的步态去识别框架，利用预训练3D扩散模型的先验知识，通过训练无关的隐代码优化实现步态隐私保护，在抑制身份信息的同时保持身体结构和运动动态的合理性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统步态去识别方法存在两难：要么身份抑制不充分，要么引入时空畸变影响下游应用。需一种既能有效抑制身份信息，又能保持步态结构合理性的方法。

Method: 提出GaitProtector框架，包含两个耦合目标：(i)混淆——将受保护的步态远离源身份；(ii)模仿——将其吸引至选定的目标身份。通过训练无关的扩散隐代码优化实现：将输入步态序列反转到预训练3D视频扩散模型的隐轨迹中，利用可微对抗目标迭代优化隐代码，合成受保护的步态。

Result: 在CASIA-B数据集上：黑盒步态识别下模仿成功率达56.7%，Rank-1识别准确率从89.6%降至15.0%；在Scoliosis1K数据集上，诊断准确率仅从91.4%降至74.2%。

Conclusion: 首个以训练无关方式利用预训练3D扩散先验进行基于轮廓的步态去识别的工作，有效平衡了隐私保护与结构合理性。

Original Abstract: Conventional gait de-identification methods often encounter an inherent trade-off: they either provide insufficient identity suppression or introduce spatiotemporal distortions that impede structure-sensitive downstream applications. We propose GaitProtector, an impersonation-driven gait de-identification framework that formulates privacy protection as a unified objective with two tightly coupled components: (i) obfuscation, which repels the protected gait from the source identity, and (ii) impersonation, which attracts it toward a selected target identity. The target identity serves as a semantic anchor that biases optimization toward structurally plausible gait patterns under the pretrained diffusion prior, helping preserve dominant body shape and motion dynamics. We instantiate this idea through a training-free diffusion latent optimization pipeline. Instead of retraining a generator for each dataset, we invert each input silhouette sequence into the latent trajectory of a pretrained 3D video diffusion model and iteratively optimize latent codes with a differentiable adversarial objective to synthesize protected gaits. Experiments on the CASIA-B dataset show that GaitProtector achieves a 56.7% impersonation success rate under black-box gait recognition and reduces Rank-1 identification accuracy from 89.6% to 15.0%, while maintaining favorable visual and temporal quality. We further evaluate downstream utility on the Scoliosis1K dataset, where diagnostic accuracy decreases only from 91.4% to 74.2%. To the best of our knowledge, this work is the first to leverage pretrained 3D diffusion priors in a training-free manner for silhouette-based gait de-identification.

中文摘要翻译: 传统的步态去识别方法常面临固有的权衡：要么身份抑制不充分，要么引入时空畸变从而阻碍对结构敏感的下游应用。我们提出GaitProtector，一种模仿驱动的步态去识别框架，将隐私保护建模为一个统一的目标，包含两个紧密耦合的组件：（i）混淆——将受保护的步态远离源身份；（ii）模仿——将其吸引至选定的目标身份。目标身份作为语义锚点，在预训练扩散先验的约束下，引导优化过程朝向结构合理的步态模式，从而帮助保留主要的身体形态和运动动态。我们通过一个无需训练的扩散隐代码优化流程来实现这一思想。无需为每个数据集重新训练生成器，我们将每个输入轮廓序列反转到预训练3D视频扩散模型的隐轨迹中，并通过可微对抗目标迭代优化隐代码，以合成受保护的步态。在CASIA-B数据集上的实验表明，GaitProtector在黑盒步态识别下实现了56.7%的模仿成功率，并将Rank-1识别准确率从89.6%降至15.0%，同时保持了良好的视觉和时间质量。我们进一步在Scoliosis1K数据集上评估了下游实用性，诊断准确率仅从91.4%下降至74.2%。据我们所知，本文是首个以无需训练的方式利用预训练3D扩散先验进行基于轮廓的步态去识别的工作。

</details>


### [113] [3D Gaussian Splatting for Efficient Retrospective Dynamic Scene Novel View Synthesis with a Standardized Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12437)
*Yunxiao Zhang,Suryansh Kumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对同步多视角动态场景的高效回顾性新视角合成方法，无需显式时间变形约束，并配套了一个基于Blender的动态多视角数据集生成框架，用于可复现的NeRF和3DGS研究。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在同步多视角设置下，每个时间步的动态场景已经受到强烈的几何约束（多视角一致性），因此显式的时间耦合或多体约束对于回顾性NVS是不必要的。作者旨在证明在同步MV场景中，无需时间变形约束即可实现高效的动态场景NVS。

Method: 通过SfM在起始时刻初始化点云，然后将优化的3D高斯随时间传播，从而在不施加时间变形约束的情况下实现回顾性NVS。同时提出了一个基于Blender的动态多视图数据集框架，生成高质量同步相机阵列和标准化训练数据。

Result: 实验表明，在同步多视角设置下，无需时间变形约束即可实现高效的回顾性动态场景NVS；所提出的数据集框架能够消除数据不一致性，支持可复现的基准测试。

Conclusion: 在同步多视角设置下，使用3DGS可以不依赖显式时间耦合实现高效的回顾性动态NVS；同时数据集框架为动态NVS方法提供了可复现、原则性的基准测试能力。

Original Abstract: Retrospective novel view synthesis (NVS) of dynamic scenes is fundamental to applications such as sports. Recent dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) approaches introduce temporally coupled formulations to enforce motion coherence across time. In this paper, we argue that, in a synchronized multi-view (MV) setting typical of sports, the dynamic scene at each time step is already strongly geometrically constrained. We posit that the availability of calibrated, synchronized viewpoints provides sufficient spatial consistency, and therefore, explicit temporal coupling, or complex multi-body constraints seems unnecessary for retrospective NVS. To this end, we propose an approach tailored for synchronized MV dynamic scene. By initializing the SfM-derived point cloud at the start time and propagating optimized Gaussians over time, we show that efficient retrospective NVS can be achieved without imposing a temporal deformation constraint. Complementing our methodological contribution, we introduce a Dynamic MV dataset framework built on Blender for reproducible NeRF and 3DGS research. The framework generates high-quality, synchronized camera rigs and exports training-ready datasets in standard formats, eliminating inconsistencies in coordinate conventions and data pipelines. Using the framework, we construct a dynamic benchmark suite and evaluate representative NeRF and 3DGS approaches under controlled conditions. Together, we show that, under a synchronized MV setup, efficient retrospective dynamic scene NVS can be achieved using 3DGS. At the same time, the dataset-generation framework enables reproducible and principled benchmarking of dynamic NVS methods.

中文摘要翻译: 动态场景的回顾性新视角合成是体育等应用的基础。最近的动态3D高斯泼溅方法引入了时间耦合公式来强制跨时间的运动一致性。在本文中，我们认为，在体育场景中典型的同步多视角设置下，每个时间步的动态场景已经受到强烈的几何约束。我们提出，经过标定和同步的多视角相机提供了足够的空间一致性，因此显式的时间耦合或复杂的多体约束对于回顾性NVS似乎是不必要的。为此，我们提出了一种针对同步多视角动态场景的方法。通过在起始时刻初始化SfM导出的点云，并将优化的高斯随时间传播，我们证明了无需施加时间变形约束即可实现高效的回顾性NVS。作为方法贡献的补充，我们引入了一个基于Blender构建的动态多视图数据集框架，用于可复现的NeRF和3DGS研究。该框架生成高质量、同步的相机阵列，并以标准格式导出训练就绪的数据集，消除了坐标惯例和数据流程中的不一致性。利用该框架，我们构建了一个动态基准套件，并在受控条件下评估了代表性的NeRF和3DGS方法。总之，我们证明了在同步多视角设置下，使用3DGS可以实现高效的回顾性动态场景NVS。同时，该数据集生成框架为动态NVS方法提供了可复现和原则性的基准测试能力。

</details>


### [114] [LychSim: A Controllable and Interactive Simulation Framework for Vision Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12449)
*Wufei Ma,Chloe Wang,Siyi Chen,Jiawei Peng,Patrick Li,Alan Yuille*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LychSim 是一个基于 Unreal Engine 5 构建的高可控、可交互仿真框架，通过简洁的 Python API、程序化数据管道和 MCP 协议集成，降低了仿真平台的使用门槛，支持闭环优化、OOD 评估、合成数据生成、强化学习对抗测试和语言驱动的场景布局生成等多种下游应用。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有仿真平台技术门槛高，需要计算机图形学和游戏开发的广泛专业知识，限制了视觉社区对仿真工具的利用；同时，自监督预训练虽然减少了对合成数据的依赖，但仿真在闭环优化和 OOD 评估中仍不可替代。

Method: (1) 简洁的 Python API 封装底层引擎复杂性；(2) 程序化数据管道生成多样化、高保真环境及丰富的 2D/3D 标注；(3) 原生集成 MCP（Model Context Protocol），使仿真器成为推理型 LLM 的动态闭环试验场；(4) 场景级程序化规则和对象级位姿对齐，实现语义对齐的 3D 标注和自动化场景修改。

Result: LychSim 在合成数据引擎、强化学习对抗检测器、交互式语言驱动场景布局生成等多个下游应用中验证了其能力。

Conclusion: LychSim 通过降低仿真技术门槛并提供丰富的可控性和交互性，为视觉社区提供了一款强大的仿真工具，将公开发布完整源代码和多种数据标注以回馈社区。

Original Abstract: While self-supervised pretraining has reduced vision systems' reliance on synthetic data, simulation remains an indispensable tool for closed-loop optimization and rigorous out-of-distribution (OOD) evaluation. However, modern simulation platforms often present steep technical barriers, requiring extensive expertise in computer graphics and game development. In this work, we present LychSim, a highly controllable and interactive simulation framework built upon Unreal Engine 5 to bridge this gap. LychSim is built around three key designs: (1) a streamlined Python API that abstracts away underlying engine complexities; (2) a procedural data pipeline capable of generating diverse, high-fidelity environments with varying out-of-distribution (OOD) visual challenges, paired with rich 2D and 3D ground truths; and (3) a native integration of the Model Context Protocol (MCP) that transforms the simulator into a dynamic, closed-loop playground for reasoning agentic LLMs. We further annotate scene-level procedural rules and object-level pose alignments to enable semantically aligned 3D ground truths and automated scene modification. We demonstrate LychSim's capability across multiple downstream applications, including serving as a synthetic data engine, powering reinforcement learning-based adversarial examiners, and facilitating interactive, language-driven scene layout generation. To benefit the broader vision community, LychSim will be made publicly available, including full source code and various data annotations.

中文摘要翻译: 虽然自监督预训练减少了视觉系统对合成数据的依赖，但仿真仍然是闭环优化和严格的分布外（OOD）评估中不可或缺的工具。然而，现代仿真平台通常存在较高的技术门槛，需要计算机图形学和游戏开发方面的广泛专业知识。在本工作中，我们提出 LychSim，一个基于 Unreal Engine 5 构建的高可控、可交互的仿真框架，以弥合这一差距。LychSim 围绕三个关键设计构建：（1）简洁的 Python API，抽象底层引擎的复杂性；（2）程序化数据管道，能够生成多样化、高保真环境，并具有各种分布外（OOD）视觉挑战，同时提供丰富的 2D 和 3D 真值标注；（3）原生集成模型上下文协议（MCP），将仿真器转变为用于推理型智能 LLM 的动态闭环实验平台。我们进一步标注了场景级程序化规则和对象级位姿对齐，以实现语义对齐的 3D 真值标注和自动化场景修改。我们在多个下游应用中展示了 LychSim 的能力，包括作为合成数据引擎、支持基于强化学习的对抗检测器，以及促进交互式、语言驱动的场景布局生成。为了惠及更广泛的视觉社区，LychSim 将公开发布，包括完整的源代码和多种数据标注。

</details>


### [115] [FuTCR: Future-Targeted Contrast and Repulsion for Continual Panoptic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12451)
*Nicholas Ikechukwu,Keanu Nichols,Deepti Ghadiyaram,Bryan A. Plummer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Future-Targeted Contrastive and Repulsive (FuTCR)框架，通过提前重构特征表示空间，解决持续全景分割中未标注背景对象对后续新类学习的干扰问题，在新类分割质量上最高提升28%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在持续全景分割任务中，训练图像包含大量未标注对象，现有方法将这些对象统一归为"背景"类，反复告诉模型所有背景类别都是相同的，导致模型忽略了不同背景类别之间的差异信息。当后续引入新类别时，模型难以从之前被忽视的背景信息中学习，造成新类分割性能不佳。

Method: FuTCR框架包含两个核心步骤：(1) 发现可靠的类未来区域：通过分组模型预测的掩码，找到那些像素被一致分类为背景但具有非背景logits的区域；(2) 像素到区域对比与排斥：从未标注区域中构建连贯的原型进行对比学习，同时将背景特征从已知类原型中推开，显式地为未来类别预留表征空间。

Result: 在六种CPS设置和多种数据集规模上的实验表明，FuTCR相比现有最优方法，新类全景质量相对提升最高达28%，同时基础类性能保持或提升最高达4%。

Conclusion: 通过在新类引入之前主动重构特征表示空间，FuTCR有效缓解了背景类别混淆对新类学习的负面影响，实现了持续全景分割任务中新类与基础类性能的双重提升。

Original Abstract: Continual Panoptic Segmentation (CPS) requires methods that can quickly adapt to new categories over time. The nature of this dense prediction task means that training images may contain a mix of labeled and unlabeled objects. As nothing is known about these unlabeled objects a priori, existing methods often simply group any unlabeled pixel into a single "background" class during training. In effect, during training, they repeatedly tell the model that all the different background categories are the same (even when they aren't). This makes learning to identify different background categories as they are added challenging since these new categories may require using information the model was previously told was unimportant and ignored. Thus, we propose a Future-Targeted Contrastive and Repulsive (FuTCR) framework that addresses this limitation by restructuring representations before new classes are introduced. FuTCR first discovers confident future-like regions by grouping model-predicted masks whose pixels are consistently classified as background but exhibit non-background logits. Next, FuTCR applies pixel-to-region contrast to build coherent prototypes from these unlabeled regions, while simultaneously repelling background features away from known-class prototypes to explicitly reserve representational space for future categories. Experiments across six CPS settings and a range of dataset sizes show FuTCR improves relative new-class panoptic quality over the state-of-the-art by up to 28%, while preserving or improving base-class performance with gains up to 4%.

中文摘要翻译: 持续全景分割要求方法能够随时间快速适应新类别。由于该密集预测任务的特性，训练图像可能包含标注对象和未标注对象的混合体。由于这些未标注对象先验未知，现有方法通常会在训练期间将所有未标注像素简单地归为单一的"背景"类。实际上，它们在训练中反复告诉模型所有不同的背景类别都是相同的（即使它们并不相同）。这使得在后续添加新类别时，模型难以学会识别不同的背景类别，因为这些新类别可能需要利用模型之前被告知不重要并被忽略的信息。为此，我们提出了面向未来的对比与排斥（FuTCR）框架，通过在新类别引入之前重构特征表示来解决这一局限性。FuTCR首先通过分组模型预测的掩码来发现可靠的类未来区域，这些掩码中的像素被一致分类为背景但表现出非背景的逻辑值。接着，FuTCR应用像素到区域的对比学习，从这些未标注区域中构建连贯的原型，同时将背景特征从已知类原型中排斥开，从而显式地为未来类别预留表征空间。在六种CPS设置和多种数据集规模上的实验表明，FuTCR相对于现有最优方法，新类全景质量相对提升最高达28%，同时基础类性能保持或提升最高达4%。

</details>


### [116] [Elastic Attention Cores for Scalable Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12491)
*Alan Z. Song,Yinjie Chen,Mu Nan,Rui Zhang,Jiahang Cao,Weijian Mai,Muquan Yu,Hossein Adeli,Deva Ramanan,Michael J. Tarr,Andrew F. Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VECA提出了一种基于核心-外围结构的线性复杂度视觉Transformer，通过一组可学习的核心令牌作为通信接口，在不进行直接补丁间交互的情况下实现了与SOTA视觉基础模型竞争的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统ViT采用全对全自注意力机制，其计算复杂度随图像分辨率呈二次方增长，限制了ViT在高分辨率场景中的应用。本文质疑了"成对令牌交互对学习丰富视觉-语义表示是必要的"这一假设。

Method: 提出VECA（Visual Elastic Core Attention）架构，引入少量可学习的核心令牌（core tokens）作为所有图像补丁令牌的通信接口，补丁令牌仅通过核心令牌间接交换信息，实现了O(N)线性复杂度；采用沿核心轴嵌套训练策略，支持推理时弹性地权衡计算量与精度。

Result: 在分类和密集预测任务上，VECA达到了与最新视觉基础模型竞争的性能，同时显著降低了计算成本。

Conclusion: 弹性核心-外围注意力机制可成为视觉Transformer的可扩展替代构建模块，验证了无需直接补丁间交互也能学习有效视觉表示。

Original Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) achieve strong data-driven scaling by leveraging all-to-all self-attention. However, this flexibility incurs a computational cost that scales quadratically with image resolution, limiting ViTs in high-resolution domains. Underlying this approach is the assumption that pairwise token interactions are necessary for learning rich visual-semantic representations. In this work, we challenge this assumption, demonstrating that effective visual representations can be learned without any direct patch-to-patch interaction. We propose VECA (Visual Elastic Core Attention), a vision transformer architecture that uses efficient linear-time core-periphery structured attention enabled by a small set of learned cores. In VECA, these cores act as a communication interface: patch tokens exchange information exclusively through the core tokens, which are initialized from scratch and propagated across layers. Because the $N$ image patches only directly interact with a resolution invariant set of $C$ learned "core" embeddings, this yields linear complexity $O(N)$ for predetermined $C$, which bypasses quadratic scaling. Compared to prior cross-attention architectures, VECA maintains and iteratively updates the full set of $N$ input tokens, avoiding a small $C$-way bottleneck. Combined with nested training along the core axis, our model can elastically trade off compute and accuracy during inference. Across classification and dense tasks, VECA achieves performance competitive with the latest vision foundation models while reducing computational cost. Our results establish elastic core-periphery attention as a scalable alternative building block for Vision Transformers.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉Transformer（ViT）通过利用全对全自注意力机制实现了强大的数据驱动扩展。然而，这种灵活性带来了与图像分辨率呈二次方增长的计算成本，限制了ViT在高分辨率领域的应用。该方法背后的假设是：成对令牌交互对于学习丰富的视觉-语义表示是必要的。在这项工作中，我们挑战了这一假设，证明了无需任何直接的补丁间交互也可以学习到有效的视觉表示。我们提出了VECA（Visual Elastic Core Attention），一种视觉Transformer架构，它通过一组可学习的核心令牌实现高效的线性时间核心-外围结构化注意力。在VECA中，这些核心令牌充当通信接口：图像补丁令牌仅通过核心令牌交换信息，核心令牌从零开始初始化并在层间传播。由于N个图像补丁仅与分辨率无关的C个可学习"核心"嵌入直接交互，这产生了预定C下的线性复杂度O(N)，绕过了二次方扩展问题。与先前的交叉注意力架构相比，VECA维护并迭代更新全部N个输入令牌，避免了小C路瓶颈。结合沿核心轴的嵌套训练，我们的模型可以在推理时弹性地权衡计算量与精度。在分类和密集任务上，VECA在降低计算成本的同时达到了与最新视觉基础模型竞争的性能。我们的实验结果确立了弹性核心-外围注意力作为视觉Transformer的可扩展替代构建模块。

</details>


### [117] [Revisiting Photometric Ambiguity for Accurate Gaussian-Splatting Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12494)
*Jiahe Li,Jiawei Zhang,Xiao Bai,Jin Zheng,Xiaohan Yu,Lin Gu,Gim Hee Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AmbiSuR提出了一种基于高斯溅射的框架，通过内在的光度歧义自指示和消解机制，实现了鲁棒且高性能的3D表面重建。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于可微渲染的表面重建方法受限于普遍存在的光度歧义问题，导致几何解不明确，性能遭遇瓶颈。

Method: 首先回溯高斯溅射表示的基础，发现其中内置的逐基元歧义及其自指示潜力；进而提出光度消歧模块来约束病态几何解以形成明确的表面；最后设计歧义指示模块，利用自指示能力识别并引导纠正欠约束的重建。

Result: 在多种具有挑战性的场景下，AmbiSuR相比现有方法实现了更优的表面重建质量，并展现出广泛的兼容性。

Conclusion: 通过挖掘高斯溅射内在的歧义自指示能力，AmbiSuR有效解决了光度歧义问题，为鲁棒的高性能3D表面重建提供了新的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Surface reconstruction with differentiable rendering has achieved impressive performance in recent years, yet the pervasive photometric ambiguities have strictly bottlenecked existing approaches. This paper presents AmbiSuR, a framework that explores an intrinsic solution upon Gaussian Splatting for the photometric ambiguity-robust surface 3D reconstruction with high performance. Starting by revisiting the foundation, our investigation uncovers two built-in primitive-wise ambiguities in representation, while revealing an intrinsic potential for ambiguity self-indication in Gaussian Splatting. Stemming from these, a photometric disambiguation is first introduced, constraining ill-posed geometry solution for definite surface formation. Then, we propose an ambiguity indication module that unleashes the self-indication potential to identify and further guide correcting underconstrained reconstructions. Extensive experiments demonstrate our superior surface reconstructions compared to existing methods across various challenging scenarios, excelling in broad compatibility. Project: https://fictionarry.github.io/AmbiSuR-Proj/ .

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，基于可微渲染的表面重建取得了令人瞩目的性能，然而普遍存在的光度歧义问题严重制约了现有方法的发展。本文提出AmbiSuR，一个在高斯溅射框架上探索内在解决方案的框架，旨在实现对抗光度歧义的高性能3D表面重建。通过从基础入手进行重新审视，我们的研究揭示了表示中两种内置的逐基元歧义，同时发现了高斯溅射中歧义自指示的内在潜力。基于此，我们首先引入光度消歧模块，约束病态的几何解以形成明确的表面。随后，我们提出一个歧义指示模块，释放自指示潜力以识别并进一步引导纠正欠约束的重建。大量实验表明，在多种具有挑战性的场景下，我们的表面重建优于现有方法，并展现出广泛的兼容性。

</details>


### [118] [AlphaGRPO: Unlocking Self-Reflective Multimodal Generation in UMMs via Decompositional Verifiable Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12495)
*Runhui Huang,Jie Wu,Rui Yang,Zhe Liu,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AlphaGRPO 将 GRPO 应用于 AR-Diffusion 统一多模态模型，无需冷启动阶段即可增强多模态生成能力；通过分解式可验证奖励（DVReward）提供稳定监督，实现推理式文本到图像生成和自我反思式精炼，在多个基准上取得显著提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型在生成任务中存在隐含用户意图推理不足、输出结果与用户需求对齐不佳的问题；同时，在实际多模态生成场景中缺乏稳定的监督信号来引导模型自我改进。

Method: 提出 AlphaGRPO 框架，核心包括：(1) 将 GRPO 应用于 AR-Diffusion 统一多模态模型，无需冷启动阶段；(2) 提出分解式可验证奖励（DVReward），利用 LLM 将复杂用户请求分解为原子化、可验证的语义与质量子问题，再通过通用 MLLM 评估提供可靠且可解释的反馈。

Result: 在 GenEval, TIIF-Bench, DPG-Bench, WISE 等多模态生成基准上取得稳健提升；在未见编辑任务训练的 GEdit 编辑任务上也实现了显著改进。

Conclusion: 自我反思式的强化学习方法能够有效利用模型内在的理解能力来指导高保真度的生成，验证了所提框架的有效性和泛化能力。

Original Abstract: In this paper, we propose AlphaGRPO, a novel framework that applies Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to AR-Diffusion Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) to enhance multimodal generation capabilities without an additional cold-start stage. Our approach unlocks the model's intrinsic potential to perform advanced reasoning tasks: Reasoning Text-to-Image Generation, where the model actively infers implicit user intents, and Self-Reflective Refinement, where it autonomously diagnoses and corrects misalignments in generated outputs. To address the challenge of providing stable supervision for real-world multimodal generation, we introduce the Decompositional Verifiable Reward (DVReward). Unlike holistic scalar rewards, DVReward utilizes an LLM to decompose complex user requests into atomic, verifiable semantic and quality questions, which are then evaluated by a general MLLM to provide reliable and interpretable feedback. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AlphaGRPO yields robust improvements across multimodal generation benchmarks, including GenEval, TIIF-Bench, DPG-Bench and WISE, while also achieving significant gains in editing tasks on GEdit without training on editing tasks. These results validate that our self-reflective reinforcement approach effectively leverages inherent understanding to guide high-fidelity generation. Project page: https://huangrh99.github.io/AlphaGRPO/

中文摘要翻译: 在本文中，我们提出 AlphaGRPO，一种将组相对策略优化（GRPO）应用于 AR-Diffusion 统一多模态模型（UMMs）的新框架，无需额外的冷启动阶段即可增强多模态生成能力。我们的方法释放了模型执行高级推理任务的内在潜力：推理式文本到图像生成（模型主动推断隐含的用户意图）和自我反思式精炼（模型自主诊断并纠正生成输出中的不一致性）。为了应对实际多模态生成中提供稳定监督的挑战，我们引入了分解式可验证奖励（DVReward）。与整体标量奖励不同，DVReward 利用大语言模型将复杂用户请求分解为原子化、可验证的语义与质量子问题，随后通过通用多模态大语言模型进行评估，以提供可靠且可解释的反馈。大量实验表明，AlphaGRPO 在 GenEval、TIIF-Bench、DPG-Bench 和 WISE 等多模态生成基准上取得了稳健的提升，同时在未经过编辑任务训练的情况下，在 GEdit 编辑任务上也取得了显著改进。这些结果验证了我们的自我反思式强化方法能够有效利用模型内在的理解能力来指导高保真度的生成。项目页面：https://huangrh99.github.io/AlphaGRPO/
