TL;DR: 提出TRACE，一种自回归脑电预训练框架，通过跨通道时序路由实现时域自适应和跨通道一致性计算，在多个下游基准任务上取得最优或竞争性结果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: EEG信号具有多通道和非平稳特性，现有架构（如统一时序计算或独立处理每个通道补丁）无法匹配其通道耦合与时域动态变化的结构特征。

Method: TRACE采用自回归方式从因果上下文预测未来EEG补丁，每个时间步从因果跨通道历史中导出专家路由决策，并联合应用于该步所有通道，从而保持瞬时跨通道相干性并允许不同时域激活不同计算路径。

Result: 在8个下游EEG基准上评估，涵盖预训练可见和完全未见数据集两种场景，在多个基准上取得最佳结果，在运动想象和临床事件分类任务上保持竞争力，消融实验支持跨通道时序路由的重要性。

Conclusion: TRACE通过跨通道时序路由机制有效建模EEG的多通道耦合和非平稳动态特性，且兼容跨语料库异构预训练，是一种有效的脑电表征学习框架。

Original Abstract: Learning transferable representations for electroencephalography (EEG) remains challenging because EEG signals are inherently multi-channel and non-stationary. Channels observed at the same time provide coupled measurements of neural activity, while the relevant temporal dynamics vary across contexts. This structure is poorly matched by architectures that apply uniform computation across time or route each channel patch independently. To this end, we propose TRACE, an autoregressive EEG pre-training framework that predicts future EEG patches from causal context while performing temporally adaptive and cross-channel coherent computation. At each temporal step, TRACE derives an expert routing decision from the causal cross-channel history and applies it jointly to all channels at that step. This preserves instantaneous cross-channel coherence while allowing different temporal regimes to activate different computation. Since routing is defined over the available channel set and causal temporal context, TRACE is compatible with heterogeneous pre-training across corpora with different channel counts, montages, sequence lengths, and recording domains. Across eight downstream EEG benchmarks, TRACE is evaluated in both settings: when downstream domains are seen only as unlabeled pre-training data and when downstream datasets are completely unseen during pre-training. It obtains the best results on several benchmarks while remaining competitive on motor imagery and clinical event classification tasks, with ablations supporting the importance of cross-channel temporal routing.

中文摘要翻译: 学习脑电图（EEG）的可迁移表征仍然具有挑战性，因为EEG信号本质上是多通道且非平稳的。同一时刻观测到的通道提供神经活动的耦合测量，而相关的时域动态在不同上下文间变化。这种结构与那些对时间施加统一计算或独立路由每个通道补丁的架构不匹配。为此，我们提出TRACE，一种自回归EEG预训练框架，从因果上下文预测未来EEG补丁，同时执行时域自适应和跨通道相干计算。在每个时间步，TRACE从因果跨通道历史中导出专家路由决策，并联合应用于该步所有通道。这既保留了瞬时跨通道相干性，又允许不同时域模式激活不同的计算路径。由于路由是在可用通道集和因果时序上下文上定义的，TRACE兼容跨语料库的异构预训练，这些语料库具有不同的通道数量、导联配置、序列长度和记录域。在八个下游EEG基准上，TRACE在两种设置下进行评估：下游域仅作为未标注预训练数据出现，以及下游数据集在预训练期间完全不可见。它在多个基准上取得了最佳结果，同时在运动想象和临床事件分类任务上保持竞争力，消融实验支持了跨通道时序路由的重要性。

</details>


### [183] [Behavioral Mode Discovery for Fine-tuning Multimodal Generative Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11387)
*Alberta Longhini,David Emukpere,Jean-Michel Renders,Seungsu Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无监督模式发现框架，在强化学习微调生成式策略时，利用互信息作为内在奖励，在提升任务成功率的同时保留行为多样性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有RL微调生成式策略（如扩散策略）的方法虽然提升了任务表现，但常常将多样行为坍缩到单一奖励最大化模式，丢失了动作分布的多模态性。需要一种既能提升任务成功率又能保持行为多样性的方法。

Method: 提出无监督模式发现框架，首先从生成式策略中挖掘潜在的隐式行为模式，然后利用这些模式计算互信息作为内在奖励，对RL微调过程进行正则化，在优化任务奖励的同时鼓励行为多样性。

Result: 在机器人操作任务上的实验表明，该方法在任务成功率和行为多样性两方面均优于传统微调方法，能够获得更高的成功率和更丰富的多模态动作分布。

Conclusion: 无监督模式发现结合互信息内在奖励能有效解决RL微调中生成式策略模式坍缩问题，实现任务性能与行为多样性的双赢。

Original Abstract: We address the problem of fine-tuning pre-trained generative policies with reinforcement learning (RL) while preserving the multimodality of their action distributions. Existing methods for RL fine-tuning of generative policies (e.g., diffusion policies) improve task performance but often collapse diverse behaviors into a single reward-maximizing mode. To mitigate this issue, we propose an unsupervised mode discovery framework that uncovers latent behavioral modes within generative policies. The discovered modes enable the use of mutual information as an intrinsic reward, regularizing RL fine-tuning to enhance task success while maintaining behavioral diversity. Experiments on robotic manipulation tasks demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms conventional fine-tuning approaches, achieving higher success rates and preserving richer multimodal action distributions.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了在保持预训练生成式策略动作分布多模态性的同时，使用强化学习（RL）对其进行微调的问题。现有的生成式策略（如扩散策略）RL微调方法虽然提升了任务性能，但常常将多样化的行为坍缩为单一的奖励最大化模式。为缓解该问题，我们提出了一种无监督模式发现框架，用于挖掘生成式策略中潜在的隐式行为模式。所发现的模式使互信息能够作为内在奖励使用，从而正则化RL微调过程，在提升任务成功率的同时保持行为多样性。在机器人操作任务上的实验表明，我们的方法在成功率上持续优于传统微调方法，并且保留了更丰富的多模态动作分布。

</details>


### [184] [MuonQ: Enhancing Low-Bit Muon Quantization via Directional Fidelity Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11396)
*Yupeng Su,Ruijie Zhang,Ziyue Liu,Yequan Zhao,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MuonQ提出了一种面向方向保真度优化的低比特Muon训练框架，通过预量化归一化、结构分解和μ律压扩量化三种技术，实现了Muon优化器状态的4比特量化，在保持全精度性能的同时，将优化器状态内存降低了最多7.3倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Muon优化器通过梯度正交化实现了比Adam更优的计算效率，但其优化器状态对量化误差高度敏感：正交化丢弃了奇异值大小、只保留方向信息，导致奇异向量方向的微小量化误差在更新中被放大。因此需要专门设计低比特量化方案来压缩Muon的优化器状态。

Method: 1) 预量化归一化：使每一步引入的量化误差大小一致，防止累积误差产生偏好方向；2) 结构分解：通过幂迭代分离量化主导奇异分量，确保量化误差仅扰动奇异值大小而非旋转奇异向量方向；3) μ律压扩量化：为密集动量值分配更高分辨率，将量化目标从离群值保留转为密集区域可区分性。

Result: 在GPT风格和LLaMA风格模型上的预训练实验表明，MuonQ在4比特精度下与全精度Muon的训练损失和下游任务准确度高度接近，同时优化器状态内存减少了高达7.3倍。

Conclusion: MuonQ通过方向保真度优化的三项技术，实现了Muon优化器状态的稳定4比特量化，大幅降低内存开销而不牺牲模型性能。

Original Abstract: The Muon optimizer has emerged as a compelling alternative to Adam for training large language models, achieving remarkable computational savings through gradient orthogonalization. However, Muon's optimizer state is more sensitive to quantization errors: because the orthogonalization discards the magnitudes of singular values and retains only directional information, even small quantization errors in singular vector directions are amplified in the update. In this work, we propose MuonQ, a low-bit Muon training framework built on the principle of directional fidelity optimization. First, we apply a pre-quantization normalization so that each step introduces quantization errors of the same magnitude, preventing the accumulated error from developing a preferred direction. Second, we introduce a structural decomposition that separately quantizes the dominant singular components via power iteration, ensuring that quantization errors perturb only singular value magnitudes rather than rotating singular vector directions. Third, we adopt $μ$-law companding quantization to allocate higher resolution to densely packed momentum values, shifting the quantization objective from outlier preservation to dense-region distinguishability. Together, these techniques enable stable 4-bit quantization of Muon's optimizer states. Pre-training experiments on GPT-style and LLaMA-style models demonstrate that MuonQ at 4-bit precision closely matches full-precision Muon in both training loss and downstream task accuracy, while reducing optimizer state memory by up to 7.3 $\times$. Our code is available at https://github.com/YupengSu/MuonQ.

中文摘要翻译: Muon优化器已成为训练大型语言模型中Adam的一种引人注目的替代方案，通过梯度正交化实现了显著的计算节省。然而，Muon的优化器状态对量化误差更为敏感：由于正交化丢弃了奇异值的大小、仅保留方向信息，奇异向量方向上的微小量化误差也会在更新中被放大。在这项工作中，我们提出了MuonQ，一个基于方向保真度优化原则构建的低比特Muon训练框架。首先，我们应用预量化归一化，使每一步引入的量化误差大小一致，防止累积误差形成偏好方向。其次，我们引入结构分解，通过幂迭代单独量化主导奇异分量，确保量化误差仅扰动奇异值大小，而非旋转奇异向量方向。第三，我们采用μ律压扩量化，为密集分布的动量值分配更高的分辨率，将量化目标从离群值保留转变为密集区域可区分性。这些技术共同实现了Muon优化器状态的稳定4比特量化。在GPT风格和LLaMA风格模型上的预训练实验表明，4比特精度的MuonQ在训练损失和下游任务准确度上均与全精度Muon高度接近，同时优化器状态内存减少了高达7.3倍。我们的代码已在https://github.com/YupengSu/MuonQ开源。

</details>


### [185] [More Than Meets the Eye: A Semantics-Aware Traffic Augmentation Framework for Generalizable Website Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11402)
*Youquan Xian,Xueying Zeng,Lingjia Meng,Lei Cui,Runhan Song,Wei Wang,Zhengquan Ding,Peng Liu,Zhiyu Hao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对深度学习网站指纹识别在真实环境下因应用层资源组成变化和跨层封装导致特征不稳定而泛化性差的问题，提出语义感知流量增强框架SATA，通过协议级语义增强和跨层特征对齐，显著提升模型在复杂场景下的性能，开放世界下ACC提升90.81%，AUROC提升48.37%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的网站指纹识别方法在封闭世界数据集上表现良好，但在真实环境中（尤其是地理和时间变化下）泛化能力差。其根本原因是应用层资源组成可变性和跨层封装导致的可观测特征不稳定性相互耦合，使得底层应用语义与可观测流量特征之间存在系统性偏移。

Method: 提出SATA框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 基于协议规则的应用层语义增强，扩展每个流中的资源组成模式和协议约束下的帧序列模式；(2) 通过知识蒸馏实现跨层特征对齐，将增强后的帧序列与数据包长度序列特征进行对齐，实现增强语义与可观测序列之间的跨层特征对齐。

Result: SATA成功生成了训练集中不存在但测试集中真实存在的流量模式，显著提升了主流模型在多样复杂场景下的性能。在开放世界设置中，ACC提升90.81%，AUROC提升48.37%。

Conclusion: 语义感知流量增强是解决网站指纹识别在真实环境下的泛化性问题的有效途径，通过应用层语义增强与跨层特征对齐，能够弥补训练集与测试集之间的分布差异，提升模型在实际部署中的鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: Deep learning-based website fingerprinting has emerged as an effective technique for inferring the websites users visit. Although existing methods achieve strong performance on closed-world datasets, they often fail to generalize to real-world environments, especially under geographic and temporal shifts. This limitation fundamentally stems from the coupled effects of two key challenges: application-layer resource composition variability and observable feature instability induced by cross-layer encapsulation. Intertwined, these factors induce systematic shifts between underlying application semantics and observable traffic features. To address the above challenges, we propose SATA , a semantics-aware traffic augmentation framework. Specifically, SATA first performs application-layer semantic augmentation based on protocol rules, expanding the resource composition patterns within each flow and frame sequence patterns under protocol constraints. Based on these augmented frame sequences, we further introduce a cross-layer feature alignment mechanism via knowledge distillation. It aligns frame sequence with packet-length sequence features, enabling cross-layer feature alignment between enhanced semantics and observable sequences. Extensive experiments show that SATA successfully generates traffic patterns that are absent from the training set but genuinely exist in the test set, and significantly improves the performance of mainstream models across diverse and complex scenarios. In particular, in open-world settings, SATA improves ACC by 90.81% and AUROC by 48.37%. The source code of the prototype system is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SATA-B6C2/.

中文摘要翻译: 基于深度学习的网站指纹识别已成为推断用户访问网站的有效技术。尽管现有方法在封闭世界数据集上取得了优异性能，但在真实环境中（尤其是地理和时间变化下）往往难以泛化。这一限制根本上源于两个关键挑战的耦合效应：应用层资源组成可变性以及跨层封装导致的可观测特征不稳定性。这些因素相互交织，导致底层应用语义与可观测流量特征之间存在系统性偏移。为解决上述挑战，我们提出了SATA，一种语义感知的流量增强框架。具体而言，SATA首先基于协议规则进行应用层语义增强，扩展每个流中的资源组成模式以及协议约束下的帧序列模式。基于这些增强后的帧序列，我们进一步通过知识蒸馏引入跨层特征对齐机制。该机制将帧序列与数据包长度序列特征进行对齐，从而实现增强语义与可观测序列之间的跨层特征对齐。大量实验表明，SATA成功生成了训练集中不存在但在测试集中真实存在的流量模式，并在多样化的复杂场景下显著提升了主流模型的性能。特别是在开放世界设置中，SATA将ACC提升了90.81%，AUROC提升了48.37%。原型系统的源代码可在https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SATA-B6C2/获取。

</details>


### [186] [fg-expo: Frontier-guided exploration-prioritized policy optimization via adaptive kl and gaussian curriculum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11403)
*Mingxiong Lin,Zhangquan Gong,Maowen Tang,Qian Li,Chuangchuang Wang,Jian Ma,Sutian Huang,Kai Tang,Haonan Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FG-ExPO，通过自适应KL缩放（AKL）和高斯课程采样（GCS）两处轻量改进，解决了GRPO在LLM数学推理中固定KL系数和均匀采样导致的探索效率问题，在多个数学推理基准上显著提升性能，尤其在pass@32指标上表现突出。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: GRPO存在两个被忽视的低效问题：一是固定KL系数限制了模型在需要大幅偏离参考策略时的探索空间；二是均匀的问题采样忽略了中等难度问题能提供最具信息量的梯度信号这一事实。

Method: FG-ExPO包含两个轻量组件：(1) 精度条件KL缩放（AKL），通过批次平均精度的平滑非线性函数动态调整KL惩罚强度，模型表现差时放松约束，表现好时加强约束；(2) 高斯课程采样（GCS），以约0.5的中等精度水平为中心的高斯分布为问题分配采样权重，聚焦模型训练于其学习前沿。

Result: 在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B和Qwen3-8B-Base上评估六个数学推理基准，FG-ExPO一致优于原始GRPO。在AIME 2025 pass@32指标上绝对提升13.34（从63.33%到76.67%），8B模型平均pass@32提升2.66。pass@32的提升幅度远大于pass@1，验证了FG-ExPO在固定推理预算下扩大了模型的有效探索空间。

Conclusion: FG-ExPO通过两处简单有效的改进，显著提升了GRPO在LLM数学推理中的训练效率和性能，尤其在扩大模型探索空间方面效果显著。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become the standard paradigm for LLM mathematical reasoning, with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) serving as the dominant algorithm. We identify two overlooked inefficiencies inherent in GRPO. First, a fixed KL coefficient overly restricts policy exploration at moments when the model needs to diverge significantly from the reference policy. Second, uniform question sampling overlooks that moderately difficult problems produce the most informative gradient signals. We propose FG-ExPO, short for Frontier-Guided Exploration-Prioritized Policy Optimization, which integrates two lightweight components. Accuracy-Conditioned KL Scaling (AKL) adjusts the KL penalty strength through a smooth nonlinear function of batch average accuracy, loosening the constraint when the model performs poorly and strengthening it when the model achieves satisfactory results. Gaussian Curriculum Sampling (GCS) assigns sampling weights to questions following a Gaussian distribution centered at a moderate accuracy level around 0.5, focusing model training on its learning frontier. We conduct evaluations on DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B and Qwen3-8B-Base across six mainstream mathematical reasoning benchmarks. Experimental results demonstrate that FG-ExPO consistently outperforms vanilla GRPO. It delivers an absolute improvement of 13.34 on the AIME 2025 pass@32 metric, rising from 63.33 percent to 76.67 percent, and obtains an average pass@32 gain of 2.66 on the 8B model. The substantially larger performance gains observed on pass@32 compared to pass@1 verify that FG-ExPO enlarges the model's effective exploration space under a fixed inference budget.

中文摘要翻译: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）已成为大型语言模型数学推理的标准范式，其中组相对策略优化（GRPO）是主导算法。我们识别出GRPO中两个被忽视的低效问题。首先，固定的KL系数过度限制了模型在需要显著偏离参考策略时的策略探索。其次，均匀的问题采样忽略了中等难度问题能提供最具信息量的梯度信号这一事实。我们提出FG-ExPO，即前沿引导的探索优先策略优化（Frontier-Guided Exploration-Prioritized Policy Optimization），它集成了两个轻量组件。精度条件KL缩放（AKL）通过批次平均精度的平滑非线性函数调整KL惩罚强度，当模型表现较差时放松约束，当模型达到令人满意的结果时加强约束。高斯课程采样（GCS）以约0.5的中等精度水平为中心的高斯分布为问题分配采样权重，将模型训练聚焦于其学习前沿。我们在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B和Qwen3-8B-Base上，跨越六个主流数学推理基准进行了评估。实验结果表明，FG-ExPO一致优于原始GRPO。它在AIME 2025 pass@32指标上实现了13.34的绝对提升，从63.33%提升至76.67%，并在8B模型上获得了平均2.66的pass@32增益。在pass@32上观察到的性能增益远大于pass@1，这验证了FG-ExPO在固定推理预算下扩大了模型的有效探索空间。

</details>


### [187] [20/20 Vision Language Models: A Prescription for Better VLMs through Data Curation Alone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11405)
*Siddharth Joshi,Haoli Yin,Rishabh Adiga,Haakon Mongstad,Alvin Deng,Aldo Carranza,Alex Fang,Amro Abbas,Anshuman Suri,Brett Larsen,Daniel Zayas,Darren Teh,David Schwab,Diego Kiner,Fan Pan,Jack Urbanek,Jason Lee,Jason Telanoff,Josh Wills,Kaleigh Mentzer,Luke Merrick,Maximilian Böther,Parth Doshi,Paul Burstein,Pratyush Maini,Ties Robroek,Tony Jiang,Vidhi Jain,Vineeth Dorna,Zhengping Wang,Bogdan Gaza,Ari Morcos,Matthew Leavitt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了仅靠数据筛选（Data Curation）能多大程度提升视觉-语言模型（VLM）的性能。在固定架构、训练方案和计算量的条件下，仅改变训练数据，通过MAmmoTH-VL单图像子集上的筛选管道，在20个公开VLM基准上平均提升11.7个百分点，在DatBench的9个能力轴上平均提升11.3个百分点。2B模型在仅约1/17训练计算量下超越InternVL3.5-2B达9.9pp，在约1/87计算量下将与Qwen3-VL-2B差距缩小至1.8pp以内。此外，数据筛选还带来了可靠性、OOD泛化、行为质量（更诚实、更具体、更简洁）以及推理成本帕累托优势等额外收益。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 数据筛选已显著提升了语言模型和对比式图像-文本预训练的质量-计算前沿，但它在视觉-语言模型（VLM）中的作用尚未得到充分研究。本文旨在探究在固定架构、训练方案和计算量的条件下，仅通过改变训练数据（即数据筛选）能将VLM性能推到何种程度。

Method: 提出了一套系统的数据筛选管道（pipeline），应用于MAmmoTH-VL单图像子集。在固定的架构、训练方案和计算资源下，仅对训练数据进行筛选和优化，并系统评估筛选后模型在20个公开VLM基准和自建高保真评估套件DatBench上的表现。

Result: 1) 在20个公开VLM基准上平均提升11.7pp，DatBench的9个能力轴上平均提升11.3pp；2) 2B模型以~17倍更少训练计算超越InternVL3.5-2B达9.9pp，以~87倍更少计算将差距缩小至Qwen3-VL-2B的1.8pp内；3) 跨训练种子的能力标准差下降~67%，4k至16k上下文长度提升稳定；4) OOD平均提升7.2pp，单图像训练下多图像BLINK提升3.09pp，Visual Correspondence提升11.8pp；5) 在约1100个开放式查询中，筛选后的2B模型更诚实、更具体，比前沿2B参考模型更简洁且更少拒绝回答；6) 在1B/2B/4B各尺度上，筛选模型均提升精度并降低推理FLOPs，4B模型以3.3倍更低推理FLOPs匹配近前沿精度。

Conclusion: 数据筛选是构建更好VLM的高杠杆工具，仅通过预训练数据筛选即可在高达~150倍更少训练计算量下达到接近前沿的精度。

Original Abstract: Data curation has shifted the quality-compute frontier for language-model and contrastive image-text pretraining, but its role for vision-language models (VLMs) is far less established. We ask how far data curation alone can take VLM performance, holding architecture, training recipe, and compute fixed and varying only the training data. Our pipeline, applied to the MAmmoTH-VL single-image subset, lifts performance by +11.7pp on average across 20 public VLM benchmarks (spanning grounding, VQA, OCR/documents, captioning, spatial/3D, counting, charts, math, brand-ID, and multi-image reasoning) and by +11.3pp on average across all nine capability axes of DatBench, our high-fidelity VLM eval suite. At 2B, our curated model surpasses InternVL3.5-2B by 9.9pp at ~17x less training compute and closes the gap to Qwen3-VL-2B to within 1.8pp at ~87x less compute, from pretraining alone. Beyond accuracy, curation delivers four further properties: (1) Reliability: per-capability std across training seeds drops by ~67% and the lift survives a 4k-to-16k context-length sweep; (2) OOD generalization: the 9-eval OOD average rises by +7.2pp, and multi-image BLINK rises by +3.09pp despite single-image-only training, with Visual Correspondence gaining +11.8pp; (3) Behavioral gains beyond benchmarks: across ~1,100 open-ended queries the curated 2B is more honest and more specific than the matched-compute baseline, and more concise and less refusal-prone than a frontier 2B reference; (4) Pareto-dominance on inference cost: at every scale (1B, 2B, 4B) the curated model raises accuracy while lowering response FLOPs vs. the matched-compute baseline, and the curated 4B matches near-frontier accuracy at 3.3x lower response FLOPs than Qwen3-VL-4B. Data curation is a high-leverage tool for building better VLMs, reaching near-frontier accuracy at up to ~150x less training compute.

中文摘要翻译: 数据筛选已经改变了语言模型和对比式图像-文本预训练的质量-计算前沿，但其在视觉-语言模型（VLM）中的作用还远未被充分确立。我们探究了在固定架构、训练方案和计算量、仅改变训练数据的条件下，数据筛选本身能将VLM性能推到何种程度。我们将所提出的管道应用于MAmmoTH-VL单图像子集，在20个公开VLM基准（涵盖接地、VQA、OCR/文档、描述、空间/3D、计数、图表、数学、品牌识别和多图像推理）上平均提升+11.7个百分点，在我们高保真VLM评估套件DatBench的全部9个能力轴上平均提升+11.3个百分点。在2B规模下，我们经筛选的模型以约17倍更少的训练计算量超越InternVL3.5-2B达9.9个百分点，并以约87倍更少的计算量（仅通过预训练）将与Qwen3-VL-2B的差距缩小至1.8个百分点以内。除了精度之外，数据筛选还带来了四个额外特性：(1) 可靠性：跨训练种子的能力标准差下降约67%，且提升在4k到16k上下文长度扫描中保持稳定；(2) 开放世界泛化：9项评估的OOD平均提升+7.2个百分点，尽管仅使用单图像训练，多图像BLINK仍提升+3.09个百分点，其中Visual Correspondence提升+11.8个百分点；(3) 超越基准的行为增益：在约1,100个开放式查询中，经筛选的2B模型比匹配计算量的基线更诚实、更具体，并且比前沿2B参考模型更简洁、更少拒绝回答；(4) 推理成本上的帕累托优势：在每个规模（1B、2B、4B）上，经筛选的模型相比匹配计算量的基线均提升了精度同时降低了响应FLOPs，且经筛选的4B模型以比Qwen3-VL-4B低3.3倍的响应FLOPs达到了接近前沿的精度。数据筛选是构建更好VLM的高杠杆工具，能够在高达约150倍更少的训练计算量下达到接近前沿的精度。

</details>


### [188] [A Boundary-Aware Non-parametric Granular-Ball Classifier Based on Minimum Description Length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11406)
*Zeqiang Xian,Caihui Liu,Yong Zhang,Wenjing Qiu,Duoqian Miao,Witold Pedrycz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于最小描述长度原理的粒球分类器（MDL-GBC），将粒球构建形式化为局部模型选择问题，通过统一描述长度准则比较三种候选模型，实现了边界感知、非参数且可解释的粒球分类。在18个基准数据集上取得了有竞争力的分类性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有粒球分类方法通常依赖手工设计的质量度量、邻域规则或启发式分裂/停止准则，导致局部构建决策的透明度不足，且难以显式建模边界敏感区域。为此，本文旨在设计一种边界感知、非参数且可解释的粒球分类方法。

Method: 基于最小描述长度（MDL）原理，将类条件粒球构建形式化为局部模型选择问题。对每个粒球，在统一描述长度准则下比较三种候选模型：单球模型、双球模型和核心-边界模型。所选模型决定当前球体是保留、几何分裂还是细化为核心和边界敏感子球。预测时采用类级混合编码规则，聚合同类稳定粒球并通过比较类间编码代价来分配测试样本。

Result: 在18个基准数据集上的实验表明，MDL-GBC在平均准确率、Macro-F1和平均排名上均取得最佳结果，优于经典分类器和代表性粒球方法，展现了有竞争力的分类性能。

Conclusion: MDL-GBC为传统的启发式粒球分类策略提供了一种有效且可解释的替代方案，通过MDL原理统一了粒球构建与分类机制，提升了决策透明度和边界感知能力。

Original Abstract: Existing granular-ball classification methods are often driven by handcrafted quality measures, neighborhood rules, or heuristic splitting and stopping criteria, which may reduce the transparency of local construction decisions and hinder explicit modeling of boundary-sensitive regions. To address this issue, this paper proposes a Minimum Description Length based Granular-Ball Classifier (MDL-GBC), a boundary-aware non-parametric and interpretable granular-ball classifier. MDL-GBC formulates class-conditional granular-ball construction as a local model selection problem under the Minimum Description Length principle. For each class, samples from the target class provide positive class evidence, while samples from the remaining classes provide negative boundary evidence. For each current granular ball, three candidate explanations are compared under a unified description-length criterion: a single-ball model, a two-ball model, and a core-boundary model. The selected model determines whether the ball is retained, geometrically split, or refined into core and boundary-sensitive child balls, thereby making local construction decisions consistent with the MDL-based classification mechanism. During prediction, a class-level mixture coding rule aggregates stable granular balls of the same class and assigns the test sample by comparing class-wise coding costs. Experiments on 18 benchmark datasets show that MDL-GBC achieves competitive classification performance against classical classifiers and representative granular-ball-based methods, obtaining the best average Accuracy, Macro-F1, and average rank. These results indicate that MDL-GBC provides an effective and interpretable alternative to conventional heuristic granular-ball classification strategies.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的粒球分类方法通常依赖手工设计的质量度量、邻域规则或启发式分裂与停止准则，这可能会降低局部构建决策的透明度，并阻碍对边界敏感区域的显式建模。为解决该问题，本文提出一种基于最小描述长度的粒球分类器（MDL-GBC），这是一种边界感知、非参数且可解释的粒球分类器。MDL-GBC将类条件粒球构建形式化为最小描述长度原理下的局部模型选择问题。对于每个类别，目标类样本提供正类证据，其余类样本提供负类边界证据。对每个当前粒球，在统一的描述长度准则下比较三种候选解释：单球模型、双球模型和核心-边界模型。所选模型决定该球体是保留、几何分裂还是细化为核心与边界敏感子球，从而使得局部构建决策与基于MDL的分类机制保持一致。在预测阶段，类级混合编码规则聚合同类的稳定粒球，并通过比较类间编码代价来分配测试样本。在18个基准数据集上的实验表明，MDL-GBC相较于经典分类器和代表性粒球方法取得了有竞争力的分类性能，获得了最佳的平均准确率、Macro-F1和平均排名。这些结果表明，MDL-GBC为传统的启发式粒球分类策略提供了一种有效且可解释的替代方案。

</details>


### [189] [Generative Diffusion Prior Distillation for Long-Context Knowledge Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11414)
*Nilushika Udayangani,Kishor Nandakishor,Marimuthu Palaniswami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出生成式扩散先验蒸馏（GDPD）框架，利用扩散模型的迭代恢复能力，将全序列教师模型的长上下文知识蒸馏到部分序列学生模型中，解决时序分类中因数据不完整导致的泛化差距问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 实际推理场景中受延迟和成本限制，输入通常为部分序列前缀，但部分数据缺乏类别判别性模式，导致分类器泛化能力显著下降。现有的知识蒸馏方法主要解决参数容量差距，而非训练数据差异（全序列 vs 部分序列）带来的泛化差距，直接将教师的全上下文特征作为学生短上下文特征的监督信号过于苛刻。

Method: 提出GDPD框架：将学生短上下文特征视为教师全上下文特征的退化观测，学习一个基于扩散模型的生成式先验，通过后验采样得到最能解释学生特征中缺失长程信息的目标教师表示，以渐进、多样且集体的方式优化学生特征，使其相对于这些目标最小化退化程度。

Result: 在多种早期分类设置、数据集和架构上的广泛实验表明，GDPD在全到部分蒸馏任务上取得了显著效果。

Conclusion: GDPD通过为每个学生特征提供与任务相关的长上下文知识分布，有效提升了部分序列分类任务的学习效果，弥合了全序列与部分序列之间的泛化差距。

Original Abstract: While traditional time-series classifiers assume full sequences at inference, practical constraints (latency and cost) often limit inputs to partial prefixes. The absence of class-discriminative patterns in partial data can significantly hinder a classifier's ability to generalize. This work uses knowledge distillation (KD) to equip partial time series classifiers with the generalization ability of their full-sequence counterparts. In KD, high-capacity teacher transfers supervision to aid student learning on the target task. Matching with teacher features has shown promise in closing the generalization gap due to limited parameter capacity. However, when the generalization gap arises from training-data differences (full versus partial), the teacher's full-context features can be an overwhelming target signal for the student's short-context features. To provide progressive, diverse, and collective teacher supervision, we propose Generative Diffusion Prior Distillation (GDPD), a novel KD framework that treats short-context student features as degraded observations of the target full-context features. Inspired by the iterative restoration capability of diffusion models, we learn a diffusion-based generative prior over teacher features. Leveraging this prior, we posterior-sample target teacher representations that could best explain the missing long-range information in the student features and optimize the student features to be minimally degraded relative to these targets. GDPD provides each student feature with a distribution of task-relevant long-context knowledge, which benefits learning on the partial classification task. Extensive experiments across earliness settings, datasets, and architectures demonstrate GDPD's effectiveness for full-to-partial distillation.

中文摘要翻译: 传统的时间序列分类器在推理时假设可以获得完整序列，但实际应用中的延迟和成本限制通常只允许输入部分前缀。部分数据中缺乏具有类别判别性的模式，这会严重阻碍分类器的泛化能力。本文利用知识蒸馏（KD）为部分时间序列分类器赋予其全序列对应模型的泛化能力。在KD中，高容量的教师模型传递监督信号以辅助学生模型学习目标任务。与教师特征匹配已被证明有助于缩小因参数容量不足导致的泛化差距。然而，当泛化差距源于训练数据差异（全序列与部分序列）时，教师的全上下文特征对于学生的短上下文特征而言可能成为一个过于苛刻的目标信号。为了提供渐进、多样且集体的教师监督，我们提出生成式扩散先验蒸馏（GDPD），一种新颖的KD框架，将学生的短上下文特征视为目标全上下文特征的退化观测。受扩散模型迭代恢复能力的启发，我们学习了基于扩散的教师特征生成式先验。利用这一先验，我们对最能解释学生特征中缺失长程信息的目标教师表示进行后验采样，并优化学生特征使其相对于这些目标的退化程度最小。GDPD为每个学生特征提供了与任务相关的长上下文知识分布，有助于部分分类任务的学习。在多种早期分类设置、数据集和架构上的广泛实验证明了GDPD在全到部分蒸馏任务中的有效性。

</details>


### [190] [FastUMAP: Scalable Dimensionality Reduction via Bipartite Landmark Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11428)
*Hongmin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FastUMAP是一种基于地标的快速降维方法，专为高维数据的重复探索性分析设计，能在保持合理精度的前提下大幅提升运行速度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在高维数据的探索性分析中，用户需要反复调整预处理、子集或超参数并重新运行降维，而非线性降维方法的标准实现会成为瓶颈。现有方法缺乏针对这种重复使用场景的高效方案。

Method: 基于地标的方法。首先构建稀疏的点-地标模糊图，然后从诱导的地标亲和矩阵计算Nyström谱热启动，最后在二分图上使用UMAP风格的目标函数对所有样本坐标进行精炼。地标比例r=m/n提供了在运行时间与保真度之间权衡的直接方式。

Result: 在9个基准数据集（样本量178到70,000）上，FastUMAP在7个数据集上取得了最低运行时间。在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST（n=70,000）上，运行时间约4.6秒，而Barnes-Hut t-SNE需要73-75秒；同时达到91.4%的平均kNN准确率，最强基线为94.6%。

Conclusion: FastUMAP最适合作为重复探索性嵌入的快速选项，而非精度优先方法的替代品。

Original Abstract: Exploratory analysis of high-dimensional data rarely stops at a single embedding. In practice, analysts rerun dimensionality reduction after changing preprocessing, subsets, or hyperparameters, and standard nonlinear methods can quickly become the bottleneck. We introduce FastUMAP (Bipartite Manifold Approximation and Projection), a landmark-based method designed for this repeated-use setting. FastUMAP builds a sparse point-landmark fuzzy graph, computes a Nystrom spectral warm start from the induced landmark affinity, and then refines all sample coordinates with a UMAP-style objective on the bipartite graph. The landmark ratio r = m/n provides a direct way to trade runtime against fidelity. On 9 benchmark datasets spanning 178 to 70,000 samples, FastUMAP has the lowest runtime on 7 datasets in our reported default-implementation comparison on one workstation. On MNIST and Fashion-MNIST (n=70000), it runs in about 4.6 seconds, compared with about 73--75 seconds for Barnes--Hut t-SNE, while reaching 91.4% mean kNN accuracy versus 94.6% for the strongest accuracy baseline. FastUMAP is therefore best viewed as a fast option for repeated exploratory embedding, rather than as a replacement for accuracy-first methods.

中文摘要翻译: 高维数据的探索性分析很少止步于单一嵌入。在实践中，分析人员在改变预处理、子集或超参数后会重新运行降维，而标准的非线性方法会迅速成为瓶颈。我们提出了FastUMAP（二分流形逼近与投影），一种专为这种重复使用场景设计的基于地标的方法。FastUMAP构建一个稀疏的点-地标模糊图，从诱导的地标亲和矩阵计算Nyström谱热启动，然后在二分图上使用UMAP风格的目标函数对所有样本坐标进行精炼。地标比例r=m/n提供了在运行时间与保真度之间进行权衡的直接方式。在9个基准数据集（样本量从178到70,000）上，在我们报告的工作站默认实现对比中，FastUMAP在7个数据集上取得了最低运行时间。在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST（n=70,000）上，它运行约4.6秒，而Barnes-Hut t-SNE需要约73-75秒，同时达到91.4%的平均kNN准确率，而最强准确率基线为94.6%。因此，FastUMAP最适合被视为重复探索性嵌入的快速选项，而非精度优先方法的替代品。

</details>


### [191] [Deep Minds and Shallow Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11448)
*Su Hyeong Lee,Risi Kondor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于对称性的神经表示探针理论：由于神经表示在重参数化下不唯一，探针家族应对表示对称性保持稳定。作者在最终读出层证明了线性探针是度数为1的坐标稳定探针，并提出了探针可见商空间用于跨模型探针迁移。实验验证了度数-2探针的优势及基于商空间的跨模型监控迁移能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 神经表示在重参数化下不唯一，现有探针方法绑定于特定坐标系，缺乏对表示对称性的稳定性保证，导致跨模型探针迁移困难。

Method: 在最终读出层这一可精确处理的设置中，研究重参数化诱导的仿射变换群作用，推导出坐标稳定探针的唯一层次结构（线性探针为度-1成员），并提出探针可见商空间作为跨模型迁移的自然对象。

Result: 1. 证明线性探针是坐标稳定探针层次中的度-1成员；2. 实验显示二次（度-2）探针在某些任务上优于线性探针；3. 基于商空间的迁移实现了跨模型族的覆盖感知监控迁移。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个基于对称性的神经探针几何表示理论框架，为跨模型探针迁移提供了可操作方案，表明覆盖感知监控迁移是该理论的具体应用成果。

Original Abstract: Neural representations are not unique objects. Even when two systems realize the same downstream computation, their hidden coordinates may differ by reparameterization. A probe family intended to reveal structure already present in a representation should therefore be stable under the relevant representation symmetries rather than be tied to a particular basis. We study this group action in the tractable exact setting of the final readout layer, where equivalent realizations induce affine changes of hidden coordinates. The resulting symmetry principle singles out a unique hierarchy of shallow coordinate-stable probes, with linear probes as its degree-1 member. We also show that a natural object for cross-model probe transfer is a shared probe-visible quotient--the representation modulo directions invisible to the probe family--rather than the full hidden state. Experiments on synthetic and real-world tasks support both predictions, showing where degree-2 probes help beyond linear ones and how quotient-based transfer enables coverage-aware monitor portability across model families. These results point toward a broader geometric representation theory of neural probing, with coverage-aware monitor transfer as a concrete operational consequence.

中文摘要翻译: 神经表示并非唯一的对象。即使两个系统实现了相同的下游计算，它们的隐藏坐标也可能因重参数化而不同。旨在揭示表示中已有结构的探针家族应当对相关的表示对称性保持稳定，而非绑定于特定基。我们在可精确处理的最终读出层设置中研究这一群作用，其中等价实现会诱导隐藏坐标的仿射变换。由此得到的对称性原理唯一确定了一个浅层坐标稳定探针的层次结构，其中线性探针为其一次成员。我们还证明了跨模型探针迁移的一个自然对象是共享的探针可见商空间——即表示模去探针家族不可见的方向——而非完整的隐藏状态。在合成任务和真实任务上的实验支持了这两个预测，展示了二次探针在何时比线性探针更有帮助，以及基于商空间的迁移如何实现跨模型族的覆盖感知监控迁移。这些结果指向了一个更广泛的神经探针几何表示理论，其中覆盖感知监控迁移是一个具体的操作化成果。

</details>


### [192] [Efficient Adjoint Matching for Fine-tuning Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11480)
*Jeongwoo Shin,Dongsoo Shin,Joonseok Lee,Jaewoong Choi,Jaemoo Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EAM通过将奖励微调中的随机最优控制问题重构成线性基础漂移形式，消除了伴随匹配方法的两大计算瓶颈（完整轨迹随机采样和反向伴随ODE模拟），实现了最高4倍的训练加速。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的Adjoint Matching (AM)方法虽然从随机最优控制角度为奖励微调提供了严谨的公式化框架，但其计算开销极大，主要包括：(i) 在无记忆动力学下对完整生成轨迹进行随机模拟，需要大量函数评估；(ii) 沿每条采样轨迹进行反向伴随ODE模拟。这两个瓶颈都与预训练模型中非平凡的基础漂移(non-trivial base drift)密切相关。

Method: 本文提出Efficient Adjoint Matching (EAM)，核心思想是将SOC问题重新公式化为具有线性基础漂移(linear base drift)和相应修改的终端成本(terminal cost)的形式。这种重公式化消除了两大低效来源：训练时采样只需使用几步确定性ODE求解器，并且伴随状态有闭式解，无需反向伴随模拟。

Result: 在标准文本到图像奖励微调基准上，EAM收敛速度比AM快4倍，在PickScore、ImageReward、HPSv2.1、CLIPScore和Aesthetics等多个指标上与AM相当或更优。

Conclusion: 通过将基础漂移简化为线性形式，EAM大幅提升了基于奖励梯度的扩散模型微调效率，验证了基础漂移形式对计算效率的关键影响。

Original Abstract: Reward fine-tuning has become a common approach for aligning pretrained diffusion and flow models with human preferences in text-to-image generation. Among reward-gradient-based methods, Adjoint Matching (AM) provides a principled formulation by casting reward fine-tuning as a stochastic optimal control (SOC) problem. However, AM inevitably requires a substantial computational cost: it requires (i) stochastic simulation of full generative trajectories under memoryless dynamics, resulting in a large number of function evaluations, and (ii) backward ODE simulation of the adjoint state along each sampled trajectory. In this work, we observe that both bottlenecks are closely tied to the \textit{non-trivial base drift} inherited from the pretrained model. Motivated by this observation, we propose \textbf{Efficient Adjoint Matching (EAM)}, which substantially improves training efficiency by reformulating the SOC problem with a \textit{linear base drift} and a correspondingly modified \textit{terminal cost}. This reformulation removes both sources of inefficiency; it enables training-time sampling with a few-step deterministic ODE solver and yields a closed-form adjoint solution that eliminates backward adjoint simulation. On standard text-to-image reward fine-tuning benchmarks, EAM converges up to 4x faster than AM and matches or surpasses it across various metrics including PickScore, ImageReward, HPSv2.1, CLIPScore and Aesthetics.

中文摘要翻译: 奖励微调已成为在文本到图像生成中将预训练扩散模型和流模型与人类偏好对齐的常用方法。在基于奖励梯度的方法中，伴随匹配(AM)通过将奖励微调建模为随机最优控制(SOC)问题，提供了一种严谨的公式化框架。然而，AM不可避免地需要巨大的计算成本：它需要(i)在无记忆动力学下对完整生成轨迹进行随机模拟，导致大量函数评估，以及(ii)沿每条采样轨迹进行反向ODE伴随状态模拟。在这项工作中，我们观察到这两个瓶颈都与预训练模型继承的\textit{非平凡基础漂移}密切相关。基于这一观察，我们提出\textbf{高效伴随匹配(EAM)}，该方法通过将SOC问题重构成\textit{线性基础漂移}和相应修改的\textit{终端成本}，大幅提升了训练效率。这一重新公式化消除了两个低效来源；它使得训练时采样只需几步确定性ODE求解器，并产生闭式伴随解，从而无需反向伴随模拟。在标准文本到图像奖励微调基准上，EAM的收敛速度比AM快4倍，并且在PickScore、ImageReward、HPSv2.1、CLIPScore和Aesthetics等多个指标上与AM相当或更优。

</details>


### [193] [Adaptive Calibration in Non-Stationary Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11490)
*Junyan Liu,Haipeng Luo,Lillian J. Ratliff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一组自适应在线预测校准算法，能够根据环境非平稳程度（以平均结果的ℓ1偏差度量C∈[0,T]）自动调整校准误差，在独立同分布和完全对抗性环境之间平滑插值，获得了ℓ1校准误差的Õ(√T + (TC)^(1/3))以及ℓ2和伪KL校准误差的Õ((1+C)^(1/3))的最优保证。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有在线预测校准文献主要关注完全对抗性环境，导致算法在更温和的环境（如结果近乎平稳）下表现次优。因此，作者希望设计一种自适应算法，使其校准误差能根据环境的非平稳程度自动调整，在独立同分布和对抗性环境之间平滑插值。

Method: 方法基于并扩展了Hu et al. [2026]和Luo et al. [2025]的工作，引入了基于轮次（epoch）的调度策略，并设计了一种新颖的非均匀预测空间划分方法，在底层真实结果附近分配更精细的分辨率。

Result: 算法在ℓ1校准误差上达到Õ(√T + (TC)^(1/3))，在ℓ2和伪KL校准误差上达到Õ((1+C)^(1/3))。当C=0（平稳情形）时匹配最优率，当C=T（完全对抗性情形）时恢复已知的最优保证。

Conclusion: 作者肯定地回答了能否设计自适应校准算法的问题，开发的算法套件在多种校准度量下都能实现自适应校准保证，理论边界在平稳和完全对抗两种极端情形下均达到最优。

Original Abstract: Making calibrated online predictions is a central challenge in modern AI systems. Much of the existing literature focuses on fully adversarial environments where outcomes may be arbitrary, leading to conservative algorithms that can perform suboptimally in more benign settings, such as when outcomes are nearly stationary. This gap raises a natural question: can we design online prediction algorithms whose calibration error automatically adapts to the degree of non-stationarity in the environment, smoothly interpolating between i.i.d. and adversarial regimes? We answer this question in the affirmative and develop a suite of algorithms that achieve adaptive calibration guarantees under multiple calibration measures. Specifically, with $T$ being the number of rounds and $C\in[0,T]$ being an unknown non-stationary measure defined as the minimal $\ell_1$ deviation of the mean outcomes, our algorithms attain $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{T}+(TC)^{\frac{1}{3}})$ for $\ell_1$ calibration error and $\widetilde{O}((1+C)^{\frac{1}{3}})$ for both $\ell_2$ and pseudo KL calibration error. These bounds match the optimal rates in the stationary case ($C=0$) and recover known guarantees in the fully adversarial regime ($C=T$). Our approach builds on and extends prior work [Hu et al., 2026, Luo et al., 2025], introducing an epoch-based scheduling together with a novel non-uniform partition of the prediction space that allocates finer resolution near the underlying ground truth.

中文摘要翻译: 在现代AI系统中，做出校准良好的在线预测是一个核心挑战。现有文献大多关注完全对抗性环境（其中结果可能是任意的），这导致算法在更温和的环境（如结果几乎平稳）中表现次优。这一差距引发了一个自然问题：我们能否设计出校准误差自动适应环境非平稳程度的在线预测算法，在独立同分布和对抗性环境之间平滑插值？我们对此问题给出了肯定回答，并开发了一套算法套件，在多种校准度量下实现自适应校准保证。具体而言，设T为轮次数，C∈[0,T]为定义为最小平均结果ℓ1偏差的未知非平稳度量，我们的算法在ℓ1校准误差上达到Õ(√T + (TC)^(1/3))，在ℓ2和伪KL校准误差上达到Õ((1+C)^(1/3))。这些边界在平稳情形（C=0）下匹配最优率，在完全对抗性情形（C=T）下恢复已知保证。我们的方法基于并扩展了Hu等人[2026]和Luo等人[2025]的先前工作，引入了基于轮次的调度策略，并采用了一种新颖的非均匀预测空间划分方法，在底层真实结果附近分配更精细的分辨率。

</details>


### [194] [Understanding and Preventing Entropy Collapse in RLVR with On-Policy Entropy Flow Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11491)
*Huimin Xu,Shuai Zhao,Xiaobao Wu,Anh Tuan Luu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从token级熵流视角分析了RLVR算法中的熵坍缩现象，发现熵减token持续多于熵增token导致熵流严重失衡，并提出了严格在策略的适应性熵流平衡机制OPEFO，在数学推理基准上提升了训练稳定性和最终性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有RLVR算法（如GRPO）存在熵坍缩问题，导致过早确定性训练和不稳定优化，而现有解决方案（熵正则化、比率裁剪启发式）要么控制粒度粗糙，要么依赖近似在策略训练，缺乏对熵坍缩根本原因的统一解释和有效应对方法。

Method: 提出基于在策略的熵流优化（OPEFO），一种自适应的熵流平衡机制：根据熵增和熵减更新对熵变的贡献重新缩放其更新幅度，同时保持严格在策略训练（strict on-policy）。

Result: 在六个数学推理基准上的实验表明，OPEFO改善了训练稳定性和最终性能。

Conclusion: token级熵流视角为解释RLVR中的熵坍缩提供了统一框架，而OPEFO通过平衡熵流有效缓解了熵坍缩，提升了RLVR训练的稳定性。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become an effective paradigm for improving the reasoning ability of large language models. However, widely used RLVR algorithms, such as GRPO, often suffer from entropy collapse, leading to premature determinism and unstable optimization. Existing remedies, including entropy regularization and ratio-based clipping heuristics, either control entropy in a coarse-grained manner or rely on approximate on-policy training. In this paper, we revisit entropy collapse from a token-level entropy flow perspective. Our analysis reveals that entropy-decreasing tokens consistently outweigh entropy-increasing ones, resulting in a severely imbalanced entropy flow. This perspective provides a unified explanation of entropy collapse in existing RLVR algorithms and highlights the importance of balancing entropy dynamics. Motivated by this analysis, we propose On-Policy Entropy Flow Optimization (OPEFO), an adaptive entropy flow balancing mechanism that rescales entropy-increasing and entropy-decreasing updates according to their contributions to entropy change, while remaining strict on-policy. Experiments on six mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that OPEFO improves training stability and final performance. We will release the code and models upon publication.

中文摘要翻译: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）已成为提升大语言模型推理能力的有效范式。然而，广泛使用的RLVR算法（如GRPO）经常遭遇熵坍缩问题，导致过早确定性和不稳定的优化。现有的补救措施，包括熵正则化和基于比率裁剪的启发式方法，要么以粗粒度的方式控制熵，要么依赖近似的在策略训练。本文从token级熵流的角度重新审视熵坍缩。我们的分析表明，熵减token始终多于熵增token，导致熵流严重失衡。这一视角为现有RLVR算法中的熵坍缩提供了统一解释，并凸显了平衡熵动力学的重要性。受此分析启发，我们提出在策略熵流优化（OPEFO），一种自适应的熵流平衡机制，该机制根据熵增和熵减更新对熵变的贡献重新缩放其更新幅度，同时保持严格在策略训练。在六个数学推理基准上的实验表明，OPEFO改善了训练稳定性和最终性能。我们将在论文发表后公开代码和模型。

</details>


### [195] [EqOD: Symmetry-Informed Stability Selection for PDE Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11524)
*Gnankan Landry Regis N'guessan,Bum Jun Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出等变算子发现(EqOD)方法，通过伽利略对称性库约简和随机LASSO稳定性筛选两种机制自动缩减微分算子候选库，在多个PDE识别任务中显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有数据驱动PDE识别方法依赖于稀疏回归，但候选库越大噪声下假阳性越多，库太小又可能遗漏真实项，两者难以平衡。

Method: EqOD包含两种自动库缩减机制：(1) 当从轨迹数据中检测到伽利略不变性时，使用对称性缩减库，排除伽利略排除定理证明不存在的项；(2) 否则应用随机LASSO稳定性筛选（基于经典假阳性界）。若上述方法退化，残差基回退机制保护不劣于全库基线。

Result: 在8个PDE、4个噪声水平下，EqOD在20%噪声的热方程上F1=1.000±0.000，远超WF-LASSO(0.475)、PySINDy 2.0(0.000)和WSINDy(0.789)。32个测试单元中EqOD赢得7个、0个败绩、25个平局。在WeakIdent和PINN-SR外部数据集上所有5个干净基准F1=1.000。

Conclusion: EqOD通过对称性约简和稳定性选择实现了全自动、高鲁棒性的PDE发现，在多种噪声条件下显著优于现有方法，但相关PDE设计矩阵的形式化保证仍为开放问题。

Original Abstract: Data-driven identification of partial differential equations (PDEs) relies on sparse regression over a candidate library of differential operators, where larger libraries inflate false positives under observation noise and smaller libraries risk missing true terms. We introduce Equivariant Operator Discovery (EqOD), a fully automatic method combining two library reduction mechanisms. When Galilean invariance is detected from trajectory data via a weak-form structural test, EqOD uses the symmetry-reduced library, eliminating terms that our Galilean exclusion result proves to be absent from the governing equation. Otherwise, it applies randomized LASSO stability selection guided by classical false-positive bounds. A residual-based fallback prevents degradation below the full-library baseline. On 8 PDEs at 4 noise levels, EqOD attains $F_1 = 1.000 \pm 0.000$ on Heat at $20\%$ noise, where WF-LASSO obtains $0.475 \pm 0.181$, official PySINDy 2.0 obtains $0.000$, and the WSINDy reimplementation obtains $0.789$. Under the strict criterion that the mean F1 difference exceeds the larger of the two standard deviations, EqOD wins 7 of 32 cells. WF-LASSO wins none, and the remaining 25 cells are ties. Across all 32 cells, EqOD outperforms PySINDy 2.0.0 in 23 of 32 cells, and all 5 PySINDy wins occur on reaction PDEs. External validation on WeakIdent and PINN-SR datasets gives $F_1 = 1.000$ on all 5 clean benchmarks. NLS, 2D, coupled-system, and cylinder-wake extensions are reported. The Galilean library reduction is proved under explicit autonomy and library assumptions. The stability-selection step is motivated by classical false-positive bounds, while formal guarantees for correlated PDE design matrices remain open.

中文摘要翻译: 数据驱动的偏微分方程(PDE)识别依赖于对微分算子候选库的稀疏回归，其中较大的库在观测噪声下会膨胀假阳性，而较小的库则有遗漏真实项的风险。我们提出了等变算子发现(EqOD)，一种结合两种库缩减机制的自动方法。当通过弱形式结构检验从轨迹数据中检测到伽利略不变性时，EqOD使用对称性缩减库，排除被我们的伽利略排除定理证明在控制方程中不存在的项。否则，它应用由经典假阳性界指导的随机LASSO稳定性筛选。残差基回退机制防止性能低于全库基线。在8个PDE、4个噪声水平下，EqOD在20%噪声的热方程上取得F1=1.000±0.000，而WF-LASSO仅0.475±0.181，官方PySINDy 2.0为0.000，WSINDy重实现为0.789。在平均F1差超过两个标准差中较大者的严格准则下，EqOD在32个测试单元中赢得7个。WF-LASSO未赢得任何单元，其余25个单元为平局。在所有32个单元中，EqOD在23个单元上优于PySINDy 2.0.0，且所有5次PySINDy获胜均发生在反应型PDE上。在WeakIdent和PINN-SR数据集上的外部验证表明，所有5个干净基准F1=1.000。还报告了NLS、二维、耦合系统和圆柱尾流扩展实验。伽利略库缩减在显式自治性和库假设下被证明。稳定性选择步骤由经典假阳性界驱动，而相关PDE设计矩阵的形式化保证仍为开放问题。

</details>


### [196] [OverNaN: NaN-Aware Oversampling for Imbalanced Learning with Meaningful Missingness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11525)
*Amanda S Barnard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OverNaN是一种轻量级的、对NaN值感知的过采样框架，用于处理类别不平衡问题，同时保留数据中的缺失值结构，不通过插补或删除来掩盖缺失信息。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统方法将缺失值视为需要删除或插补的缺陷，但缺失本身可能携带信息。消除或掩盖缺失结构会扭曲类别边界、引入偏差并降低泛化能力，尤其是在少数类本就代表性不足的不平衡数据集中。

Method: OverNaN扩展了常见的合成过采样方法，使其能够直接在带有缺失值的特征向量上操作，允许根据明确定义的策略保留、传播或选择性插补缺失值，将缺失视为特征空间的一部分而非待修复的缺陷。

Result: 通过代表性示例展示了在有意义的缺失结构可以在过采样过程中被保留，而不会引入人为的确定性。

Conclusion: OverNaN适用于科学和工程领域中处理具有缺失值且不可回避的小型不平衡数据集，在这些场景下缺失值往往携带信息。

Original Abstract: Missing values are routinely treated as defects to be eliminated through deletion or imputation prior to machine learning. In many applied domains, however, missingness itself carries information, reflecting experimental constraints, measurement choices, or systematic mechanisms tied to the data-generating process. Eliminating or masking this structure can distort class boundaries, introduce bias, and reduce generalisability; particularly in imbalanced datasets where minority classes are already under-represented. OverNaN is a lightweight, NaN-aware oversampling framework designed to address class imbalance without erasing missingness structure. It extends common synthetic oversampling methods to operate directly on incomplete feature vectors, allowing missing values to be preserved, propagated, or selectively interpolated according to explicitly defined strategies. Rather than repairing missing data, OverNaN treats missingness as part of the feature space over which synthetic samples are generated. This paper situates OverNaN within the broader landscape of imbalanced learning, missing-data handling, and NaN-tolerant algorithms. Using representative examples included with the software, we demonstrate that meaningful missingness can be retained during oversampling without introducing artificial certainty. OverNaN is intended for practitioners working with small, incomplete, and imbalanced datasets in scientific and engineering domains where missingness is unavoidable and often informative.

中文摘要翻译: 缺失值通常被视为缺陷，需要在机器学习之前通过删除或插补来消除。然而在许多应用领域中，缺失本身携带着信息，反映了实验约束、测量选择或与数据生成过程相关的系统性机制。消除或掩盖这种结构会扭曲类别边界、引入偏差并降低泛化能力，尤其是在少数类本就代表性不足的不平衡数据集中。OverNaN是一种轻量级的、对NaN值感知的过采样框架，旨在处理类别不平衡问题而不抹除缺失值结构。它扩展了常见的合成过采样方法，使其能够直接在不完整的特征向量上操作，允许根据明确定义的策略保留、传播或选择性插补缺失值。OverNaN并非修复缺失数据，而是将缺失视为合成样本生成过程中特征空间的一部分。本文将OverNaN置于不平衡学习、缺失数据处理和NaN容忍算法的更广泛背景中。通过软件附带的代表性示例，我们证明了有意义的缺失结构可以在过采样过程中被保留，而不会引入人为的确定性。OverNaN适用于在科学和工程领域中处理小型、不完整且不平衡的数据集的从业者，这些场景中缺失值是不可避免且通常携带信息的。

</details>


### [197] [FERMI: Exploiting Relations for Membership Inference Against Tabular Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11527)
*Abtin Mahyar,Masoumeh Shafieinejad,Yuhan Liu,Xi He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FERMI方法，针对多表关系型数据场景下的扩散模型成员推断攻击，通过引入关联表的辅助关系信号来增强单表特征，从而更准确地评估隐私泄露风险。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有成员推断攻击仅针对单表设定，忽略了真实敏感数据中多表关系结构带来的隐私泄露风险，缺乏利用关联表辅助信息进行攻击的方法。

Method: FERMI（特征映射关系成员推断）方法：在训练攻击模型时利用目标表关联的父表等辅助关系信息，将其转化为关系成员信号，与单表特征融合，从而在推理时仅需目标记录属性即可完成攻击。

Result: 在三种表格扩散架构和三个真实关系数据集上，FERMI相比单表基线持续提升攻击性能，白盒设定下TPR@0.1FPR提升高达53%，黑盒设定下提升22%。

Conclusion: 利用多表关系结构中的辅助信息能显著提升成员推断攻击效果，揭示了扩散模型在多表关系数据场景下存在更严重的隐私泄露风险。

Original Abstract: Diffusion models are the leading approach for tabular data synthesis and are increasingly used to share sensitive records. Whether they actually protect privacy has become a pressing question. Membership inference attacks are the standard tool for this purpose, yet existing attacks assume a single-table setting and ignore the multi-relational structure of real sensitive data. A core challenge in assessing privacy risks from membership inference attacks in multi-table settings is how to leverage auxiliary information from relations associated with the target table, such as its parent tables. Particularly, we study a practical setting in which such auxiliary information is available only when training the attack model. At inference time, the attacker observes only the attribute values of the target record from the target table. We propose FERMI (FEature-mapping for Relational Membership Inference), which resolves this gap by enriching single-table features with relational membership signal. Across three tabular diffusion architectures and three real-world relational datasets, FERMI consistently improves attack performance over single-table baselines, with TPR@$0.1$FPR rising by up to 53% over the single-table baseline in the white-box setting and 22% in the black-box setting.

中文摘要翻译: 扩散模型是表格数据合成的主要方法，并越来越多地用于共享敏感记录。它们是否真的能保护隐私已成为一个紧迫的问题。成员推断攻击是评估隐私风险的标准工具，但现有攻击假设单表设定，忽略了真实敏感数据的多关系结构。在多表设定下评估成员推断攻击的隐私风险时，一个核心挑战是如何利用与目标表关联的辅助信息（例如其父表）。具体而言，我们研究了一种实际场景：在训练攻击模型时可获得此类辅助信息，而在推理时，攻击者仅能观察到目标表中目标记录的属性值。我们提出了FERMI（特征映射关系成员推断），通过用关系成员信号增强单表特征来解决这一不足。在三种表格扩散架构和三个真实世界关系数据集上，FERMI相比单表基线持续提升攻击性能，在白盒设定下TPR@0.1FPR提升高达53%，在黑盒设定下提升22%。

</details>


### [198] [Primal-Dual Policy Optimization for Linear CMDPs with Adversarial Losses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11535)
*Kihyun Yu,Seoungbin Bae,Dabeen Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个在对抗性线性约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP）中达到次线性遗憾和约束违反界的算法，通过加权LogSumExp softmax策略、周期性策略混合和正则化对偶更新，实现了$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(K^{3/4})$的界。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有线性CMDP研究主要集中在损失和成本固定或来自固定分布的随机环境，无法抵御对抗性环境变化。本文旨在填补在线对抗性线性CMDP这一空白。

Method: 提出一种原始-对偶策略优化算法，包含三个关键组件：(i) 加权LogSumExp softmax策略类以适应对抗性损失；(ii) 周期性策略混合以控制覆盖数；(iii) 正则化对偶更新以控制对偶变量。算法在全信息反馈下处理对抗性损失，在Bandit反馈下处理随机成本。

Result: 算法首次在该设置下实现$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(K^{3/4})$的遗憾和约束违反界，并通过数值实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 本文成功将线性CMDP拓展到对抗性环境，所提算法具有理论保证和实际有效性，为在线学习下的约束强化学习提供了新框架。

Original Abstract: Existing work on linear constrained Markov decision processes (CMDPs) has primarily focused on stochastic settings, where the losses and costs are either fixed or drawn from fixed distributions. However, such formulations are inherently vulnerable to adversarially changing environments. To overcome this limitation, we propose a primal-dual policy optimization algorithm for online finite-horizon {adversarial} linear CMDPs, where the losses are adversarially chosen under full-information feedback and the costs are stochastic under bandit feedback. Our algorithm is the \emph{first} to achieve sublinear regret and constraint violation bounds in this setting, both bounded by $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(K^{3/4})$, where $K$ denotes the number of episodes. The algorithm introduces and runs with a new class of policies, which we call weighted LogSumExp softmax policies, designed to adapt to adversarially chosen loss functions. Our main result stems from the following key contributions: (i) a new covering number argument for the weighted LogSumExp softmax policies, and (ii) two novel algorithmic components -- periodic policy mixing and a regularized dual update -- which allow us to effectively control both the covering number and the dual variable. We also report numerical results that validate our theoretical findings on the performance of the algorithm.

中文摘要翻译: 现有关于线性约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP）的研究主要集中于随机环境，其中损失和成本是固定的或来自固定分布。然而，这类公式本质上容易受到对抗性变化环境的影响。为克服这一局限，我们针对在线有限时域对抗性线性CMDP提出了一种原始-对偶策略优化算法，其中损失在全信息反馈下由对手选择，成本在Bandit反馈下是随机的。我们的算法是首个在此设定下达到次线性遗憾和约束违反界的算法，两者均以$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(K^{3/4})$为界，其中$K$表示回合数。该算法引入并运行了一类新的策略——我们称之为加权LogSumExp softmax策略——旨在适应对抗性选择的损失函数。我们的主要结果源于以下关键贡献：(i) 关于加权LogSumExp softmax策略的新的覆盖数论证；(ii) 两个新颖的算法组件——周期性策略混合和正则化对偶更新——使我们能够有效控制覆盖数和对偶变量。我们还报告了数值结果，验证了算法性能的理论发现。

</details>


### [199] [Fast MoE Inference via Predictive Prefetching and Expert Replication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11537)
*Ankit Jyothish,Ali Jannesari,Aishwarya Sarkar,Joseph Zuber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种动态专家复制策略，通过预测并复制可能过载的专家，在MoE推理中实现并行处理，显著提升GPU利用率和推理速度（最高3倍），同时保持约90-95%的模型性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: MoE推理存在GPU利用率低、负载不均衡、延迟高的问题，根本原因在于专家激活的稀疏性导致多个token等待同一专家计算。

Method: 提出动态专家复制策略：预测可能过载的专家，为即将到来的token批次复制这些专家，使复制后的专家能够在各层间并发处理批次token，从而提升并行度、减少GPU空闲时间。

Result: 在Switch-base-128和Switch-base-256模型上的实验表明：GPU利用率接近100%，推理速度提升最高达3倍，同时保留了基线架构约90-95%的性能。

Conclusion: 动态专家复制策略能有效解决MoE推理中的负载不均衡和低GPU利用率问题，在几乎不牺牲模型性能的前提下大幅提升推理速度。

Original Abstract: The Mixture of Experts (MoE) architecture has become a fundamental building block in state-of-the-art large language models (LLMs), improving domain-specific expertise in LLMs and scaling model capacity without proportionally increasing their computational overhead. However, MoE inference often suffers from suboptimal GPU utilization, load imbalance, and elevated latency arising from multiple tokens waiting on the same experts for their computation which arises from sparsity of expert activation. To address these challenges, we propose a dynamic expert replication strategy that predicts which experts are likely to be overloaded and replicates them for upcoming batches of tokens. The replicated experts process batch tokens concurrently across layers, which leads to improved parallelism, shorter GPU idle time, and significantly faster inference. Experimental evaluations conducted on large-scale MoE models, including Switch-base-128 and Switch-base-256, demonstrate that our method achieves near-complete GPU utilization (approx 100%), leading to upto 3x improvement in inference speed while preserving approximately 90-95% of the performance of baseline architectures

中文摘要翻译: 混合专家（MoE）架构已成为当前最先进大语言模型（LLMs）的基本构建模块，它在提升LLMs领域特定专业知识的同时，能够在不按比例增加计算开销的情况下扩展模型容量。然而，MoE推理常常面临GPU利用率低下、负载不均衡以及延迟升高的问题，这些问题源于专家激活的稀疏性导致多个token等待同一专家进行计算。为了解决这些挑战，我们提出了一种动态专家复制策略，该策略预测哪些专家可能出现负载过重，并为即将到来的token批次复制这些专家。复制后的专家在各层间并发处理批次token，从而提升了并行度，缩短了GPU空闲时间，并显著加快了推理速度。在大规模MoE模型（包括Switch-base-128和Switch-base-256）上进行的实验评估表明，我们的方法实现了近乎完全的GPU利用率（约100%），推理速度提升最高达3倍，同时保留了基线架构约90-95%的性能。

</details>


### [200] [FedOUI: OUI-Guided Client Weighting for Federated Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11571)
*Alberto Fernández-Hernández,Jose I. Mestre,Cristian Pérez-Corral,Manuel F. Dolz,Jose Duato,Enrique S. Quintana-Ortí*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedOUI聚合规则，利用基于激活值且无标签的过拟合-欠拟合指标（OUI）来检测结构异常客户端，在强非独立同分布和噪声环境下优于FedAvg、FedProx等基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 联邦学习通常仅依据数据集大小或梯度级指标聚合客户端更新，忽略了各客户端模型在训练过程中如何组织其输入空间的内部信号，而内部激活结构可能提供有用的聚合信息。

Method: 提出FedOUI聚合规则：每个参与客户端发送本地更新及在固定探针批次上计算的OUI值，服务器根据轮次OUI分布通过平滑重加权规则为结构异常的客户端分配更低权重。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上的强非独立同分布和噪声客户端条件下，FedOUI相比FedAvg、FedProx和梯度对齐基线方法表现更好，在强异质性场景下聚合质量提升最明显，且保持轻量和可解释性。

Conclusion: 内部激活结构能够为联邦聚合提供超越客户端大小和梯度几何的有用信息。

Original Abstract: Federated learning usually aggregates client updates using dataset size or gradient-level criteria, while overlooking internal signals about how each client model is organizing its input space during training. We introduce FedOUI, a simple aggregation rule based on the Overfitting-Underfitting Indicator (OUI), an activation-based and label-free metric. Each participating client sends its local update together with a OUI value computed on a fixed probe batch, and the server estimates the round-wise OUI distribution to assign lower weights to structurally atypical clients through a smooth reweighting rule. We evaluate FedOUI on CIFAR-10 under strong non-IID partitioning and noisy-client conditions, comparing it with FedAvg, FedProx, and a gradient-alignment baseline. The clearest gains appear under strong heterogeneity, where OUI-based weighting improves aggregation quality while remaining lightweight and interpretable. These results show that internal activation structure can provide useful information for federated aggregation beyond client size and gradient geometry.

中文摘要翻译: 联邦学习通常依据数据集大小或梯度级指标来聚合客户端更新，而忽略了各客户端模型在训练过程中如何组织其输入空间的内部信号。我们提出FedOUI，一种基于过拟合-欠拟合指标（OUI）的简单聚合规则，该指标是基于激活值且无需标签的度量指标。每个参与客户端发送其本地更新以及在一个固定探针批次上计算的OUI值，服务器通过平滑重加权规则估计轮次OUI分布，为结构异常的客户端分配较低权重。我们在CIFAR-10上对FedOUI在强非独立同分布划分和噪声客户端条件下进行评估，并与FedAvg、FedProx以及一种梯度对齐基准方法进行比较。最明显的提升出现在强异质性场景下，基于OUI的加权在提升聚合质量的同时保持了轻量和可解释性。这些结果表明，内部激活结构能够为联邦聚合提供超越客户端大小和梯度几何的有用信息。

</details>


### [201] [Learning Weakly Communicating Average-Reward CMDPs: Strong Duality and Improved Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11586)
*Kihyun Yu,Beomhan Baek,Dabeen Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了弱连通平均奖励约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP），建立了强对偶性，并提出了一种原对偶裁剪值迭代算法，在遗憾和约束违规方面实现了$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$的界，改进了现有最优结果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 弱连通平均奖励CMDP缺乏线性规划表述，导致非凸性，强对偶性是否成立尚不明确；同时，现有学习算法的遗憾界有待改进。

Method: 通过仔细利用占据测度集的几何结构证明弱连通平均奖励CMDP的强对偶性；在此基础上，提出原对偶裁剪值迭代算法，将裁剪值迭代扩展到约束设置并适配到有限视界近似，利用强对偶性分解复合拉格朗日遗憾。

Result: 算法在遗憾和约束违规方面达到$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$的界，优于已知最优结果。

Conclusion: 弱连通平均奖励CMDP的强对偶性成立，所提算法在理论上具有更优的遗憾界。

Original Abstract: We study infinite-horizon average-reward constrained Markov decision processes (CMDPs) under the weakly communicating assumption. Our contributions are twofold. First, we establish strong duality for weakly communicating average-reward CMDPs over stationary policies with finite state and action spaces. Despite the absence of a linear programming formulation and the resulting nonconvexity under the weakly communicating setting, we show that strong duality still holds by carefully exploiting the geometric structure of the occupation measure set. Second, building on this result, we propose a primal--dual clipped value iteration algorithm for learning weakly communicating average-reward linear CMDPs. Our algorithm achieves regret and constraint violation bounds of $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$, improving upon the best known bounds, where $T$ denotes the number of interactions. Our approach extends clipped value iteration to the constrained setting and adapts it to a finite-horizon approximation, which stabilizes the dual variable and is crucial for achieving improved regret bounds. To analyze this, we develop a novel approach based on strong duality that enables the decomposition of the composite Lagrangian regret into separate bounds on regret and constraint violation.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了弱连通假设下的无限视界平均奖励约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP）。我们的贡献有两方面。首先，我们在有限状态和动作空间上，针对平稳策略建立了弱连通平均奖励CMDP的强对偶性。尽管在弱连通设置下缺乏线性规划表述且因此导致非凸性，我们通过仔细利用占据测度集的几何结构，证明了强对偶性仍然成立。其次，基于这一结果，我们提出了一种用于学习弱连通平均奖励线性CMDP的原对偶裁剪值迭代算法。我们的算法在遗憾和约束违规方面达到了$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$的界，改进了已知最优界，其中$T$表示交互次数。我们的方法将裁剪值迭代扩展到约束设置，并将其适配到有限视界近似，从而稳定了对偶变量，这对实现改进的遗憾界至关重要。为分析这一点，我们基于强对偶性开发了一种新方法，能够将复合拉格朗日遗憾分解为独立的遗憾界和约束违规界。

</details>


### [202] [SoK: Unlearnability and Unlearning for Model Dememorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11592)
*Mengying Zhang,Derui Wang,Ruoxi Sun,Xiaoyu Xia,Shuang Hao,Minhui Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统整合了两种模型去记忆方法——可用性中毒（不可学习性）和机器遗忘，提出了统一分类法，通过实验揭示了两者的脆弱性（浅层去记忆）和相互影响，并给出了首个关于认证遗忘的去记忆深度的理论保证。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管不可学习性和机器遗忘都旨在保护数据不被滥用，但二者的脆弱性、共同基础以及相互影响尚不清晰。具体而言，两种方法都存在浅层去记忆问题（如权重扰动下虚假的不可学习或遗忘），输入扰动可能影响下游遗忘效果，而遗忘可能意外恢复被不可学习性隐藏的领域知识。目前缺乏对浅层去记忆防御的形式化理论保证。

Method: (i) 提出了不可学习性与可扩展遗忘方法的统一分类法；(ii) 通过实验评估了主流方法的鲁棒性、相互影响和浅层去记忆问题；(iii) 给出了首个经过认证遗忘处理的模型去记忆深度的理论保证。

Result: 实验揭示了主流方法在鲁棒性、相互影响和浅层去记忆方面的表现；理论方面给出了首个关于认证遗忘的去记忆深度的形式化保证。

Conclusion: 这些结果为统一机器学习生命周期中的去记忆机制奠定了基础，有助于实现对敏感知识更深层次的不可记忆状态。

Original Abstract: Advanced model dememorization methods, including availability poisoning (unlearnability) and machine unlearning, are emerging as key safeguards against data misuse in machine learning (ML). At the training stage, unlearnability embeds imperceptible perturbations into data before release to reduce learnability. At the post-training stage, unlearning removes previously acquired information from models to prevent unauthorized disclosure or use. While both defenses aim to preserve the right to withhold knowledge, their vulnerabilities and shared foundations remain unclear. Specifically, both unlearnability and unlearning suffer from issues such as shallow dememorization, leading to falsely claimed data learnability reduction or forgetting in the presence of weight perturbations. Moreover, input perturbations may affect the effectiveness of downstream unlearning, while unlearning may inadvertently recover domain knowledge hidden by unlearnability. This interplay calls for deeper investigation. Finally, there is a lack of formal guarantees to provide theoretical insights into current defenses against shallow dememorization. In this Systematization of Knowledge, we present the first integrated analysis of model dememorization approaches leveraging unlearnability and unlearning. Our contributions are threefold: (i) a unified taxonomy of unlearnability and scalable unlearning methods; (ii) an empirical evaluation revealing the robustness, interplay, and shallow dememorization of leading methods; and (iii) the first theoretical guarantee on dememorization depth for models processed through certified unlearning. These results lay the foundation for unifying dememorization mechanisms across the ML lifecycle to achieve a deeper immemor state for sensitive knowledge.

中文摘要翻译: 先进的模型去记忆方法，包括可用性中毒（不可学习性）和机器遗忘，正成为保护机器学习中数据免遭滥用的关键安全措施。在训练阶段，不可学习性在数据发布前嵌入难以察觉的扰动以降低可学习性。在训练后阶段，遗忘从模型中移除先前获取的信息，以防止未经授权的披露或使用。虽然这两种防御机制都旨在保护保留知识的权利，但它们的脆弱性和共同基础仍不清楚。具体而言，不可学习性和遗忘都存在浅层去记忆问题，导致在权重扰动下声称的数据可学习性降低或遗忘效果虚假。此外，输入扰动可能影响下游遗忘的有效性，而遗忘可能意外恢复被不可学习性隐藏的领域知识。这种相互作用需要更深入的探究。最后，当前针对浅层去记忆的防御缺乏形式化保证来提供理论洞见。在这篇知识系统化工作中，我们首次对利用不可学习性和遗忘的模型去记忆方法进行了整合分析。我们的贡献包括三个方面：(i) 不可学习性和可扩展遗忘方法的统一分类法；(ii) 揭示主流方法的鲁棒性、相互影响和浅层去记忆问题的实证评估；(iii) 首个关于经过认证遗忘处理的模型去记忆深度的理论保证。这些结果为统一机器学习生命周期中的去记忆机制奠定了基础，以实现对敏感知识更深层次的不可记忆状态。

</details>


### [203] [EpiCastBench: Datasets and Benchmarks for Multivariate Epidemic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11598)
*Madhurima Panja,Danny D'Agostino,Huitao Li,Tanujit Chakraborty,Nan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EpiCastBench，一个包含40个多变量流行病数据集的大规模基准框架，并基于该框架对15种多变量预测模型进行了系统评估。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有流行病预测方法缺乏高质量的多变量基准数据集，难以全面评估和比较不同模型的预测性能，制约了该领域的发展。

Method: 构建了EpiCastBench基准框架，包含40个跨多种传染病和地理区域的多元时间序列数据集；建立了标准化的评估设置（统一预测 horizon、预处理流程、多样化的性能指标和统计显著性检验）；基于该框架对15种多变量预测模型（从统计基线到最先进的深度学习和基础模型）进行全面评估。

Result: 完成了对15种多变量预测模型的系统评估，所有数据集和代码已公开发布在Kaggle和GitHub上。

Conclusion: EpiCastBench为流行病预测研究提供了一个标准化、可复现的基准平台，有助于推动该领域的方法创新和公平比较。

Original Abstract: The increasing adoption of data-driven decision-making in public health has established epidemic forecasting as a critical area of research. Recent advances in multivariate forecasting models better capture complex temporal dependencies than conventional univariate approaches, which model individual series independently. Despite this potential, the development of robust epidemic forecasting methods is constrained by the lack of high-quality benchmarks comprising diverse multivariate datasets across infectious diseases and geographical regions. To address this gap, we present EpiCastBench, a large-scale benchmarking framework featuring 40 curated (correlated) multivariate epidemic datasets. These publicly available datasets span a wide range of infectious diseases and exhibit diverse characteristics in terms of temporal granularity, series length, and sparsity. We analyze these datasets to identify their global features and structural patterns. To ensure reproducibility and fair comparison, we establish standardized evaluation settings, including a unified forecasting horizon, consistent preprocessing pipelines, diverse performance metrics, and statistical significance testing. By leveraging this framework, we conduct a comprehensive evaluation of 15 multivariate forecasting models spanning statistical baselines to state-of-the-art deep learning and foundation models. All datasets and code are publicly available on Kaggle (https://www.kaggle.com/datasets/aimltsf/epicastbench) and GitHub (https://github.com/aimltsf/EpiCastBench).

中文摘要翻译: 数据驱动决策在公共卫生领域的日益普及，使流行病预测成为一个关键研究方向。近期多变量预测模型的进展比传统单变量方法（独立建模单个序列）更好地捕捉了复杂的时间依赖性。尽管潜力巨大，但稳健的流行病预测方法的开发因缺乏涵盖不同传染病和地理区域的高质量多变量数据集基准而受到制约。为弥补这一空白，我们提出了EpiCastBench，一个大规模基准测试框架，包含40个精心整理的相关多变量流行病数据集。这些公开可用的数据集覆盖广泛的传染病类型，并在时间粒度、序列长度和稀疏性方面展现出多样化特征。我们分析了这些数据集以识别其全局特征和结构模式。为确保可复现性和公平比较，我们建立了标准化评估设置，包括统一的预测视界、一致的预处理流程、多样化的性能指标和统计显著性检验。利用该框架，我们对15种多变量预测模型进行了全面评估，涵盖了从统计基线到最先进的深度学习和基础模型。所有数据集和代码已在Kaggle和GitHub上公开发布。

</details>


### [204] [Anti-Self-Distillation for Reasoning RL via Pointwise Mutual Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11609)
*Guobin Shen,Xiang Cheng,Chenxiao Zhao,Lei Huang,Jindong Li,Dongcheng Zhao,Xing Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Anti-Self-Distillation（AntiSD）方法，通过反转学生与教师模型间的散度方向，并引入熵触发门控机制，解决了同策略自蒸馏在数学推理任务中效果不一致的问题，在2到10倍更少的训练步数下达到或超越GRPO基线，最高提升11.5个准确率点。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 同策略自蒸馏（on-policy self-distillation）在数学推理任务中效果不一致，即使同一方法在其他领域表现良好。通过点互信息分析发现，失败原因在于特权上下文（privileged context）本身：它会膨胀教师模型在已由解隐含的token（如结构连接词、可验证断言）上的置信度，同时压低驱动多步搜索的推敲token（如“Wait”、“Let”、“Maybe”）上的置信度，从而损害了推理能力。

Method: 提出Anti-Self-Distillation (AntiSD)方法，核心包括：(1) 反转散度方向：上升而非下降学生与教师之间的散度，从而翻转逐token的梯度符号，在单步内产生自然有界的优势；(2) 熵触发门控机制：一旦教师熵坍塌（teacher entropy collapses）则禁用该项，实现了对默认自蒸馏的即插即用替代。

Result: 在4B到30B参数的五个模型上，针对数学推理基准（如AIME、MATH等），AntiSD以2到10倍更少的训练步数达到GRPO基线的准确率，并将最终准确率最高提升11.5个百分点。

Conclusion: AntiSD为可扩展的自我改进开辟了一条路径，使语言模型能够通过自身的训练信号引导其推理能力，实现自举式的推理提升。

Original Abstract: On-policy self-distillation, where a student is pulled toward a copy of itself conditioned on privileged context (e.g., a verified solution or feedback), offers a promising direction for advancing reasoning capability without a stronger external teacher. Yet in math reasoning the gains are inconsistent, even when the same approach succeeds elsewhere. A pointwise mutual information analysis traces the failure to the privileged context itself: it inflates the teacher's confidence on tokens already implied by the solution (structural connectives, verifiable claims) and deflates it on deliberation tokens ("Wait", "Let", "Maybe") that drive multi-step search. We propose Anti-Self-Distillation (AntiSD), which ascends a divergence between student and teacher rather than descending it: this reverses the per-token sign and yields a naturally bounded advantage in one step. An entropy-triggered gate disables the term once the teacher entropy collapses, completing a drop-in replacement for default self-distillation. Across five models from 4B to 30B parameters on math reasoning benchmarks, AntiSD reaches the GRPO baseline's accuracy in 2 to 10x fewer training steps and improves final accuracy by up to 11.5 points. AntiSD opens a path to scalable self-improvement, where a language model bootstraps its own reasoning through its training signal.

中文摘要翻译: 同策略自蒸馏——学生模型向其自身的、以特权上下文（例如已验证的解答或反馈）为条件的副本靠拢——为在没有更强外部教师的情况下提升推理能力提供了一条有前景的路径。然而，在数学推理中，即使同一方法在其他领域取得成功，其增益却并不一致。点互信息分析将失败追溯到特权上下文本身：它膨胀了教师模型在已由解答隐含的token（结构连接词、可验证断言）上的置信度，同时压低了驱动多步搜索的推敲token（如“Wait”、“Let”、“Maybe”）上的置信度。我们提出反自蒸馏（Anti-Self-Distillation, AntiSD），该方法上升而非下降学生与教师之间的散度：这翻转了逐token的符号，并在单步内产生自然有界的优势。一个熵触发的门控机制在教师熵坍塌时禁用该项，从而完成对默认自蒸馏的即插即用替代。在4B到30B参数的五个模型上，针对数学推理基准，AntiSD以2到10倍更少的训练步数达到GRPO基线的准确率，并将最终准确率最高提升11.5个百分点。AntiSD开辟了一条可扩展的自我改进路径，使语言模型能够通过其自身的训练信号自举推理能力。

</details>


### [205] [MIST: Reliable Streaming Decision Trees for Online Class-Incremental Learning via McDiarmid Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11617)
*Phu-Hoa Pham,Chi-Nguyen Tran,Nguyen Lam Phu Quy,Dao Sy Duy Minh,Huynh Trung Kiet,Long Tran-Thanh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析发现流式决策树在在线类增量学习中因信息增益随类别数K对数增长而失效，提出MIST模型，通过K无关的McDiarmid置信半径、贝叶斯继承协议和KLL分位数草图三个组件解决该问题，在非高斯数据上表现优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 流式决策树因局部更新、有界内存和静态决策边界，本是开放世界持续学习的天然候选，但在线类增量学习中存在两个耦合的误校准问题：(i) 分裂准则随类别数K增加而不可靠；(ii) 分裂时缺乏知识迁移。两者根源在于信息增益的范围内在随log2 K缩放，导致基于Hoeffding的置信半径必然随类别数增长，使得K独立的分裂准则在结构上不可能实现。

Method: MIST包含三个集成组件：(1) 针对Gini分裂的紧致、K无关的McDiarmid置信半径，作为结构正则化器；(2) 贝叶斯继承协议，通过截断高斯矩将父节点统计量投影到子节点，在分裂最保守时方差缩减保证最强；(3) 每叶节点的KLL分位数草图，支持连续阈值评估和几何自适应叶节点预测，均来自单一数据结构。

Result: 在标准表和压力测试流数据上，MIST在近高斯基准上与全局参数方法表现相当，在非高斯几何数据上具有独特鲁棒性，而此时SOTA基准方法崩溃失效。

Conclusion: MIST通过理论驱动的设计解决了流式决策树在类增量学习中的根本性缺陷，实现了K无关的稳定分裂，并在复杂非高斯数据分布上展现出显著优势。

Original Abstract: Streaming decision trees are natural candidates for open-world continual learning, as they perform local updates, enjoy bounded memory, and static decision boundaries. Despite these, they still fail in online class-incremental learning due to two coupled miscalibrations: (i) their split criterion grows unreliable as the class count K expands, and (ii) the absence of knowledge transfer at split time. Both failures share a common root: the range of Information Gain intrinsically scales with log2 K. Consequently, any Hoeffding-style confidence radius derived from it must inevitably grow with the class count, making a K-independent split criterion structurally impossible, taking away the potential benefits of applying streaming decision trees to continual learning. To fix this issue, we present MIST (McDiarmid Incremental Streaming Tree), which resolves both failures through three integrated components: (i) a tight, K-independent McDiarmid confidence radius for Gini splitting that acts as a structural regulariser; (ii) a Bayesian inheritance protocol that projects parent statistics to child nodes via truncated-Gaussian moments, with variance reduction guarantees strongest precisely when splitting is most conservative; and (iii) per-leaf KLL quantile sketches that support both continuous threshold evaluation and geometry-adaptive leaf prediction from a single data structure. On standard and stress-test tabular streams, MIST is competitive with global parametric methods on near-Gaussian benchmarks and uniquely robust on non-Gaussian geometry where SOTA benchmarks collapse.

中文摘要翻译: 流式决策树因其局部更新、有界内存和静态决策边界的特性，本是开放世界持续学习的天然候选。然而，由于两个耦合的误校准问题，它们仍无法有效处理在线类增量学习：(i) 随着类别数K的增长，其分裂准则变得不可靠；(ii) 分裂时缺乏知识迁移。这两个失效问题共享同一个根源：信息增益的范围内在随log2 K缩放。因此，任何基于Hoeffding的不等式推导出的置信半径必然随类别数增长，使得K独立的分裂准则在结构上不可能实现，从而剥夺了流式决策树在持续学习中的潜在优势。为解决这一问题，我们提出MIST（McDiarmid增量流式树），通过三个集成组件同时解决上述两个失效问题：(i) 针对Gini分裂的紧致、K无关的McDiarmid置信半径，作为结构正则化器；(ii) 贝叶斯继承协议，通过截断高斯矩将父节点统计量投影到子节点，当分裂最保守时方差缩减保证最强；(iii) 每叶节点的KLL分位数草图，支持从单一数据结构中同时进行连续阈值评估和几何自适应叶节点预测。在标准表和压力测试流数据上，MIST在近高斯基准上与全局参数方法表现相当，在非高斯几何数据上具有独特的鲁棒性，而现有SOTA基准方法在此类数据上完全崩溃。

</details>


### [206] [Evolutionary Task Discovery: Advancing Reasoning Frontiers via Skill Composition and Complexity Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11666)
*Liqin Ye,Yanbin Yin,Michael Galarnyk,Yuzhao Heng,Sudheer Chava,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EvoTD框架，将数据合成视为在算法技能和复杂度属性双轴流形上的有向搜索，通过结构化进化算子（交叉与参数化变异）和动态最近发展区过滤器，系统性地扩展LLM推理边界。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于RLVR的后训练方法受限于训练数据的多样性和复杂度；当前数据合成方法（如非结构化变异或探索）同质性严重，无法系统性扩展推理边界。

Method: 提出进化任务发现框架EvoTD：将数据合成建模为在算法技能-复杂度属性双轴流形上的有向搜索；引入结构化进化算子——交叉算子合成新技能组合以增强多样性，参数化变异算子扩展结构约束（如输入规模、树深度）以驱动鲁棒泛化；集成动态最近发展区过滤器确保任务处于模型可学习区域内。

Result: EvoTD在多种模型架构、预训练方法和规模上持续带来显著的推理能力提升，且泛化性能稳定。

Conclusion: 结构化进化课程能够有效支持大语言模型推理能力的提升。

Original Abstract: The reasoning frontier of Large Language Models (LLMs) has advanced significantly through modern post-training paradigms (e.g., Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR)). However, the efficacy of these methods remains fundamentally constrained by the diversity and complexity of the training data. One practical solution is data synthesis; yet, prevalent methods relying on unstructured mutation or exploration suffer from homogeneity collapse, failing to systematically expand the reasoning frontier. To overcome this, we propose Evoutionary Task Discovery (EvoTD), a framework that treats data synthesis as a directed search over a dual-axis manifold of Algorithmic Skills and Complexity Attributes. We introduce structured evolutionary operators to navigate this space: a Crossover operator that synthesizes novel skill compositions to enhance diversity, and a Parametric Mutation operator that scales structural constraints (e.g., input size, tree depth) to drive robust generalization. Crucially, we integrate a dynamic Zone of Proximal Development filter, ensuring tasks lie within the learnable region of the model. Empirically, EvoTD delivers substantial reasoning gains that generalize consistently across model architectures, pretraining regimes, and scales, demonstrating that structured evolutionary curricula can effectively support reasoning improvement. We release our code on https://github.com/liqinye/EvoTD.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型（LLMs）的推理前沿通过现代后训练范式（如基于可验证奖励的强化学习，RLVR）取得了显著进展。然而，这些方法的有效性仍从根本上受限于训练数据的多样性和复杂度。一种实用的解决方案是数据合成；然而，当前依赖非结构化变异或探索的方法存在同质性坍缩问题，无法系统性扩展推理边界。为解决这一问题，我们提出了进化任务发现（EvoTD）框架，将数据合成视为在算法技能和复杂度属性双轴流形上的有向搜索。我们引入结构化进化算子来导航这一空间：交叉算子通过合成新颖的技能组合增强多样性，参数化变异算子通过扩展结构约束（如输入规模、树深度）驱动鲁棒泛化。关键的是，我们集成了一个动态最近发展区过滤器，确保任务位于模型的可学习区域内。实验表明，EvoTD带来了显著的推理能力提升，且在不同模型架构、预训练方法和规模上持续泛化，证明结构化进化课程能够有效支持推理改进。我们已在https://github.com/liqinye/EvoTD上发布代码。

</details>


### [207] [Shaping Zero-Shot Coordination via State Blocking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11688)
*Mingu Kang,Sunwoo Lee,Yonghyeon Jo,Seungyul Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出State-Blocked Coordination (SBC)框架，通过状态阻塞生成多样化虚拟环境，在不直接修改环境的前提下提升智能体在零样本协调（ZSC）中与未见同伴的泛化能力，包括与人类伙伴的协作。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有零样本协调方法主要依赖增加训练过程中的同伴多样性，但往往难以可靠地泛化到未见同伴，亟需一种更有效的策略来提升智能体与未见伙伴（包括人类）的协调能力。

Method: 通过状态阻塞（state blocking）生成一系列虚拟环境，使得智能体能够在训练中体验广泛的不同次优同伴策略，从而提升零样本协调能力，且无需直接修改原始环境。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，SBC在零样本协调任务上取得了优越性能，并且展现出对未见同伴（包括人类伙伴）的强泛化能力。

Conclusion: SBC是一种简单而有效的零样本协调框架，通过状态阻塞机制提升智能体与未见同伴的协作泛化能力，在多项基准上表现优异。

Original Abstract: Zero-shot coordination (ZSC) aims to enable agents to cooperate with independently trained partners without prior interaction, a key requirement for real-world multi-agent systems and human-AI collaboration. Existing approaches have largely emphasized increasing partner diversity during training, yet such strategies often fall short of achieving reliable generalization to unseen partners. We introduce State-Blocked Coordination (SBC), a simple yet effective framework that improves ZSC by inducing diverse interaction scenarios without direct environment modification. Specifically, SBC generates a family of virtual environments through state blocking, allowing agents to experience a wide range of suboptimal partner policies. Across multiple benchmarks, SBC demonstrates superior performance in zero-shot coordination, including strong generalization to human partners.

中文摘要翻译: 零样本协调（ZSC）旨在使智能体能够与独立训练的伙伴在没有先验交互的情况下进行协作，这是现实世界多智能体系统和人机协作的关键要求。现有方法主要强调在训练中增加同伴多样性，然而这些策略往往难以实现向未见同伴的可靠泛化。我们提出状态阻塞协调（SBC），一种简单而有效的框架，通过诱导多样化的交互场景来提升零样本协调，而无需直接修改环境。具体来说，SBC通过状态阻塞生成一系列虚拟环境，使智能体能够体验广泛的次优同伴策略。在多个基准测试中，SBC在零样本协调任务上表现出优越的性能，包括对人类伙伴的强泛化能力。

</details>


### [208] [Slicing and Dicing: Configuring Optimal Mixtures of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11689)
*Margaret Li,Sneha Kudugunta,Danielle Rothermel,Luke Zettlemoyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过2000+次预训练实验，系统研究了MoE架构中专家数量、粒度、共享专家、负载均衡等核心设计选择，发现专家数量和粒度是影响模型质量的关键因素，其他设计选择影响较小。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有对MoE架构设计选择（专家数量、粒度、共享专家、负载均衡、令牌丢弃等）的研究仅局限于小范围单变量或双变量分析，缺乏系统性研究来探究这些设计选择是否可独立优化而无需考虑交互效应。

Method: 对超过2000次预训练运行进行系统性研究，模型总参数最高达6.6B，全面变化专家总数、专家维度、单层内异构专家大小、共享专家大小和负载均衡机制。

Result: (1) 在每个活跃参数规模下，即使活跃专家参数比达到128:1，增加总MoE参数仍能持续提升性能；(2) 最优专家大小几乎不随总参数数量变化，仅取决于活跃参数数量；(3) 共享专家、异构专家和负载均衡设置的影响远小于专家数量和粒度，但无丢弃路由能带来一致增益。

Conclusion: 提出更简单的设计配方：专注于专家数量和粒度，其他设计选择对最终质量影响甚微。

Original Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures have become standard in large language models, yet many of their core design choices - expert count, granularity, shared experts, load balancing, token dropping - have only been studied one or two at a time over narrow configuration ranges. It remains an open question whether these choices can be optimized independently, without considering interactions. We present the first systematic study of over 2,000 pretraining runs spanning models up to 6.6B total parameters, in which we exhaustively vary total experts, expert dimension, heterogeneous expert sizing within a single layer, shared expert size and load-balancing mechanisms. We find that at every active-parameter scale that we study, performance consistently improves with total MoE parameters even at extreme active expert parameter ratios like 128.Further, the optimal expert size is nearly invariant to total parameter count and depends only on active parameter count. Third, we see that other choices like shared experts, heterogeneous experts and load-balancing settings have small effects relative to expert count and granularity, although dropless routing yields a consistent gain. Overall, our results suggest a simpler recipe: focus on expert count and granularity, other choices have minimal effect on final quality.

中文摘要翻译: 混合专家（Mixture-of-Experts, MoE）架构已成为大型语言模型的标准配置，但其诸多核心设计选择——专家数量、粒度、共享专家、负载均衡、令牌丢弃——此前仅通过窄配置范围下的单变量或双变量研究进行探讨。这些设计选择能否在不考虑交互效应的情况下独立优化，仍然是一个悬而未决的问题。本文首次对超过2000次预训练运行进行了系统性研究，模型总参数高达6.6B，全面变化了专家总数、专家维度、单层内异构专家大小、共享专家大小以及负载均衡机制。我们发现，在每个我们研究的活跃参数规模下，即使活跃专家参数比达到128:1这样的极端情况，模型性能也随着总MoE参数的增加而持续提升。此外，最优专家大小几乎不随总参数数量变化，仅取决于活跃参数数量。第三，我们发现共享专家、异构专家和负载均衡设置相对于专家数量和粒度的影响较小，尽管无丢弃路由能带来一致的性能增益。总体而言，我们的结果表明一个更简单的配方：专注于专家数量和粒度，其他设计选择对最终质量影响甚微。

</details>


### [209] [Augmented Lagrangian Method for Last-Iterate Convergence for Constrained MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11694)
*Michael Lu,Max Qiushi Lin,Mo Chen,Sharan Vaswani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于不精确增广拉格朗日方法的通用框架，用于约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP），能够在表格、对数线性及复杂非线性策略下实现全局最后迭代收敛，弥补了理论最优混合策略与实际部署单一策略之间的鸿沟。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有CMDP的理论保证通常针对混合策略，但部署混合策略计算和存储开销大，实际中必须部署单一（最后迭代）策略。现有最后迭代收敛的工作大多局限于表格场景或实践中少用的算法变体，缺乏适用于通用策略且具有实用性的理论框架。

Method: 采用经典的约束优化中的不精确增广拉格朗日方法（AL），将CMDP问题分解为子问题；在表格设置中使用投影Q上升（PQA）求解AL子问题；将对数线性策略下的结果推广至投影PQA变体；并进一步扩展到复杂非线性策略。

Result: 在表格设置中建立了全局最后迭代收敛保证；在对数线性策略下实现了与先前工作相当的最后迭代收敛；在连续控制任务上验证了框架对复杂非线性策略的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 提出的基于不精确AL的通用框架可有效实现CMDP在多种策略类下的最后迭代收敛，弥合了理论与实际部署之间的差距，具有较好的实用性和可扩展性。

Original Abstract: We study policy optimization for infinite-horizon, discounted constrained Markov decision processes (CMDPs). While existing theoretical guarantees typically hold for the mixture policy, deploying such a policy is computationally and memory intensive. This leads to a practical mismatch where a single (last-iterate) policy must be deployed. Recent theoretical works have thus focused on proving last-iterate convergence, but are largely limited to the tabular setting or to algorithmic variants that are rarely used in practice. To address this, we use the classic inexact augmented Lagrangian ($\texttt{AL}$) method from constrained optimization, and propose a general framework with provable last-iterate convergence for CMDPs. We first focus on the tabular setting and propose to solve the $\texttt{AL}$ sub-problem with projected Q-ascent ($\texttt{PQA}$). Combining the theoretical guarantees of $\texttt{PQA}$ and the standard $\texttt{AL}$ analysis enables us to establish global last-iterate convergence. We generalize these results to handle log-linear policies, and demonstrate that an efficient, projected variant of $\texttt{PQA}$ can achieve last-iterate convergence with comparable guarantees as prior work. Finally, we demonstrate that our framework scales to complex non-linear policies, and evaluate it on continuous control tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了无限视界、折扣约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP）的策略优化问题。虽然现有的理论保证通常适用于混合策略，但部署此类策略在计算和内存上非常密集。这导致了一个实际中的不匹配问题：必须部署单一（最后迭代）策略。因此，近期的理论工作聚焦于证明最后迭代收敛，但大多局限于表格场景或实践中很少使用的算法变体。为解决这一问题，我们采用经典的约束优化中的不精确增广拉格朗日（AL）方法，并提出了一个具有可证明的最后迭代收敛保证的CMDP通用框架。我们首先关注表格场景，提出使用投影Q上升（PQA）来求解AL子问题。结合PQA的理论保证和标准AL分析，我们能够建立全局最后迭代收敛。我们将这些结果推广到处理对数线性策略，并证明一种高效的投影PQA变体能够实现与先前工作相当的最后迭代收敛保证。最后，我们展示了该框架可扩展到复杂的非线性策略，并在连续控制任务上进行了评估。

</details>


### [210] [GRAFT: Graph-Tokenized LLMs for Tool Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11706)
*Xinyi Gao,Xinyu Ren,Junliang Yu,Tong Chen,Quoc Viet Hung Nguyen,Hongzhi Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GRAFT框架，通过将工具图内化为特殊token表示并在表示空间中学习有向依赖关系，解决LLM在复杂任务中工具选择的依赖违例和错误累积问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法通过检索、序列化或提示注入等方式将工具图与LLM结合，但遵循匹配范式，无法使工具选择与子任务结构对齐，产生语义合理但违反图约束的计划，且错误累积导致后续预测偏离有效执行路径。

Method: 提出GRAFT框架：1) 将每个工具节点映射为专用特殊token，在表示空间内化工具图并学习有向依赖关系；2) 引入基于策略的工具上下文蒸馏（on-policy tool context distillation），在模型自身采样的轨迹上训练并蒸馏逐步规划信号。

Result: 在精确序列匹配和依赖合法性指标上达到最先进性能，支持更可靠的LLM工具规划。

Conclusion: GRAFT通过将工具图内化为token表示并引入策略蒸馏训练，有效解决了工具规划中的依赖违例和错误累积问题，显著提升了复杂工作流中工具规划的可靠性。

Original Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to complete complex tasks by selecting and coordinating external tools across multiple steps. This requires aligning tool choices with subtask intent while satisfying directional execution dependencies among tools. To do this, existing methods model these dependencies as tool graphs and incorporate the graphs with LLMs through retrieval, serialization, or prompt-level injection. However, these external graph-use strategies all follow a matching paradigm, which often fails to align tool choices with the underlying subtask structure, producing semantically plausible plans that violate graph constraints. This issue is further exacerbated by error accumulation, where an early incorrect tool selection shifts the plan into an invalid graph state and causes subsequent predictions to drift away from the valid execution path. To address these challenges, we propose GRAFT, a graph-tokenized language model framework for dependency-aware tool planning. GRAFT internalizes the tool graph by mapping each tool node to a dedicated special token and learning directed tool dependencies within the representation space. It further introduces on-policy tool context distillation, training the model on its own sampled trajectories while distilling stepwise planning signals. Experiments show that GRAFT achieves state-of-the-art performance in exact sequence matching and dependency legality, supporting more reliable LLM tool planning in complex workflows.

中文摘要翻译: 大型语言模型（LLM）越来越多地被用于通过选择和协调外部工具来跨多步骤完成复杂任务。这需要将工具选择与子任务意图对齐，同时满足工具之间的有向执行依赖关系。为此，现有方法将这些依赖关系建模为工具图，并通过检索、序列化或提示注入将图与LLM结合。然而，这些外部图使用策略都遵循匹配范式，往往无法使工具选择与底层子任务结构对齐，产生语义合理但违反图约束的计划。这一问题还会因错误累积而进一步恶化——早期错误的工具选择会将计划转移到无效的图状态，导致后续预测偏离有效执行路径。为应对这些挑战，我们提出GRAFT，一种图分词化的语言模型框架，用于依赖感知的工具规划。GRAFT通过将每个工具节点映射为专用的特殊token并在表示空间中学习有向工具依赖关系，将工具图内化。它还引入了基于策略的工具上下文蒸馏，在模型自身采样的轨迹上训练同时蒸馏逐步规划信号。实验表明，GRAFT在精确序列匹配和依赖合法性方面达到了最先进的性能，支持在复杂工作流中更可靠的LLM工具规划。

</details>


### [211] [Debiased Model-based Representations for Sample-efficient Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11711)
*Jiafei Lyu,Zichuan Lin,Scott Fujimoto,Kai Yang,Yangkun Chen,Saiyong Yang,Zongqing Lu,Deheng Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DR.Q算法，通过最大化当前状态-动作对与下一状态表示的互信息并结合衰减优先级经验回放，来消除基于模型的表示学习中的偏差，从而提升离线策略actor-critic学习的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的基于模型的表示方法无法捕获足够的变量相关信息，且会过度拟合回放缓冲区中的早期经验，导致表示和actor-critic学习产生偏差，性能不佳。

Method: 提出DR.Q算法：1）显式最大化当前状态-动作对表示与下一状态表示之间的互信息，同时最小化它们的偏差；2）采用衰减优先级经验回放（faded prioritized experience replay）采样转换。

Result: 在多个连续控制基准任务上使用单套超参数进行评估，DR.Q能够匹配或超越近期强基线方法，有时以较大幅度超越它们。

Conclusion: DR.Q通过去偏的基于模型的表示学习，有效提升离线策略actor-critic学习的性能，在连续控制任务中表现出色。

Original Abstract: Model-based representations recently stand out as a promising framework that embeds latent dynamics information into the representations for downstream off-policy actor-critic learning. It implicitly combines the advantages of both model-free and model-based approaches while avoiding the training costs associated with model-based methods. Nevertheless, existing model-based representation methods can fail to capture sufficient information about relevant variables and can overfit to early experiences in the replay buffer. These incur biases in representation and actor-critic learning, leading to inferior performance. To address this, we propose Debiased model-based Representations for Q-learning, tagged DR.Q algorithm.
